# Cover or Pack: New Upper and Lower Bounds for Massively Parallel Joins*

覆盖或打包：大规模并行连接的新上下界*

Xiao Hu

胡晓

Duke University

杜克大学

Durham, NC, USA

美国北卡罗来纳州达勒姆

xh102@cs.duke.edu

## ABSTRACT

## 摘要

This paper considers the worst-case complexity of multi-round join evaluation in the Massively Parallel Computation (MPC) model. Unlike the sequential RAM model, in which there is a unified optimal algorithm based on the AGM bound for all join queries, worst-case optimal algorithms have been achieved on a very restrictive class of joins in the MPC model. The only known lower bound is still derived from the AGM bound, in terms of the optimal fractional edge covering number of the query.

本文研究大规模并行计算(MPC)模型中多轮连接评估的最坏情况复杂度。与基于AGM界存在统一最优算法的顺序RAM模型不同，MPC模型目前仅在极受限的连接类别中实现了最坏情况最优算法。现有唯一的下界仍源自AGM界，通过查询的最优分数边覆盖数表示。

In this work, we make efforts towards bridging this gap. We design an instance-dependent algorithm for the class of $\alpha$ -acyclic join queries. In particular, when the maximum size of input relations is bounded, this complexity has a closed form in terms of the optimal fractional edge covering number of the query, which is worst-case optimal. Beyond acyclic joins, we surprisingly find that the optimal fractional edge covering number does not lead to a tight lower bound. More specifically, we prove for a class of cyclic joins a higher lower bound in terms of the optimal fractional edge packing number of the query, which is matched by existing algorithms, thus optimal. This new result displays a significant distinction for join evaluation, not only between acyclic and cyclic joins, but also between the fine-grained RAM and coarse-grained MPC model.

本研究致力于弥合这一差距。我们为$\alpha$无环连接查询类设计了实例依赖算法。特别地，当输入关系的最大规模受限时，该复杂度可表示为查询最优分数边覆盖数的闭式解，具有最坏情况最优性。对于有环连接，我们意外发现最优分数边覆盖数无法构成紧下界。具体而言，我们证明某类有环连接的下界应通过查询的最优分数边填充数表示，该下界与现有算法匹配，故为最优。这一新结果不仅揭示了无环与有环连接的本质差异，也展现了细粒度RAM模型与粗粒度MPC模型在连接评估中的深刻区别。

## 1 INTRODUCTION

## 1 引言

Evaluating join queries is one of the central problems in relational databases, both in theory and practice. The worst-case complexity of join evaluation started to be unraveled, largely thanks to the work of Atserials, Grohe, and Marx [5], who gave a worst-case bound on the join size, known as AGM bound. More specifically, the maximum possible join size is always bounded by $O\left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$ ,where $N$ is the maximum size of input relations and ${\rho }^{ * }$ is the optimal fractional edge covering number of the join query, which is also tight with an instance outputting $\Theta \left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$ join results. This then led to the worst-case optimal join algorithms $\left\lbrack  {{22},{26}}\right\rbrack$ in the RAM model, where all joins display a unified form of the worst-case time complexity of $O\left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$ . Ngo,Ré,and Rudra [23] presented a nice survey of these results, and also gave a simpler and unified proof for both the AGM bound and the running time of the algorithm.

连接查询评估是关系数据库理论与实践中核心问题之一。最坏情况复杂度的研究主要得益于Atserials、Grohe和Marx的工作[5]，他们提出的连接尺寸最坏情况上界被称为AGM界。具体而言，最大可能连接尺寸始终受$O\left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$约束，其中$N$表示输入关系的最大规模，${\rho }^{ * }$为连接查询的最优分数边覆盖数，该界是紧致的——存在实例能输出$\Theta \left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$个连接结果。这催生了RAM模型中最坏情况最优连接算法$\left\lbrack  {{22},{26}}\right\rbrack$，其时间复杂度统一表示为$O\left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$。Ngo、Ré和Rudra[23]对这些成果进行了系统综述，并为AGM界及算法时间复杂度提供了更简洁的统一证明。

Meanwhile, massively parallel algorithms have received much more attention in recent years, due to the rapid development of massively parallel systems such as MapReduce [10] and Spark [28]. Join evaluation in massively parallel computational model is quite different from the RAM model, where an efficient algorithm should make the best use of data locality, i.e., it should strive to send as many tuples that can be joined as possible to one machine so that it can produce their join results. Some intriguing questions then arise: Is there a unified worst-case optimal join algorithm in the massively parallel computational model? Is the worst-case optimal complexity only related to the optimal fractional edge covering number of join query? If not, what other query-dependent quantities? This work will answer these questions.

近年来，随着MapReduce[10]和Spark[28]等大规模并行系统的快速发展，并行算法研究备受关注。大规模并行计算模型中的连接评估与RAM模型存在显著差异：高效算法需充分利用数据局部性，即尽可能将可连接的元组分配至同一机器以生成连接结果。这引发若干关键问题：大规模并行计算模型中是否存在统一的最坏情况最优连接算法？最坏情况最优复杂度是否仅与查询的最优分数边覆盖数相关？若否，还涉及哪些查询相关量？本研究将解答这些问题。

### 1.1 Join Query

### 1.1 连接查询

A (natural) join is defined as a hypergraph $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,where the vertices $\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{n}}\right\}$ model the attributes and the hyperedges $\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},\ldots ,{e}_{m}}\right\}   \subseteq  {2}^{\mathcal{V}}$ model the relations [1]. Let $\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$ be the domain of attribute $x \in  \mathcal{V}$ . An instance of $Q$ is a set of relations $\mathcal{R} = \{ R\left( e\right)  : e \in  \mathcal{E}\}$ ,where $R\left( e\right)$ is a set of tuples,where each tuple is an assignment that assigns a value from $\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$ to $x$ for every $x \in  e$ . We use $N = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\left| {R\left( e\right) }\right|$ to denote the maximum size of input relations. The join results of $\mathcal{Q}$ on $\mathcal{R}$ ,denoted as $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ ,consist of all combinations of tuples,one from each $R\left( e\right)$ ,such that they share common values on their common attributes. We study the data complexity of join algorithms, i.e., assume that the query size, namely $n$ and $m$ ,are constants. Hence,the total number of input tuples,denoted as input size,is always $O\left( N\right)$ .

（自然）连接被定义为一个超图$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，其中顶点$\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{n}}\right\}$建模属性，超边$\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},\ldots ,{e}_{m}}\right\}   \subseteq  {2}^{\mathcal{V}}$建模关系[1]。设$\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$为属性$x \in  \mathcal{V}$的定义域。$Q$的实例是一组关系$\mathcal{R} = \{ R\left( e\right)  : e \in  \mathcal{E}\}$，其中每个$R\left( e\right)$是元组的集合，每个元组是从$\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$到$x$的赋值映射（对每个$x \in  e$）。我们用$N = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\left| {R\left( e\right) }\right|$表示输入关系的最大规模。$\mathcal{Q}$在$\mathcal{R}$上的连接结果记为$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$，由所有元组的组合构成（每个$R\left( e\right)$贡献一个元组），这些元组在共有属性上需具备相同值。我们研究连接算法的数据复杂度，即假设查询规模（$n$和$m$）为常数。因此输入元组总数（记为输入规模）恒为$O\left( N\right)$。

For a join query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,two query-related quantities noted as edge covering and edge packing will be commonly used throughout this paper. Let $f$ be a mapping from $\mathcal{E}$ to $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ . Note that $f$

对于连接查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，两个与查询相关的量——边覆盖（edge covering）和边打包（edge packing）将贯穿本文。设$f$为从$\mathcal{E}$到$\lbrack 0, + \infty )$的映射。注意$f$

is a fractional edge covering if

当满足以下条件时为分数边覆盖：

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E} : v \in  e}}f\left( e\right)  \geq  1\text{,for all}v \in  \mathcal{V}
$$

and a fractional edge packing if

当满足以下条件时为分数边打包：

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E} : v \in  e}}f\left( e\right)  \leq  1\text{,for all}v \in  \mathcal{V}
$$

The quantity $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{e}f\left( e\right)$ is noted as the number of $f$ ,where the optimal fractional edge covering is the one with minimum number, denoted as ${\rho }^{ * }$ ,and the optimal fractional edge packing is the one with maximum number,denoted as ${\tau }^{ * }$ . Generally,there is no clear relation between ${\tau }^{ * }$ and ${\rho }^{ * }$ ,except for some specific joins.

量$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{e}f\left( e\right)$被称为$f$的数量，其中最优分数边覆盖是取最小值的覆盖（记为${\rho }^{ * }$），最优分数边打包是取最大值的打包（记为${\tau }^{ * }$）。通常除特定连接外，${\tau }^{ * }$与${\rho }^{ * }$间无明确关联。

### 1.2 The model of computation

### 1.2 计算模型

We use the Massively Parallel Computation (MPC) model [3, 4, 7, 8, 18-20], which has become the standard model of computation for studying massively parallel algorithms, especially for join algorithms. In the MPC model, data is initially distributed evenly over $p$ servers with each server holding $O\left( \frac{N}{p}\right)$ tuples. Computation proceeds in rounds. In each round, each server first sends messages to other servers, receives messages from other servers, and then does some local computation. The complexity of an MPC algorithm is measured by the number of rounds and the load,denoted as $L$ , which is the maximum size of messages received by any server in any round. A linear load $L = O\left( \frac{N}{p}\right)$ is the ideal case (since the initial load is already $\left. \frac{N}{p}\right)$ ,while if $L = O\left( N\right)$ ,all problems can be solved trivially in one round by simply sending all data to one server. Initial efforts were mostly spent on what can be done in a single round of computation $\left\lbrack  {4,7,8,{19},{20}}\right\rbrack$ ,but recently,more interests have been given to multi-round (but still a constant) algorithms [3, 18, 19], since new main memory based systems, such as Spark and Flink, have much lower overhead per round than previous generations like Hadoop.

我们采用大规模并行计算(MPC)模型[3,4,7,8,18-20]，该模型已成为研究大规模并行算法（特别是连接算法）的标准计算模型。在MPC模型中，数据最初均匀分布在$p$台服务器上，每台服务器持有$O\left( \frac{N}{p}\right)$个元组。计算以轮次进行，每轮中每台服务器首先向其他服务器发送消息，接收来自其他服务器的消息，然后进行本地计算。MPC算法的复杂度通过轮次数量和负载（记为$L$）来衡量，负载指任意轮次中任意服务器接收消息的最大规模。线性负载$L = O\left( \frac{N}{p}\right)$是最理想情况（因为初始负载已达$\left. \frac{N}{p}\right)$），而若$L = O\left( N\right)$，所有问题均可通过将所有数据发送至单一服务器在一轮内简单解决。早期研究主要集中于单轮计算$\left\lbrack  {4,7,8,{19},{20}}\right\rbrack$能完成的任务，但近年来，随着Spark、Flink等新型基于主内存的系统每轮开销远低于Hadoop等前代系统，多轮（但仍为常数轮）算法[3,18,19]受到更多关注。

We confine ourselves to tuple-based algorithms, i.e., tuples are atomic elements that must be processed and communicated in their entirety. The only way to create a tuple is by making a copy, from either the original tuple or one of its copies. We say that an MPC algorithm computes the join query $\mathcal{Q}$ on instance $\mathcal{R}$ if the following is achieved: For any join result $\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2},\ldots ,{t}_{m}}\right)  \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ where ${t}_{i} \in  R\left( {e}_{i}\right) ,i = 1,2,\ldots ,m$ ,these $m$ tuples (or their copies) must all be present on the same server at some point. Then the server will call a zero-cost function emit $\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2},\ldots ,{t}_{m}}\right)$ to report the join result. Note that since we only consider constant-round algorithms, whether a server is allowed to keep the tuples it has received from previous rounds is irrelevant: if not, it can just keep sending all these tuples to itself over the rounds, increasing the load by a constant factor. All known join algorithms in the MPC model are tuple-based and obey these requirements. Our lower bounds are combinatorial in nature: we only count the number of tuples that must be communicated in order to emit all join results, while all other information can be communicated for free. The upper bounds include all messages,with a tuple and an integer of $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ bits both counted as 1 unit of communication.

我们限定基于元组的算法，即元组作为原子元素必须整体处理和传输。创建元组的唯一方式是通过复制原始元组或其副本。当满足以下条件时，我们称MPC算法在实例$\mathcal{R}$上计算了连接查询$\mathcal{Q}$：对于任何连接结果$\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2},\ldots ,{t}_{m}}\right)  \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$（其中${t}_{i} \in  R\left( {e}_{i}\right) ,i = 1,2,\ldots ,m$），这些$m$个元组（或其副本）必须在某时刻共处于同一服务器，该服务器将调用零成本函数emit$\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2},\ldots ,{t}_{m}}\right)$来报告连接结果。由于我们仅考虑常数轮算法，服务器是否保留前几轮接收的元组并不重要：若不保留，只需在每轮将这些元组发送给自己，使负载增加常数倍。MPC模型中所有已知连接算法均基于元组并遵循这些要求。我们的下界本质上是组合性的：仅计算为输出所有连接结果必须传输的元组数量，其他信息可免费传输。上界包含所有消息，一个元组和一个$O\left( {\log N}\right)$位整数均计为1个通信单位。

### 1.3 Worst-case optimal join algorithms

### 1.3 最坏情况最优连接算法

In this work, we will focus on worst-case optimality for algorithm design, which is the most commonly-used measurement and provides theoretical guarantees in the worst case. More specifically, the entire space of input instances is divided into classes, where instances in the same class share the same input size $N$ . An algorithm is worst-case optimal if its complexity is optimal on the worst instance for each class. Further subdividing the instance space leads to more refined analyses, for example, output-optimality takes both input size $N$ and output size OUT as parameters to divide the instance space, and instance-optimality pushes this idea to the extreme case that each class contains just one instance. Note that by definition, an instance-optimal algorithm must be output-optimal, and an output-optimal algorithm must be worst-case optimal, but the reserve direction may not be true. We refer interested readers to [16] for the fine-grained join algorithms in the MPC model. All results with respect to the worst-case optimality in the MPC model are put into Table 1 and the relationships between join queries mentioned in this work are clarified in Figure 1.

本工作聚焦算法设计中的最坏情况最优性——这是最常用的衡量标准，能在最坏情况下提供理论保证。具体而言，我们将所有输入实例空间划分为若干类别，同一类别中的实例具有相同的输入规模$N$。若某算法在每个类别的最坏实例上都具有最优复杂度，则称其为最坏情况最优算法。进一步细分实例空间可得到更精细的分析，例如输出最优性同时以输入规模$N$和输出规模OUT作为参数划分实例空间，而实例最优性则将这一思想推向极致——每个类别仅包含单个实例。需注意根据定义，实例最优算法必然输出最优，输出最优算法必然最坏情况最优，但反之未必成立。关于MPC模型中细粒度连接算法的深入探讨，建议读者参阅[16]。MPC模型中所有最坏情况最优性相关结论已汇总至表1，本工作涉及的连接查询关系如图1所示。

Most of previous efforts have been put to understand what can be done in a single round in the MPC model. Initially, a one-round hashing-based algorithm $\left\lbrack  {4,7}\right\rbrack$ ,named as hypercube,was proposed for computing all joins on non-skewed input instances with load $\widetilde{O}{\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}}\right) }^{1}$ . Later,an improved algorithm built upon hypercube has been proposed for tackling arbitrary input instances [19], but it incurs a higher load of $\widetilde{O}\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\psi }^{ * }}}\right)$ ,where ${\psi }^{ * }$ is the optimal fractional edge quasi-packing number ${}^{2}$ of the query. This result has been proved to be optimal (up to a polylog factor) for single-round computation with arbitrary input instances. Note that ${\psi }^{ * } \geq  {\tau }^{ * }$ [19].

先前研究主要集中于探索MPC模型单轮计算的可能性。最初提出的基于哈希的单轮算法$\left\lbrack  {4,7}\right\rbrack$（称为超立方算法）能以$\widetilde{O}{\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}}\right) }^{1}$负载计算非偏斜输入实例的所有连接。后续研究针对任意输入实例提出了改进算法[19]，但会导致更高的$\widetilde{O}\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\psi }^{ * }}}\right)$负载（其中${\psi }^{ * }$表示查询的最优分数边拟填充数${}^{2}$）。该结果已被证明是任意输入实例单轮计算的最优解（相差多项式对数因子）。注意${\psi }^{ * } \geq  {\tau }^{ * }$ [19]。

---

${}^{1}$ The $\widetilde{O}$ notation suppresses polylog factors.

${}^{1}$ $\widetilde{O}$符号表示忽略多项式对数因子。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

However, people found that even allowing a constant number of rounds may bring a significant (polynomially) reduction in the overall cost. Consider an example join query $Q = {R}_{1}\left( A\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {A,B}\right)  \bowtie$ ${R}_{3}\left( B\right)$ ,which has ${\psi }^{ * } = {\tau }^{ * } = 2$ by choosing ${R}_{1},{R}_{3}$ in the fractional edge packing and ${\rho }^{ * } = 1$ by choosing ${R}_{2}$ in the fractional edge cover. If targeting a single round,it can be computed with load $\widetilde{O}\left( \frac{N}{\sqrt{p}}\right) \left\lbrack  {19}\right\rbrack$ . However, if just allowing one more round, it can be computed through two steps of semi-joins with linear load $\widetilde{O}\left( \frac{N}{p}\right)$ (see Section 2). The $\sqrt{p}$ -gap can be further enlarged to ${p}^{\frac{n - 1}{n}}$ on the star-dual join $Q = {R}_{0}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},\cdots ,{x}_{n}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{1}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \bowtie  \cdots  \bowtie  {R}_{n}\left( {x}_{n}\right)$ . This opens up new door for join evaluation in the MPC model.

然而，人们发现即使允许固定轮次，也可能带来显著的（多项式级）总体成本降低。以连接查询$Q = {R}_{1}\left( A\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {A,B}\right)  \bowtie$${R}_{3}\left( B\right)$为例，通过分数边包装选择${R}_{1},{R}_{3}$时其规模为${\psi }^{ * } = {\tau }^{ * } = 2$，通过分数边覆盖选择${R}_{2}$时则为${\rho }^{ * } = 1$。若采用单轮计算，其负载为$\widetilde{O}\left( \frac{N}{\sqrt{p}}\right) \left\lbrack  {19}\right\rbrack$；但若增加一轮，则可通过两轮半连接以线性负载$\widetilde{O}\left( \frac{N}{p}\right)$完成计算（参见第2节）。在星型对偶连接$Q = {R}_{0}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},\cdots ,{x}_{n}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{1}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \bowtie  \cdots  \bowtie  {R}_{n}\left( {x}_{n}\right)$上，$\sqrt{p}$的差距可进一步扩大至${p}^{\frac{n - 1}{n}}$。这为MPC模型中的连接计算开启了新思路。

The goal of a multi-round worst-case optimal algorithm in the MPC model is believed to achieve a load of $O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$ . The reason why $\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$ is a lower bound can be argued by the following counting argument: Each server can only produce $O\left( {L}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$ join results in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with its load limited to $L$ (also implied by the AGM bound [5]),so all the $p$ servers can produce $O\left( {p \cdot  {L}^{{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$ join results. Then,producing $\Theta \left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$ join results requires $L = \Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$ . So far, this bound (up to polylog factors) has been achieved on some specific classes of joins $\left\lbrack  {{18},{19},{25}}\right\rbrack$ ,such as binary-relation join where each relation has at most two attributes, and Loomis-Whitney join ${}^{3}$ . All these algorithms resort to the heavy-light decomposition technique for tackling data skew, and then invoke the hypercube algorithm as primitives for handling non-skewed instances. However, whether this bound can be achieved for arbitrary joins, or even just $\alpha$ -acyclic joins,is still open.

MPC模型中多轮最坏情况最优算法的目标负载被认为是$O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$。该下界可通过以下计数论证说明：每个服务器在$O\left( 1\right)$轮中仅能生成$O\left( {L}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$个连接结果（受限于$L$负载，这也符合AGM界[5]），因此所有$p$个服务器共可生成$O\left( {p \cdot  {L}^{{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$个结果。故要生成$\Theta \left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$个结果需要$L = \Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$。目前该界限（至多包含多对数因子）已在特定连接类$\left\lbrack  {{18},{19},{25}}\right\rbrack$上实现，如二元关系连接（每个关系至多两个属性）和Loomis-Whitney连接${}^{3}$。这些算法均采用轻重分解技术处理数据倾斜，并调用超立方体算法处理非倾斜实例。但对于任意连接（甚至仅$\alpha$-无环连接）能否达到该界限，仍是未解难题。

Besides, several output-optimal (or output-sensitive) algorithms have been proposed for join queries in the MPC model. For example, an output-optimal algorithm has been proposed for r-hierarchical joins [16], which is also worst-case optimal. Note that r-hierarchical join is a very restrictive class of join queries; for example, the simplest line-3 join query ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {B,C}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {C,D}\right)$ is not r-hierarchical. Meanwhile, the classical Yannakakis algorithm [27] can be easily parallelized for computing $\alpha$ -acyclic joins with load complexity $O\left( {\frac{N}{p} + \frac{\mathrm{{OUT}}}{p}}\right)$ . Very recently,it has been improved to $O\left( {\frac{N}{p} + \frac{\sqrt{N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}}{p}}\right) \left\lbrack  {16}\right\rbrack$ ,which is output-optimal if $\mathrm{{OUT}} = O\left( {p \cdot  N}\right)$ . However, in the worst-case when OUT approaches the AGM bound $\Theta \left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$ ,this complexity would degenerate to $O\left( \frac{{N}^{\left( {{\rho }^{ * } + 1}\right) /2}}{p}\right)$ ,which is rather far away from our target $\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$ .

此外，针对MPC模型中的连接查询，已有若干输出最优（或输出敏感）算法被提出。例如，针对r-层次化连接[16]提出的输出最优算法，该算法同时也是最坏情况最优的。需注意r-层次化连接是一类限制性极强的连接查询；例如最简单的line-3连接查询${R}_{1}\left( {A,B}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {B,C}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {C,D}\right)$就不属于r-层次化连接。与此同时，经典的Yannakakis算法[27]可轻松并行化用于计算$\alpha$-无环连接，其负载复杂度为$O\left( {\frac{N}{p} + \frac{\mathrm{{OUT}}}{p}}\right)$。最新研究已将其改进至$O\left( {\frac{N}{p} + \frac{\sqrt{N \cdot  \mathrm{{OUT}}}}{p}}\right) \left\lbrack  {16}\right\rbrack$，当$\mathrm{{OUT}} = O\left( {p \cdot  N}\right)$时即达到输出最优。然而在最坏情况下，当OUT接近AGM上界$\Theta \left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$时，该复杂度会退化至$O\left( \frac{{N}^{\left( {{\rho }^{ * } + 1}\right) /2}}{p}\right)$，与我们的目标值$\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$相去甚远。

It is remarkable that a reduction from the MPC model to the external memory (EM) model has been established in [19] in a cost-preserving way, such that any MPC algorithm running in $r$ rounds with load $L\left( {N,p}\right)$ can be converted to an EM algorithm incurring $\widetilde{O}\left( {\frac{N}{B} + r{p}^{ * }\frac{M}{B}}\right) \mathrm{I}/{\mathrm{{Os}}}^{4}$ ,where ${p}^{ * } = \mathop{\min }\limits_{p}\{ L\left( {N,p}\right)  \leq  M/r\}$ . Implied by this reduction, worst-case optimal algorithms can be automatically obtained for LW join ${}^{5}$ and binary-relation join in the EM model. It is worth mentioning that a worst-case optimal algorithm has been proposed [15] for Berge-acyclic join in the EM model using $\widetilde{O}\left( {{\left( \frac{N}{M}\right) }^{{\rho }^{ * }} \cdot  \frac{M}{B}}\right)$ I/Os,without a counterpart in the MPC model. However, Berge-acyclic join is a very restrictive sub-class of $\alpha$ -acyclic join; for example,a simple join query ${R}_{0}\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{1}\left( {A,B,D}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {B,C,E}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {A,C,F}\right)$ is $\alpha$ -acyclic but not Berge-cyclic, thus cannot be handled by the algorithm in [15]. On the other hand, there is no result showing any conversion from the sequential EM model to the parallel MPC model. We won't pursue this dimension further.

值得注意的是，文献[19]以成本保留的方式建立了从MPC模型到外部存储(EM)模型的归约转换，使得任何在$r$轮次内以$L\left( {N,p}\right)$负载运行的MPC算法都可转换为I/O复杂度为$\widetilde{O}\left( {\frac{N}{B} + r{p}^{ * }\frac{M}{B}}\right) \mathrm{I}/{\mathrm{{Os}}}^{4}$的EM算法（其中${p}^{ * } = \mathop{\min }\limits_{p}\{ L\left( {N,p}\right)  \leq  M/r\}$）。基于此归约，LW连接${}^{5}$和二元关系连接的最坏情况最优算法可自动适用于EM模型。值得一提的是，文献[15]针对EM模型中的Berge-无环连接提出了最坏情况最优算法，使用$\widetilde{O}\left( {{\left( \frac{N}{M}\right) }^{{\rho }^{ * }} \cdot  \frac{M}{B}}\right)$次I/O操作，但该算法在MPC模型中尚无对应实现。不过Berge-无环连接是$\alpha$-无环连接中限制极强的子类；例如简单连接查询${R}_{0}\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{1}\left( {A,B,D}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {B,C,E}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {A,C,F}\right)$属于$\alpha$-无环连接但非Berge-无环，因此无法被[15]中的算法处理。另一方面，目前尚无研究能实现从顺序EM模型到并行MPC模型的转换。我们暂不深入探讨这一维度。

### 1.4 Our Results

### 1.4 我们的研究成果

Our main results are also summarized in Table 1, which can be split into two parts: new upper bound for $\alpha$ -acyclic joins and new lower bound for some cyclic joins. We also include a brief connection of these results from both sides.

我们的主要成果亦总结于表1中，可分为两部分：针对$\alpha$-无环连接的新上界，以及针对某些循环连接的新下界。我们还将简要阐述这两类结果之间的关联。

New Upper Bound. The primary class of join queries we target in this work is the $\alpha$ -acyclic join [9],which is the most-commonly studied class of acyclic ${}^{6}$ joins in database theory. Formally,a join query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is $\alpha$ -acyclic if there exists an undirected tree $\mathcal{T}$ whose nodes are in one-to-one correspondence with the edges in $\mathcal{E}$ such that for any vertex $v \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,all nodes containing $v$ form a connected subtree. Such a tree $\mathcal{T}$ is called the join tree of $Q$ . An example of an acyclic ${}^{7}$ join is illustrated in Figure 4.

新上界。本文研究的主要连接查询类型是$\alpha$-无环连接[9]，这是数据库理论中最常研究的无环${}^{6}$连接类。形式化地说，当存在一个无向树$\mathcal{T}$，其节点与$\mathcal{E}$中的边一一对应，且对于任意顶点$v \in  \mathcal{V}$，包含$v$的所有节点构成连通子树时，连接查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$就是$\alpha$-无环的。这样的树$\mathcal{T}$称为$Q$的连接树。图4展示了一个无环${}^{7}$连接的示例。

We propose a generic MPC algorithm for computing any acyclic join, whose load complexity is related to the choices made by this non-deterministic algorithm while running. We give a characterization of "good" choices for this algorithm, such that its load complexity can be bounded with $O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$ ,achieving the worst-case optimality, as long as it always makes a good choice in each step.

我们提出了一种通用MPC算法用于计算任意无环连接，其负载复杂度与该非确定性算法运行时的选择相关。我们给出了该算法"优质"选择的特征描述，只要其在每一步都做出优质选择，就能用$O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$界定负载复杂度，实现最坏情况最优性。

This result has reduced the complexity for acyclic join evaluation from $O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\psi }^{ * }}}\right)$ to $O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$ since ${\psi }^{ * } \geq  {\rho }^{ * }\left\lbrack  {19}\right\rbrack$ ,only increasing the number of rounds from 1 to a constant. This improvement could be significant because of the possibly huge gap between ${\psi }^{ * }$ and ${\rho }^{ * }$ ,as we have seen on the example in Section 1.3. In general, we notice several important sub-classes of acyclic joins on which this gap can be as large as $\Theta \left( {m + n}\right)$ ,in terms of the query size,including path join ${}^{8}$ ,star-dual join and some tree joins ${}^{9}$ . We refer interested reader to [19] for details. Moreover, by the MPC-EM reduction, this result automatically implies an EM algorithm for computing acyclic joins with $\widetilde{O}\left( \frac{{N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}}{{M}^{{\rho }^{ * } - 1}B}\right)$ I/Os,shadowing the previous work [12].

该结果将无环连接求值的复杂度从$O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\psi }^{ * }}}\right)$降至$O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$（自${\psi }^{ * } \geq  {\rho }^{ * }\left\lbrack  {19}\right\rbrack$起），仅将轮次从1增加到常数。如第1.3节示例所示，由于${\psi }^{ * }$与${\rho }^{ * }$之间可能存在巨大差距，这一改进意义重大。我们注意到在路径连接${}^{8}$、星对偶连接及某些树连接${}^{9}$等查询规模下，无环连接的若干重要子类可能存在高达$\Theta \left( {m + n}\right)$的复杂度差距。详见文献[19]。此外，通过MPC-EM归约，该结果自然推导出具有$\widetilde{O}\left( \frac{{N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}}{{M}^{{\rho }^{ * } - 1}B}\right)$次I/O的无环连接EM算法，超越了先前工作[12]。

${}^{4}$ The EM model has main memory of size $M$ with disk block size $B$ .

${}^{5}$ 文献[13]独立提出了LW连接的最坏情况I/O最优算法。

${}^{6}$ Other notions of acyclicity have been proposed,including berge-acyclicity, $\gamma$ - acyclicity and $\beta$ -acyclicity. Moreover,berge-acyclicity implies $\gamma$ -acyclicity which implies $\beta$ -acyclicity which implies $\alpha$ -acyclicity.

${}^{6}$ 其他无环性定义包括Berge-无环性、$\gamma$-无环性和$\beta$-无环性。其中Berge-无环性蕴含$\gamma$-无环性，后者又蕴含$\beta$-无环性，继而蕴含$\alpha$-无环性。

${}^{7}$ In the following of this work,"acyclic" always means " $\alpha$ -acyclic" if not specified.

${}^{7}$ 下文所述"无环"若无特别说明，均指"$\alpha$-无环"。

${}^{8}$ A path join $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is defined as $\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},\cdots ,{x}_{n}}\right\}$ and $\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {e}_{i}\right.  =$ $\left\{  {{x}_{i},{x}_{i + 1}}\right\}   : i \in  \{ 1,2,\cdots ,n - 1\} \} .$

${}^{8}$ 路径连接$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$定义为$\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},\cdots ,{x}_{n}}\right\}$与$\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {e}_{i}\right.  =$$\left\{  {{x}_{i},{x}_{i + 1}}\right\}   : i \in  \{ 1,2,\cdots ,n - 1\} \} .$

${}^{9}$ A join query $Q$ is a tree join if it is acyclic and each relation contains at most two relations. A tree join can be decomposed into a set of vertex-disjoint path joins.

${}^{9}$ 当连接查询$Q$无环且每个关系最多包含两个关系时，称为树连接。树连接可分解为若干顶点不相交的路径连接。

${}^{2}$ For a join query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,the edge quasi-packing number is defined as follows. Let $x \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ be any subset of vertices of $\mathcal{V}$ . Define the residual hypergraph after removing attributes $x$ as ${\mathcal{Q}}_{x} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{x},{\mathcal{E}}_{x}}\right)$ ,where ${\mathcal{V}}_{x} = \mathcal{V} - x$ and ${\mathcal{E}}_{x} = \{ e - x : e \in  E\}$ . The edge quasi-packing number of $\mathcal{Q}$ is the maximum optimal fractional edge packing number over all ${\mathcal{Q}}_{x}$ ’s,i.e., ${\psi }^{ * } = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{x \subseteq  \mathcal{V}}}{\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{x}\right)$ .

${}^{2}$ 对于连接查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，边拟包装数定义如下：设$x \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$为$\mathcal{V}$顶点的任意子集，移除属性$x$后定义的残差超图为${\mathcal{Q}}_{x} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{x},{\mathcal{E}}_{x}}\right)$，其中${\mathcal{V}}_{x} = \mathcal{V} - x$且${\mathcal{E}}_{x} = \{ e - x : e \in  E\}$。$\mathcal{Q}$的边拟包装数是指所有${\mathcal{Q}}_{x}$上的最大最优分数边包装数，即${\psi }^{ * } = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{x \subseteq  \mathcal{V}}}{\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{x}\right)$。

${}^{3}$ A join query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is a Loomis-Whitney (LW) join if $\mathcal{E} = \{ \mathcal{V} - \{ x\}  : \forall x \in$ $\mathcal{V}\}$ . Moreover,it has ${\rho }^{ * } = {\tau }^{ * } = n/\left( {n - 1}\right)$ ,where $n = \left| \mathcal{V}\right|$ . As it is a very restrict class of joins with highly symmetric structures, we omit it in the following discussion.

${}^{3}$ 若连接查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$满足$\mathcal{E} = \{ \mathcal{V} - \{ x\}  : \forall x \in$$\mathcal{V}\}$，则称其为Loomis-Whitney（LW）连接。此外，该查询具有${\rho }^{ * } = {\tau }^{ * } = n/\left( {n - 1}\right)$特性（其中$n = \left| \mathcal{V}\right|$）。由于这类连接具有高度对称结构的严格限制条件，后续讨论中将予以省略。

New Lower Bound. Finally, we turn to cyclic joins. Surprisingly, we find that $O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$ is not necessarily a correct target for multi-round worst-case optimal join algorithms, since the existing lower bound $\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$ is not tight any more. We start by answering an open question posed in [18]: On the $\boxminus$ -join ${Q}_{ \boxminus  } = {R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \bowtie$ ${R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$ ,does there exist a better upper bound than $\widetilde{O}\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right)$ ,or a better lower bound than $\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{\rho }^{1/2}}\right)$ ? As shown in Figure $2,{\mathcal{Q}}_{ \boxminus  }$ has ${\rho }^{ * } = 2$ by choosing $\left\{  {{R}_{1},{R}_{2}}\right\}$ in the fractional edge cover and ${\tau }^{ * } = 3$ by choosing $\left\{  {{R}_{3},{R}_{4},{R}_{5}}\right\}$ in the fractional edge packing. We show a probabilistic hard instance on which any MPC algorithm computing it in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds must incur a load of $\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right)$ . The intuition is that such an instance has "dense" join results, which is indeed as large as the AGM bound, but each server cannot achieve high efficiency in emitting the join results, no matter which combinations of input tuples it receives. Any attempts in lowering this bound further would break the counting argument that every join result must be emitted at least once, thus violating the correctness of join algorithms. Meanwhile, the existing algorithm [19] can compute it in a single round with load $\widetilde{O}{\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right) }^{10}$ , which is already optimal implied by our new lower bound.

新下界证明。最后我们考察循环连接。令人惊讶的是，我们发现$O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$并非多轮最坏情况最优连接算法的正确目标，因为现有下界$\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$不再紧致。我们首先回答文献[18]提出的开放性问题：在$\boxminus$-连接${Q}_{ \boxminus  } = {R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \bowtie$${R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$上，是否存在优于$\widetilde{O}\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right)$的上界或优于$\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{\rho }^{1/2}}\right)$的下界？如图$2,{\mathcal{Q}}_{ \boxminus  }$所示，通过分数边覆盖选择$\left\{  {{R}_{1},{R}_{2}}\right\}$得到${\rho }^{ * } = 2$，通过分数边填充选择$\left\{  {{R}_{3},{R}_{4},{R}_{5}}\right\}$得到${\tau }^{ * } = 3$。我们构造了一个概率难例，证明任何MPC算法在$O\left( 1\right)$轮内计算时必然产生$\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right)$的负载。其核心在于该实例具有"稠密"连接结果（规模达到AGM界），但无论服务器接收何种输入元组组合，都无法高效输出连接结果。任何进一步降低此下界的尝试都将破坏"每个连接结果至少输出一次"的计数论证，从而违反连接算法的正确性。而现有算法[19]能以$\widetilde{O}{\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right) }^{10}$负载单轮完成计算，这已是我们新下界所暗示的最优解。

This framework of lower bound proof for ${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$ can be extended to a larger class of cyclic join queries, noted as degree-two joins, in which each attribute appears in two relations. Observe that the dual ${}^{11}$ of a degree-two join is a binary-relation join,hence enjoying very nice properties [24]. More specifically, we characterize the edge-packing-provable condition, under which there exists a better (at least not worse) lower bound in terms of $\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$ for computing any degree-two join in the MPC model.

该${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$下界证明框架可推广至更广泛的循环连接查询类——二阶连接（每个属性出现在两个关系中）。注意到二阶连接的对偶${}^{11}$是二元关系连接，因而具备优良性质[24]。具体而言，我们刻画了边填充可证条件，在此条件下针对MPC模型中任意二阶连接的计算，存在基于$\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$的更优（至少不更差）下界。

Cover or Pack. From the lower bound side, we know that the worst-case complexity of join evaluation is $\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$ for binary-relation joins,LW join and acyclic joins,and $\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$ for some class of cyclic joins, including the $\boxminus$ -join. However, there is no clear distinction on the relative ordering between ${\rho }^{ * }$ and ${\tau }^{ * }$ ,at least for acyclic joins and binary-relation joins. A natural question arises: cover or pack, which one is the correct quantity in determining the worst-case complexity of join evaluation?

覆盖抑或填充。从下界角度可知，二元关系连接、LW连接和无环连接的最坏情况复杂度为$\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$，而包括$\boxminus$-连接在内的某些循环连接类复杂度为$\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$。但至少在无环连接和二元关系连接中，${\rho }^{ * }$与${\tau }^{ * }$的相对顺序尚无明确区分。这引出一个本质问题：在判定连接计算的最坏情况复杂度时，覆盖和填充究竟何者是正确度量？

To clarify this question, we first introduce the notion of reduced join. A reduce procedure on a hypergraph(V,E)is to remove an edge $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ if there exists another edge ${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ such that $e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ . We can repeatedly apply the reduce procedure until no more edge can be removed, and the resulting hypergraph is said to be reduced. From the upper bound side,a join query can be reduced in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with linear load (see Section 2), thus the hardness of multi-round computation comes from the reduced join.

为阐明此问题，我们首先引入归约连接(reduced join)的概念。在超图(V,E)上的归约操作是指：若存在另一条边${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$使得$e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$，则可删除边$e \in  \mathcal{E}$。重复执行归约操作直至无法继续删除边，所得超图即称为归约超图。从上界角度看，连接查询可在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内以线性负载完成归约（参见第2节），因此多轮计算的复杂性源于归约连接。

<!-- Media -->

<!-- figureText: $\alpha$ -acyclic berge-acyclic cyclic (unclear) binary-relation degree-two [18] [Lemma 7] (conjectured) [Lemma A.3] -->

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/0196522e-b0fd-7d0b-bd81-6797a8018bea_3.jpg?x=936&y=235&w=703&h=208&r=0"/>

Figure 3: Relationship between optimal fractional edge covering number ${\rho }^{ * }$ and optimal fractional edge packing number ${\tau }^{ * }$ of reduced join queries.

图3：归约连接查询的最优分数边覆盖数${\rho }^{ * }$与最优分数边填充数${\tau }^{ * }$的关系

<!-- Media -->

Now, we can draw some distinction for the reduced join queries: (1) ${\tau }^{ * } \leq  {\rho }^{ * }$ holds for reduced berge-acyclic joins (see Lemma A.3) and reduced binary-relation joins ${\left\lbrack  {18}\right\rbrack  }^{12}$ ; and (2) ${\rho }^{ * } \leq  {\tau }^{ * }$ holds for reduced degree-two joins (see Lemma 21), as shown in Figure 3. Moreover,we conjecture that ${\tau }^{ * } \leq  {\rho }^{ * }$ holds for reduced $\alpha$ -acyclic joins, but the formal proof is currently open. At last, we come to the following conjecture. The worst-case complexity of computing a join query $Q$ in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds is conjectured to be $\Theta \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/\max \left\{  {{\rho }^{ * },{\tau }^{ * }}\right\}  }}\right)$ , where ${\rho }^{ * },{\tau }^{ * }$ are the optimal fractional edge covering and packing numbers of the reduced join of $Q$ . This conjecture can be verified by existing results, but is still open for general cyclic joins.

现可对归约连接查询作出以下区分：(1) 归约Berge-无环连接(见引理A.3)和归约二元关系连接${\left\lbrack  {18}\right\rbrack  }^{12}$满足${\tau }^{ * } \leq  {\rho }^{ * }$；(2) 如图3所示，归约二度连接(见引理21)满足${\rho }^{ * } \leq  {\tau }^{ * }$。此外，我们推测归约$\alpha$-无环连接也满足${\tau }^{ * } \leq  {\rho }^{ * }$，但形式化证明尚未完成。最终我们提出以下猜想：在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内计算连接查询$Q$的最坏情况复杂度应为$\Theta \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/\max \left\{  {{\rho }^{ * },{\tau }^{ * }}\right\}  }}\right)$，其中${\rho }^{ * },{\tau }^{ * }$是该归约连接的最优分数边覆盖数与填充数。该猜想可通过现有结果验证，但对一般循环连接仍悬而未决。

### 1.5 Outline

### 1.5 论文结构

This paper is organized as follows. In Section 2, we review some basic primitives that will be commonly used in our MPC algorithm. In Section 3 and 4, we present the new results from upper bound side. More specifically, we introduce a generic algorithm for acyclic joins in Section 3.1, analyze its complexity in Section 3.2, and identify the worst-case optimal run in Section 4. In Section 5, we move to the lower bound for cyclic joins. We first prove an edge-packing-based lower bound for the $\boxminus$ -join in Section 5.1,and then extend it to degree-two joins in Section 5.2.

本文结构如下：第2节回顾MPC算法中常用的基础原语；第3、4节从上界角度展示新成果，具体包括第3.1节介绍无环连接的通用算法，第3.2节分析其复杂度，第4节确定最坏情况最优解；第5节转向循环连接的下界证明，第5.1节首先建立基于边填充的$\boxminus$-连接下界，第5.2节将其推广至二度连接。

## 2 MPC PRELIMINARIES

## 2 MPC基础理论

We first mention the following deterministic primitives in the MPC model,which can be computed with load $O\left( \frac{N}{p}\right)$ in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds. Assume $N > {p}^{1 + \epsilon }$ where $\epsilon  > 0$ is any small constant.

首先说明MPC模型中以下确定性原语可在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内以$O\left( \frac{N}{p}\right)$负载完成计算，其中$N > {p}^{1 + \epsilon }$（$\epsilon  > 0$为任意小常数）。

Reduce-by-key [14]. Given $N$ pairs in terms of (key,value),compute the "sum" of values for each key, where the "sum" is defined by any associative operator. This primitive will also be frequently used to compute data statistics, for example the degree. The degree of value $a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$ in relation $R\left( e\right)$ is defined as the number of tuples in $R\left( e\right)$ having this value in attribute $x$ ,i.e., $\left| {{\sigma }_{x = a}R\left( e\right) }\right|$ . Each tuple $t \in  R\left( e\right)$ is considered to have "key" ${\pi }_{X}t$ and "value" 1 .

键值归约[14]。给定$N$组(key,value)键值对，计算每个键对应值的"和"（"和"由任意结合运算符定义）。该原语也常用于计算数据统计量，如度数。关系$R\left( e\right)$中值$a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$的度数定义为该关系在属性$x$上具有该值的元组数量，即$\left| {{\sigma }_{x = a}R\left( e\right) }\right|$。每个元组$t \in  R\left( e\right)$被视为具有"键"${\pi }_{X}t$和"值"1。

Semi-Join [16]. Given two relations ${R}_{1}$ and ${R}_{2}$ with a common attribute $v$ ,the semi-join ${R}_{1} \ltimes  {R}_{2}$ returns all the tuples in ${R}_{1}$ whose value on $v$ matches that of at least one tuple in ${R}_{2}$ . For any acyclic join, all dangling tuples, i.e., those that will not participate in the full join results, can be removed by a series of semi-joins [27].

半连接(Semi-Join) [16]。给定两个具有共同属性$v$的关系${R}_{1}$和${R}_{2}$，半连接${R}_{1} \ltimes  {R}_{2}$会返回${R}_{1}$中所有在该属性上与${R}_{2}$至少一个元组匹配的元组。对于任何无环连接，所有悬垂元组（即不会参与完整连接结果的元组）都可以通过一系列半连接操作消除[27]。

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{10}$ The ${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$ has optimal fractional edge quasi-packing number ${\psi }^{ * } = 3$ .

${}^{10}$ 该${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$具有最优分数边拟填充数${\psi }^{ * } = 3$。

${}^{11}$ The dual of a join query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is define as ${Q}^{\prime } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime },\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,where each vertex

${}^{11}$ 连接查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$的对偶定义为${Q}^{\prime } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime },\mathcal{E}}\right)$，其中每个顶点

$v \in  \mathcal{V}$ is an hyperedge in ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ and each hyperedge $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ in a vertex in ${\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }$ . Vertex $e \in  {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }$ is included by edge $v \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ if $v \in  e$ in $Q$ .

$v \in  \mathcal{V}$ 是${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$中的超边，而每条超边$e \in  \mathcal{E}$是${\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }$中的顶点。当$v \in  e$在$Q$中时，边$v \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$包含顶点$e \in  {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }$。

${}^{12}$ In [18],the term "simple" is used as equivalent to "reduced".

${}^{12}$ 在[18]中，术语"simple"被用作"reduced"的同义词。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

Parallel-packing [16]. Given $N$ numbers ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},\cdots ,{x}_{N}$ where $0 < {x}_{i} \leq  1$ for $i \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack$ ,group them into $m$ sets ${Y}_{1},{Y}_{2},\cdots ,{Y}_{m}$ such that $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  {Y}_{j}}}{x}_{i} \leq  1$ for all $j$ ,and $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  {Y}_{j}}}{x}_{i} \geq  \frac{1}{2}$ for all but one $j$ . Initially,the $N$ numbers are distributed arbitrarily across all servers, and the algorithm should produce all pairs(i,j)if $i \in  {Y}_{j}$ when done. Note that $m \leq  1 + 2\mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}{x}_{i}$ .

并行填充(Parallel-packing) [16]。给定$N$个数字${x}_{1},{x}_{2},\cdots ,{x}_{N}$，其中对于$i \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack$有$0 < {x}_{i} \leq  1$，将它们分组为$m$个集合${Y}_{1},{Y}_{2},\cdots ,{Y}_{m}$，使得对所有$j$满足$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  {Y}_{j}}}{x}_{i} \leq  1$，且除一个$j$外都满足$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  {Y}_{j}}}{x}_{i} \geq  \frac{1}{2}$。初始时，这些数字任意分布在所有服务器上，算法完成时应输出所有满足$i \in  {Y}_{j}$的(i,j)对。注意$m \leq  1 + 2\mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}{x}_{i}$。

## 3 UPPER BOUND FOR ACYCLIC JOINS

## 3 无环连接的上界

In this section, we study how to compute the class of acyclic joins efficiently in the MPC model. Before dividing into the algorithmic details, we first define the following notions to simplify our description (notations used by the algorithm are in Table 2).

本节研究如何在MPC模型中高效计算无环连接类。在深入算法细节前，我们先定义以下概念简化描述（算法使用的符号见表2）。

In a join query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,denote ${\mathcal{E}}^{\mathbf{x}} = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E} : \mathbf{x} \in  e\}$ as the set of relations containing attribute $\mathbf{x} \in  \mathcal{V}$ . Recall that in an acyclic join,its relations can be organized into a join tree $\mathcal{T}$ such that for each attribute, the nodes containing this attribute form a connected subtree in $\mathcal{T}$ . An example is illustrated in Figure 4. To be more general, $\mathcal{T}$ could be a forest consisting a set of node-disjoint connected subtrees, such that each one is a valid single join tree, and the union of nodes over all subtrees is exactly the set of relations $\mathcal{E}$ . In this way,if there exists some relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ which doesn’t share any common attributes with other relations, then we just treat it as a single connected component of $\mathcal{T}$ . In a join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,an attribute is unique if it only appears in one node.

在连接查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$中，用${\mathcal{E}}^{\mathbf{x}} = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E} : \mathbf{x} \in  e\}$表示包含属性$\mathbf{x} \in  \mathcal{V}$的关系集合。无环连接的关系可组织成连接树$\mathcal{T}$，其中每个属性对应的节点在$\mathcal{T}$中形成连通子树（示例见图4）。更一般地，$\mathcal{T}$可以是由节点互不相交的连通子树组成的森林，每棵子树都是有效的单连接树，且所有子树的节点并集正好是关系集$\mathcal{E}$。若存在不与其他关系共享属性的关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$，则将其视为$\mathcal{T}$的单独连通分量。在连接树中，仅出现在一个节点的属性称为唯一属性。

图4：连接查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$的联结树$\mathcal{T}$，其中$\mathcal{V} = \{ A,B,C,D,E,F,G,H,I,J,K\}$与$\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{0}\left( {ABCH}\right) ,{e}_{1}\left( {ABD}\right) }\right.$、$\left. {{e}_{2}\left( {BCE}\right) ,{e}_{3}\left( {ACF}\right) ,{e}_{4}\left( {ABHJ}\right) ,{e}_{5}\left( {AHI}\right) ,{e}_{6}\left( {AIK}\right) ,{e}_{7}\left( {AIG}\right) }\right\}$。

### 3.1 Generic Join Algorithm

### 3.1 通用连接算法

We describe our generic algorithm for computing the result $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ on the input join tree $\mathcal{T}$ . The high-level idea is to recursively decompose the join into multiple subqueries based on data statistics, and apply a different join strategy for each subquery. For a clean presentation, we only focus on the algorithmic description now and delay its analysis to Section 3.2 and Section 4.2.

我们描述用于在输入联结树$\mathcal{T}$上计算结果$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$的通用算法。核心思想是根据数据统计信息递归地将连接分解为多个子查询，并为每个子查询应用不同的连接策略。为清晰表述，当前仅聚焦算法描述，其分析将延至3.2节与4.2节。

Our algorithm chooses a fixed threshold $L$ ,whose value will be determined later. Moreover,we introduce $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  \subseteq  {2}^{\mathcal{E}}$ as a set of subsets of relations,and a quantity $\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$ for each subset of relations $S \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ ,which together determine the complexity of our algorithm. We give more details in Section 3.2 and Section 4.2. Note that $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  \subseteq  {2}^{\mathcal{E}}$ can be computed locally since the query has constant size. Intuitively, $\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$ is the number of servers required for computing the join query induced by relations in $S$ with load complexity $O\left( L\right)$ .

本算法选择固定阈值$L$（其值将在后续确定），并引入关系子集集合$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  \subseteq  {2}^{\mathcal{E}}$及每个关系子集$S \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$对应的量$\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$，二者共同决定算法复杂度。详见3.2节与4.2节。注意$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  \subseteq  {2}^{\mathcal{E}}$可本地计算，因查询规模恒定。直观上，$\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$表示以$O\left( L\right)$负载复杂度计算$S$内关系所导出的连接查询时所需的服务器数量。

Base Case. When there is only one relation in $Q$ ,say $\mathcal{E} = \{ e\}$ ,we just let all servers emit tuples in $R\left( e\right)$ directly.

基础情形。当$Q$中仅存单个关系（如$\mathcal{E} = \{ e\}$）时，直接令所有服务器输出$R\left( e\right)$中的元组。

表2：第3.1节使用的符号说明

<!-- Media -->

General Cases. In general,we distinguish an input acyclic join $Q$ with its join tree $\mathcal{T}$ into two cases.

通用情形。通常我们将无环连接查询$Q$及其连接树$\mathcal{T}$分为两种情况。

Case I: $\mathcal{T}$ is a single join tree. We first remove dangling tuples. If there is a pair of nodes $e,{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ such that $e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ ,we just apply the semi-join $R\left( {e}^{\prime }\right)  \ltimes  R\left( e\right)$ and remove $e$ from the join query. We recursively apply this procedure until the join is reduced.

情形一：$\mathcal{T}$是单一连接树。首先移除悬空元组。若存在节点对$e,{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$满足$e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$，则应用半连接$R\left( {e}^{\prime }\right)  \ltimes  R\left( e\right)$并将$e$从连接查询中移除。递归执行此过程直至连接被简化。

On a reduced join,we start with an arbitrary leaf node ${e}_{1}$ in $\mathcal{T}$ . Denote its parent as ${e}_{0}$ . Let $\mathbf{x} \in  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{0}$ be an arbitrary join attribute between ${e}_{1}$ and ${e}_{0}$ . The algorithm chooses a subset of relations ${S}^{\mathbf{x}} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}^{\mathbf{x}}$ with ${e}_{1} \in  {S}^{\mathbf{x}}$ to tackle in this case.

在简化后的连接中，我们从连接树$\mathcal{T}$的任意叶节点${e}_{1}$开始。记其父节点为${e}_{0}$。设$\mathbf{x} \in  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{0}$为${e}_{1}$与${e}_{0}$之间的任意连接属性。算法选择具有${e}_{1} \in  {S}^{\mathbf{x}}$的关系子集${S}^{\mathbf{x}} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}^{\mathbf{x}}$进行处理。

EXAMPLE 1. Consider the reduced join query in Figure 4. Assume ${e}_{1}$ is the chosen leaf node,with ${e}_{0}$ as its parent. If $\mathbf{x} = A$ ,then ${\mathcal{E}}^{\mathbf{x}} =$ $\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{0},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5},{e}_{6},{e}_{7}}\right\}$ . If $\mathbf{x} = B$ ,then ${\mathcal{E}}^{\mathbf{x}} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{0},{e}_{4}}\right\}$ .

示例1. 考虑图4中的简化连接查询。假设叶节点${e}_{1}$被选中，其父节点为${e}_{0}$。若$\mathbf{x} = A$，则${\mathcal{E}}^{\mathbf{x}} =$$\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{0},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5},{e}_{6},{e}_{7}}\right\}$。若$\mathbf{x} = B$，则${\mathcal{E}}^{\mathbf{x}} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{0},{e}_{4}}\right\}$。

Step (1): Compute data statistics. For each value a over attribute $\mathbf{x}$ ,we compute its degree in every relation $R\left( e\right)$ for $e \in  {S}^{\mathbf{x}}$ ,using the reduce-by-key primitive. A value a over attribute $\mathbf{x}$ is heavy if its degree in $R\left( e\right)$ is greater than $L$ for any $e \in  {S}^{\mathbf{X}}$ ,and light otherwise. Denote the set of heavy value as

步骤(1)：计算数据统计量。对属性$\mathbf{x}$的每个取值a，使用键值归约原语计算其在每个关系$R\left( e\right)$中关于$e \in  {S}^{\mathbf{x}}$的度。若属性$\mathbf{x}$的某个取值a在$R\left( e\right)$中的度大于$L$（对任意$e \in  {S}^{\mathbf{X}}$），则称其为重值，否则为轻值。记重值集合为

$$
H\left( {\mathbf{x},{S}^{\mathbf{x}}}\right)  = \left\{  {\mathbf{a} \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( \mathbf{x}\right)  : \exists e \in  {S}^{\mathbf{x}},\left| {{\sigma }_{\mathbf{x} = \mathbf{a}}R\left( e\right) }\right|  \geq  L}\right\}  .
$$

Note that $\left| {H\left( {\mathbf{x},{S}^{\mathbf{x}}}\right) }\right|  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  {S}^{\mathbf{x}}}}\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}$ . Moreover,for all light values over attribute $\mathbf{x}$ ,we run the parallel-packing primitive to put them into $k = O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  {S}^{\mathrm{x}}}}\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}}\right)$ groups ${I}_{1},{I}_{2},\cdots ,{I}_{k}$ ,where the values in each group have a total degree of $O\left( L\right)$ in ${ \cup  }_{e \in  {S}^{ \times  }}R\left( e\right)$ .

注意$\left| {H\left( {\mathbf{x},{S}^{\mathbf{x}}}\right) }\right|  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  {S}^{\mathbf{x}}}}\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}$。此外，对属性$\mathbf{x}$的所有轻值，我们使用并行打包原语将其分组为$k = O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  {S}^{\mathrm{x}}}}\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}}\right)$个集合${I}_{1},{I}_{2},\cdots ,{I}_{k}$，每个集合中的值在${ \cup  }_{e \in  {S}^{ \times  }}R\left( e\right)$中的总度数为$O\left( L\right)$。

Step (2): Decompose the join query. In this way, we can decompose the original join query into multiple subqueries:

步骤(2)：分解连接查询。通过这种方式，原始连接查询可被分解为多个子查询：

$$
{Q}_{?}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = { \bowtie  }_{e \in  \mathcal{E}}{\sigma }_{?}R\left( e\right) 
$$

where ? is either $\mathbf{x} = \mathbf{a}$ for some $\mathbf{a} \in  H\left( {\mathbf{x},{S}^{\mathbf{x}}}\right)$ or $\mathbf{x} \in  {I}_{j}$ for some $j \in  \{ 1,2,\cdots ,k\}$ . There are $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  {S}^{\mathrm{x}}}}\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}}\right)$ subqueries in total.

其中 ? 可以是某个 $\mathbf{x} = \mathbf{a}$ 对应的 $\mathbf{a} \in  H\left( {\mathbf{x},{S}^{\mathbf{x}}}\right)$，或是某个 $j \in  \{ 1,2,\cdots ,k\}$ 对应的 $\mathbf{x} \in  {I}_{j}$。总计存在 $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  {S}^{\mathrm{x}}}}\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}}\right)$ 个子查询。

We introduce a residual join query ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{x}} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{x}},{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)$ by removing $\mathbf{x}$ from all relations,where ${\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{x}} = \mathcal{V} - \{ \mathbf{x}\}$ and ${\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}} = \{ e - \mathbf{x} : e \in  \mathcal{E}\}$ . Each heavy value $\mathbf{a} \in  H\left( {\mathbf{x},{S}^{\mathbf{x}}}\right)$ derives an instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}} = \left\{  {{\sigma }_{\mathbf{x} = \mathbf{a}}R\left( e\right) }\right.$ : $e \in  \mathcal{E}\}$ for the subquery ${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$ . Similarly,each light group ${I}_{j}$ derives an instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}} = \left\{  {{\sigma }_{\mathbf{x} \in  {I}_{j}}R\left( e\right)  : e \in  \mathcal{E}}\right\}$ for the join query $\mathcal{Q}$ . Note that all subqueries have disjoint results and their union is exactly the result of original join, i.e.,

我们通过从所有关系中移除 $\mathbf{x}$ 引入残差连接查询 ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{x}} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{x}},{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)$，其中 ${\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{x}} = \mathcal{V} - \{ \mathbf{x}\}$ 且 ${\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}} = \{ e - \mathbf{x} : e \in  \mathcal{E}\}$。每个重度值 $\mathbf{a} \in  H\left( {\mathbf{x},{S}^{\mathbf{x}}}\right)$ 会为子查询 ${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$ 生成一个实例 ${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}} = \left\{  {{\sigma }_{\mathbf{x} = \mathbf{a}}R\left( e\right) }\right.$：$e \in  \mathcal{E}\}$。类似地，每个轻度分组 ${I}_{j}$ 会为连接查询 $\mathcal{Q}$ 生成实例 ${\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}} = \left\{  {{\sigma }_{\mathbf{x} \in  {I}_{j}}R\left( e\right)  : e \in  \mathcal{E}}\right\}$。注意所有子查询结果互不相交，其并集恰好构成原始连接结果，即

$$
\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  = \left( {{ \cup  }_{\mathbf{a} \in  H\left( {\mathbf{x},{S}^{\mathbf{x}}}\right) }{\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{x}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}}\right) }\right)  \cup  \left( {{ \cup  }_{j \in  \{ 1,2,\cdots ,k\} }\mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}\right) }\right) 
$$

thus, the completeness is guaranteed.

因此可确保完备性。

Step (3): Compute all subqueries in parallel. The next step is to allocate appropriate number of servers to each subquery and compute them in parallel. For each subquery ${Q}_{\mathrm{x}}$ with input instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}}$ ,we allocate ${p}_{\mathbf{a}} = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}\right) }}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil$ servers, and invoke the whole algorithm for computing ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{x}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}}\right)$ recursively.

步骤(3)：并行计算所有子查询。下一步是为每个子查询分配适量服务器并进行并行计算。对于输入实例为 ${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}}$ 的子查询 ${Q}_{\mathrm{x}}$，我们分配 ${p}_{\mathbf{a}} = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}\right) }}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil$ 台服务器，并递归调用完整算法来计算 ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{x}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}}\right)$。

Let $y$ be the set of unique attributes contained by any relation in ${S}^{\mathbf{x}}$ . We introduce a residual query ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{y}},{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{y}}}\right)$ by removing all attributes in $\mathbf{y}$ and relations in ${S}^{\mathbf{x}}$ ,where ${\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{y}} = \mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}$ and ${\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{y}} = \mathcal{E} - {S}^{\mathbf{x}}$ . Let ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ be the resulting join tree by removing nodes in ${S}^{\mathbf{x}}$ from $\mathcal{T}$ ,which may contain multiple connected subtrees.

设 $y$ 为 ${S}^{\mathbf{x}}$ 中任意关系包含的唯一属性集合。我们通过移除 $\mathbf{y}$ 中所有属性及 ${S}^{\mathbf{x}}$ 中的关系来引入残差查询 ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{y}},{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{y}}}\right)$，其中 ${\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{y}} = \mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}$ 且 ${\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{y}} = \mathcal{E} - {S}^{\mathbf{x}}$。设 ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ 为从 $\mathcal{T}$ 移除 ${S}^{\mathbf{x}}$ 中节点后得到的连接树，该树可能包含多个连通子树。

For each subquery $Q$ with input instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}$ ,we allocate ${p}_{j} =$ $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{y}}\right) }}\left\lbrack  {\Psi \left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}},S,L}\right) }\right\rbrack$ servers. To compute $\mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}\right)$ ,we first broadcast all tuples in ${ \cup  }_{e \in  {S}^{\mathbf{x}}}{\sigma }_{\mathbf{x} \in  {I}_{j}}R\left( e\right)$ to the ${p}_{j}$ servers and then compute ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}\right)$ by running the whole algorithm recursively. At last,each server just emits the combination $\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2}}\right)$ for each join result ${t}_{1} \in  { \bowtie  }_{e \in  {S}^{\mathbf{x}}}{\sigma }_{\mathbf{x} \in  {I}_{j}}R\left( e\right)$ and each join result ${t}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}\right)$ if they can be joined by local computation.

对于每个输入实例为${\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}$的子查询$Q$，我们分配${p}_{j} =$$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{y}}\right) }}\left\lbrack  {\Psi \left( {{\mathcal{T}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}},S,L}\right) }\right\rbrack$台服务器。计算$\mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}\right)$时，首先将${ \cup  }_{e \in  {S}^{\mathbf{x}}}{\sigma }_{\mathbf{x} \in  {I}_{j}}R\left( e\right)$中的所有元组广播到${p}_{j}$台服务器，然后通过递归运行整个算法来计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}\right)$。最终，每台服务器仅针对每个连接结果${t}_{1} \in  { \bowtie  }_{e \in  {S}^{\mathbf{x}}}{\sigma }_{\mathbf{x} \in  {I}_{j}}R\left( e\right)$和${t}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}\right)$（若可通过本地计算连接）输出组合$\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2}}\right)$。

EXAMPLE 2. Continue with the example in Figure 4. Assume ${e}_{1}$ is the leaf node with ${e}_{0}$ as its parent. If $\mathbf{x} = A$ and ${S}^{\mathbf{x}} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{0}}\right\}$ ,Figure 5 shows the join trees for residual join queries in step (2).

示例2. 延续图4中的示例。假设${e}_{1}$是父节点为${e}_{0}$的叶节点。若$\mathbf{x} = A$且${S}^{\mathbf{x}} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{0}}\right\}$，图5展示了步骤(2)中残余连接查询的连接树。

Case II: $\mathcal{T}$ consists of multiple connected subtrees. Let ${\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\mathcal{T}}_{2}$ , $\ldots ,{\mathcal{T}}_{k}$ be the connected subtrees in $\mathcal{T}$ and ${\mathcal{E}}_{1},{\mathcal{E}}_{2},\ldots ,{\mathcal{E}}_{k}$ be the corresponding set of relations in each subtree. Define ${\mathcal{R}}_{i} = \{ R\left( e\right)$ : $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{i}\}$ ,and ${\mathcal{Q}}_{i} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{i},{\mathcal{E}}_{i}}\right)$ ,where ${\mathcal{V}}_{i} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{i}}}e$ . In this case, it computes a Cartesian product ${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{1}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{Q}}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{k}\right)$ ,where computing each ${Q}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$ is captured by Case I.

情形二：$\mathcal{T}$由多个连通子树组成。设${\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\mathcal{T}}_{2}$、$\ldots ,{\mathcal{T}}_{k}$为$\mathcal{T}$中的连通子树，${\mathcal{E}}_{1},{\mathcal{E}}_{2},\ldots ,{\mathcal{E}}_{k}$为各子树中对应的关系集合。定义${\mathcal{R}}_{i} = \{ R\left( e\right)$：$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{i}\}$，且${\mathcal{Q}}_{i} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{i},{\mathcal{E}}_{i}}\right)$（其中${\mathcal{V}}_{i} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{i}}}e$）。此情况下，系统将计算笛卡尔积${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{1}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{Q}}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{k}\right)$，其中每个${Q}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$的计算归入情形一处理。

We arrange servers into a ${p}_{1} \times  {p}_{2} \times  \cdots  \times  {p}_{k}$ hypercube,where

我们将服务器排列成${p}_{1} \times  {p}_{2} \times  \cdots  \times  {p}_{k}$超立方体结构，其中

$$
{p}_{i} = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{i}\right) }}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\mathcal{R}}_{i},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil  .
$$

Each server is identified with coordinates $\left( {{c}_{1},{c}_{2},\cdots ,{c}_{k}}\right)$ ,where ${c}_{i} \in  \left\lbrack  {p}_{i}\right\rbrack$ . For every combination ${c}_{1},\ldots ,{c}_{i - 1},{c}_{i + 1},\ldots ,{c}_{k}$ ,the ${p}_{i}$ servers with coordinates $\left( {{c}_{1},\cdots ,{c}_{i - 1},*,{c}_{i + 1},\cdots ,{c}_{k}}\right)$ form a group to compute ${Q}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$ (using the algorithm under Case I). Consider a particular server $\left( {{c}_{1},{c}_{2},\ldots ,{c}_{k}}\right)$ . It participates in $k$ groups,one for each ${Q}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right) ,i = 1,\ldots ,k$ . For each ${Q}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$ ,it emits a subset of its join results,denoted ${Q}_{i}\left( {{\mathcal{R}}_{i},{c}_{1}\ldots ,{c}_{k}}\right)$ . Then the server computes the Cartesian product ${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\left( {{\mathcal{R}}_{1},{c}_{1}\ldots ,{c}_{k}}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{Q}}_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{R}}_{k},{c}_{1}\ldots ,{c}_{k}}\right)$ locally and emit the join results if the participating tuples can be truly joined. Note that for each group of servers computing ${\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$ , the ${p}_{i}$ servers in the group emit ${Q}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$ with no redundancy,so there is no redundancy in emitting the join result.

每个服务器通过坐标$\left( {{c}_{1},{c}_{2},\cdots ,{c}_{k}}\right)$进行标识，其中${c}_{i} \in  \left\lbrack  {p}_{i}\right\rbrack$。对于每个组合${c}_{1},\ldots ,{c}_{i - 1},{c}_{i + 1},\ldots ,{c}_{k}$，具有坐标$\left( {{c}_{1},\cdots ,{c}_{i - 1},*,{c}_{i + 1},\cdots ,{c}_{k}}\right)$的${p}_{i}$台服务器组成一个计算组来运算${Q}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$（采用情形I下的算法）。考虑特定服务器$\left( {{c}_{1},{c}_{2},\ldots ,{c}_{k}}\right)$，它会参与$k$个计算组，每组对应一个${Q}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right) ,i = 1,\ldots ,k$。对于每个${Q}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$，该服务器会发射其连接结果的子集（记作${Q}_{i}\left( {{\mathcal{R}}_{i},{c}_{1}\ldots ,{c}_{k}}\right)$），然后在本地计算笛卡尔积${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\left( {{\mathcal{R}}_{1},{c}_{1}\ldots ,{c}_{k}}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{Q}}_{k}\left( {{\mathcal{R}}_{k},{c}_{1}\ldots ,{c}_{k}}\right)$，若参与元组能实际连接则发射连接结果。需注意：对于每组计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$的服务器，组内${p}_{i}$台服务器发射的${Q}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$不存在冗余，因此连接结果的发射过程也无冗余。

In step (2), ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{x}} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{x}},{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)$ where ${\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{x}} = \{ B,C,D,E,F,G,H,I,J,K\}$ and ${\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}} = \left\{  {{e}_{0}\left( {BCH}\right) }\right.$ , ${e}_{1}\left( {BD}\right) ,{e}_{2}\left( {BCE}\right) ,{e}_{3}\left( {CF}\right) ,{e}_{4}\left( {BHJ}\right) ,{e}_{5}\left( {HI}\right) ,{e}_{6}\left( {IK}\right) ,{e}_{7}\left( {IG}\right) \}$ ,and ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{y}},{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{y}}}\right)$ where ${\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{y}} = \{ A,B,C,E,F,G,H,I,J,K\}$ and ${\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{y}} =$ $\left\{  {{e}_{2}\left( {BCE}\right) ,{e}_{3}\left( {ACF}\right) ,{e}_{4}\left( {ABHJ}\right) ,{e}_{5}\left( {AHI}\right) ,{e}_{6}\left( {AIK}\right) ,{e}_{7}\left( {AIG}\right) }\right\}$ .
