### 3.2 Analysis

### 3.2 分析

In this part, we analyze the complexity of the generic algorithm. To illustrate the key idea, we present a detailed analysis for one simple class of runs. The analysis for other runs follows the same framework, but users can choose more complicated functions of $\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$ and $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$ ,as long as they satisfy the recurrence formulas implied by the generic algorithm.

本部分将分析通用算法的复杂度。为阐明核心思想，我们针对一类简单运行过程展开详细分析。其他运行情况的分析遵循相同框架，用户只需确保所选$\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$与$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$的复杂函数满足通用算法隐含的递推公式。

As an example, we will focus on the most conservative run if the generic algorithm always chooses ${S}^{\mathbf{X}} = \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}$ in Case I. Before diving into the details, we introduce the notion of subjoin first.

以最保守运行为例，若通用算法在案例I中始终选择${S}^{\mathbf{X}} = \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}$。在深入细节前，我们首先引入子连接(subjoin)概念。

Definition 1 (Subjoin). For a join query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ with join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,and instance $\mathcal{R}$ ,the subjoin of a subset of relations $S \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ is

定义1（子连接）。对于具有连接树$\mathcal{T}$的连接查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$及实例$\mathcal{R}$，关系子集$S \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$的子连接为

$$
 \oplus  \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S}\right)  = { \times  }_{{S}_{i} \in  \mathcal{T}\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack  }{ \bowtie  }_{e \in  {S}_{i}}R\left( e\right) 
$$

where $\mathcal{T}\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack$ is the set of maximally connected components of $S$ on $\mathcal{T}$ .

其中$\mathcal{T}\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack$表示$S$在$\mathcal{T}$上的最大连通分量集合。

EXAMPLE 3. Let's take two examples in Figure 4 for illustration. ${S}_{1} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{3},{e}_{7}}\right\}$ is a single connected component since all share the common attribute $A$ ; however,they are not directly connected on the join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,so $\mathcal{T}\left\lbrack  {S}_{1}\right\rbrack   = \left\{  {\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{7}\right\}  }\right\}$ . Adding one more edge ${e}_{0}$ to ${S}_{1}$ yields ${S}_{2} = \left\{  {{e}_{0},{e}_{1},{e}_{3},{e}_{7}}\right\}$ . Note that ${S}_{2}$ is still a single connected component,but ${e}_{0},{e}_{1},{e}_{3}$ form a connected component on the join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,so $\mathcal{T}\left\lbrack  {S}_{2}\right\rbrack   = \left\{  {\left\{  {{e}_{0},{e}_{1},{e}_{3}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{7}\right\}  }\right\}$ . The subjoin of ${S}_{1}$ is defined as $\oplus  \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},{S}_{1}}\right)  = R\left( {e}_{1}\right)  \times  R\left( {e}_{3}\right)  \times  R\left( {e}_{7}\right)$ and that of ${S}_{2}$ is defined as $\oplus  \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},{S}_{2}}\right)  = \left( {R\left( {e}_{0}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{3}\right) }\right)  \times  R\left( {e}_{7}\right) .$

例3. 我们以图4中的两个示例进行说明。${S}_{1} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{3},{e}_{7}}\right\}$是单一连通分量，因为所有元素共享共同属性$A$；然而它们在连接树$\mathcal{T}$上并不直接相连，因此$\mathcal{T}\left\lbrack  {S}_{1}\right\rbrack   = \left\{  {\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{7}\right\}  }\right\}$。向${S}_{1}$添加一条边${e}_{0}$得到${S}_{2} = \left\{  {{e}_{0},{e}_{1},{e}_{3},{e}_{7}}\right\}$。注意${S}_{2}$仍是单一连通分量，但${e}_{0},{e}_{1},{e}_{3}$在连接树$\mathcal{T}$上形成连通分量，故$\mathcal{T}\left\lbrack  {S}_{2}\right\rbrack   = \left\{  {\left\{  {{e}_{0},{e}_{1},{e}_{3}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{7}\right\}  }\right\}$。${S}_{1}$的子连接定义为$\oplus  \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},{S}_{1}}\right)  = R\left( {e}_{1}\right)  \times  R\left( {e}_{3}\right)  \times  R\left( {e}_{7}\right)$，${S}_{2}$的子连接定义为$\oplus  \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},{S}_{2}}\right)  = \left( {R\left( {e}_{0}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{3}\right) }\right)  \times  R\left( {e}_{7}\right) .$

From Example 3,we can see the difference between subjoin of $S$ and the join result of relations in $S$ ,or even the projection of final join results on attributes appearing in any relation of $S$ ,i.e.,

从例3可以看出$S$的子连接与$S$中关系的连接结果之间的差异，甚至不同于$S$中任意关系属性上最终连接结果的投影，即

$$
{\pi }_{S}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  \subseteq  { \bowtie  }_{e \in  S}R\left( e\right)  \subseteq   \oplus  \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S}\right) 
$$

Moreover, we mention an important observation for acyclic join as follows, which will be used in our analysis.

此外，我们针对无环连接提出以下重要观察结论，该结论将在后续分析中使用。

LEMMA 2. For an acyclic join $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ with its join tree $\mathcal{T}$ , consider an arbitrary leaf node ${e}_{1}$ and its parent ${e}_{0}$ . For any $e \in$ $\mathcal{E} - \left\{  {{e}_{0},{e}_{1}}\right\}  ,\left( {e \cap  {e}_{1}}\right)  - {e}_{0} = \varnothing .$

引理2. 对于具有连接树$\mathcal{T}$的无环连接$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，考虑任意叶节点${e}_{1}$及其父节点${e}_{0}$。对于任意$e \in$$\mathcal{E} - \left\{  {{e}_{0},{e}_{1}}\right\}  ,\left( {e \cap  {e}_{1}}\right)  - {e}_{0} = \varnothing .$

Proof. By contradiction,assume $v \in  \left( {e \cap  {e}_{1}}\right)  - {e}_{0}$ . Implied by the definition of join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,all edges containing $v$ form a connected subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ . As ${e}_{1}$ is only connected to ${e}_{0},v \in  {e}_{0}$ if $v \in  e \cap  {e}_{1}$ , coming to a contradiction.

证明. 用反证法，假设$v \in  \left( {e \cap  {e}_{1}}\right)  - {e}_{0}$。根据连接树$\mathcal{T}$的定义，所有包含$v$的边构成$\mathcal{T}$的连通子树。由于${e}_{1}$仅当$v \in  e \cap  {e}_{1}$时与${e}_{0},v \in  {e}_{0}$相连，故产生矛盾。

THEOREM 3. For a join query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ with join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,an instance $\mathcal{R}$ and a parameter $L$ ,the $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be computed using $O\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) }\right)$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load complexity $O\left( L\right)$ ,where $\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)  = \frac{\left| \oplus \left( \mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S\right) \right| }{{L}^{\left| S\right| }}$ and $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  = {2}^{\mathcal{E}}$ .

定理3. 对于具有连接树$\mathcal{T}$的连接查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，给定实例$\mathcal{R}$和参数$L$，$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可通过$O\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) }\right)$台服务器在$O\left( 1\right)$轮计算完成，其负载复杂度为$O\left( L\right)$，其中$\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)  = \frac{\left| \oplus \left( \mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S\right) \right| }{{L}^{\left| S\right| }}$且$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  = {2}^{\mathcal{E}}$。

Proof. We first prove the complexity of the generic algorithm in Theorem 3 by induction on the size of $Q$ ,and then show how to compute $\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$ efficiently at last.

证明. 我们首先通过对$Q$规模的归纳来证明定理3中通用算法的复杂度，最后说明如何高效计算$\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$。

In the base case when there is only one relation,say $\mathcal{E} = \{ e\}$ , emitting all tuples in $R\left( e\right)$ with $\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}$ servers achieves a load of $O\left( L\right)$ , matching the bound in Theorem 3,since $\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},\{ e\} ,L}\right)  = \frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}$ . In general, we first point out that all primitives can be computed using $O\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}}\right)$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load complexity $O\left( L\right)$ . As $\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L} = \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},\{ e\} ,L}\right)$ ,the complexity of these primitives can be bounded by Theorem 3. Next we will analyze the complexity for two cases separately.

基础情形下当仅存在一个关系（如$\mathcal{E} = \{ e\}$）时，使用$\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}$台服务器发射$R\left( e\right)$中所有元组可实现$O\left( L\right)$的负载，这与定理3中的界相符，因为$\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},\{ e\} ,L}\right)  = \frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}$。一般而言，我们首先指出所有基本操作均可通过$O\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}}\right)$台服务器在$O\left( 1\right)$轮内以$O\left( L\right)$的负载复杂度完成。由于$\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L} = \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},\{ e\} ,L}\right)$，这些基本操作的复杂度可由定理3界定。接下来我们将分别分析两种情形的复杂度。

Case I. Recall that the algorithm only peels a leaf ${e}_{1}$ from its parent node ${e}_{0}$ ,where ${e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{0} \neq  \varnothing$ . In this case,a single join tree won’t be broken except at ${e}_{1}$ . We have the following hypotheses for handling the subqueries ${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$ and ${Q}_{\mathbf{y}}$ separately,which will be used later.

情形I. 回顾该算法仅从其父节点${e}_{0}$剥离叶节点${e}_{1}$，其中${e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{0} \neq  \varnothing$。在此情形下，除${e}_{1}$外单个连接树不会被破坏。我们有以下分别处理子查询${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$和${Q}_{\mathbf{y}}$的假设，后续将使用这些假设。

Hypothesis 1. For a join query ${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$ with join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,an input instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}}$ ,and a pre-determined parameter $L$ ,the result can be computed using $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right) }\right)$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load complexity $O\left( L\right)$ .

假设1. 对于具有连接树$\mathcal{T}$的连接查询${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$，给定输入实例${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}}$和预设参数$L$，结果可通过$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right) }\right)$台服务器在$O\left( 1\right)$轮内以$O\left( L\right)$的负载复杂度计算得出。

Hypothesis 2. For a join query ${Q}_{\mathrm{y}}$ with join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,an instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}$ and a pre-determined parameter $L$ ,the result ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}\right)$ can be computed using $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{y}}}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}},S,L}\right) }\right)$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load complexity $O\left( L\right)$ .

假设2. 对于具有连接树$\mathcal{T}$的连接查询${Q}_{\mathrm{y}}$，给定实例${\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}$和预设参数$L$，结果${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}\right)$可通过$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{y}}}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}},S,L}\right) }\right)$台服务器在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内计算完成，其负载复杂度为$O\left( L\right)$。

Complexity of computing heavy subqueries. Implied by hypothesis 2 , it remains to bound the number of servers allocated over all heavy values as follows:

计算重型子查询的复杂度。由假设2可推知，仍需按如下方式限制所有重型值分配的服务器总数：

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathbf{a} \in  H\left( {\mathbf{x},\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  }\right) }}{p}_{\mathbf{a}} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathbf{a} \in  H\left( {\mathbf{x},\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  }\right) }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}}}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil  
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathbf{a} \in  H\left( {\mathbf{x},\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  }\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right) 
$$

$$
 + {2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| } \cdot  \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  ,L}\right) 
$$

where the second inequality is implied by the fact that each heavy value in $H\left( {\mathbf{x},\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  }\right)$ has degree more than $L$ in relation $R\left( {e}_{1}\right)$ . We distinguish each $S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}$ into two cases. If ${e}_{0} \notin  S$ and ${e}_{1} \notin  S$ ,the term induced on $S$ can be bounded by

其中第二个不等式源于$H\left( {\mathbf{x},\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  }\right)$中每个重型值在关系$R\left( {e}_{1}\right)$中的度数超过$L$。我们将每个$S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}$分为两种情况：若${e}_{0} \notin  S$且${e}_{1} \notin  S$，则$S$上引发的项可被限制为

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathbf{a} \in  H\left( {\mathbf{x},\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  }\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right)  \leq  \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  ,L}\right)  \cdot  \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S \cup  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  ,L}\right) 
$$

where the first inequality is implied by the fact that there are at most $O\left( \frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{1}\right) \right| }{L}\right)$ heavy values and the second one is implied by the fact that ${e}_{1}$ forms a single connected component in $\mathcal{T}\left\lbrack  {S \cup  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  }\right\rbrack$ from Lemma 2. Otherwise, ${e}_{0} \notin  S$ and ${e}_{1} \notin  S$ . This term induced on $S$ can be directly bounded by $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{\mathbf{a}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right)  \leq  \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$ .

其中第一个不等式源于最多存在$O\left( \frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{1}\right) \right| }{L}\right)$个重型值，第二个不等式由引理2可知${e}_{1}$在$\mathcal{T}\left\lbrack  {S \cup  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  }\right\rbrack$中形成单个连通分量。否则${e}_{0} \notin  S$且${e}_{1} \notin  S$，此时$S$上引发的项可直接被$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{\mathbf{a}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right)  \leq  \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$限制。

Complexity of computing light subqueries. In Step 3 of computing $\mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}\right)$ ,each server receives at most $L$ input tuples from ${\sigma }_{\mathbf{x} \in  {I}_{j}}R\left( {e}_{1}\right)$ and $O\left( L\right)$ tuples in computing ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}\right)$ by hypothesis. So this step has a load of $O\left( L\right)$ . It remains to bound that the total number of servers allocated to all light groups as follows:

计算轻型子查询的复杂度。在计算$\mathcal{Q}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}\right)$的步骤3中，根据假设，每台服务器最多从${\sigma }_{\mathbf{x} \in  {I}_{j}}R\left( {e}_{1}\right)$接收$L$个输入元组，并在计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}}\right)$时处理$O\left( L\right)$个元组，故此步骤负载为$O\left( L\right)$。仍需如下限制所有轻型组分配的服务器总数：

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{j}{p}_{j} \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{j}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{y}}}}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil  
$$

$$
 \leq  {2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| } \cdot  \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  ,L}\right)  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{y}}}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{j}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}},S,L}\right) 
$$

Recall that ${e}_{1} \notin  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{y}}$ by definition. We distinguish each $S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{y}}$ into two cases: ${e}_{0} \in  S$ and ${e}_{0} \notin  S$ . If ${e}_{0} \notin  S$ ,this term induced on $S$ can be bounded by

根据定义可知${e}_{1} \notin  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{y}}$。我们将每个$S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{y}}$分为两种情况：${e}_{0} \in  S$和${e}_{0} \notin  S$。若${e}_{0} \notin  S$，则$S$上引发的项可被限制为

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{j}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}},S,L}\right)  \leq  \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  ,L}\right)  \cdot  \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) 
$$

$$
 = \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S \cup  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  ,L}\right) 
$$

where the first inequality is implied by the fact that there are at most $O\left( \frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{1}\right) \right| }{L}\right)$ light groups and the second one is implied by the fact that ${e}_{1}$ forms a single connected component in $\mathcal{T}\left\lbrack  {S \cup  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  }\right\rbrack$ from Lemma 2. when ${e}_{0} \notin  S$ . Otherwise, ${e}_{0} \in  S$ . This term induced on $S$ can be directly bounded by $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{j}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{R}_{j},S,L}\right)  \leq  \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$ .

第一个不等式成立的原因是至多存在$O\left( \frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{1}\right) \right| }{L}\right)$个轻量组，第二个不等式源于引理2中当${e}_{0} \notin  S$时，${e}_{1}$在$\mathcal{T}\left\lbrack  {S \cup  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  }\right\rbrack$内形成单个连通分量。否则，${e}_{0} \in  S$。该项在$S$上引发的效应可直接由$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{j}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{R}_{j},S,L}\right)  \leq  \Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$界定。

Over all subqueries, the total number of servers allocated in total can be bounded by (big-Oh of)

所有子查询中，服务器分配总量上限为（大O记号）

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{x}} : {e}_{1},{e}_{0} \notin  S}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S \cup  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  ,L}\right)  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{x}} : {e}_{1} \in  S,\text{ or }{e}_{0} \in  S}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) 
$$

$$
 + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{y}} : {e}_{0} \notin  S}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S \cup  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  ,L}\right)  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{y}} : {e}_{0} \in  S}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  4 \cdot  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{E}}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) 
$$

where the last inequality is implied by the following facts:

末项不等式由以下事实推导：

- $S \cup  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}   \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ for each $S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}$ but ${e}_{0},{e}_{1} \notin  S$ ;

- 对每个$S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}$（除${e}_{0},{e}_{1} \notin  S$外）有$S \cup  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}   \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$；

- $S \cup  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}   \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ for each $S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{y}}$ but ${e}_{0} \notin  S$ ;

- 对每个$S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{y}}$（除${e}_{0} \notin  S$外）有$S \cup  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}   \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$；

- $S \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ for each $S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}$ and $S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{y}}$ ,

- 对每个$S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}$和$S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{y}}$均有$S \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$，

thus completing the induction proof for Case I.

至此完成情形I的归纳证明。

Case II. Recall that the algorithm computes the Cartesian product of ${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{1}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{Q}}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{k}\right)$ over all connected subtrees of $\mathcal{Q}$ .

情形II。需注意该算法计算的是$\mathcal{Q}$所有连通子树上${\mathcal{Q}}_{1}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{1}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{Q}}_{k}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{k}\right)$的笛卡尔积。

Hypothesis 3. For a join query ${Q}_{i}$ with join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,an input instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{i}$ ,and a pre-determined parameter $L$ ,the result ${Q}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$ can be computed using $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{i}}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{i},S,L}\right) }\right)$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load complexity $O\left( L\right)$ .

假设3。对于具有连接树$\mathcal{T}$的连接查询${Q}_{i}$，给定输入实例${\mathcal{R}}_{i}$和预设参数$L$，结果${Q}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{i}\right)$可在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内使用$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{i}}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{i},S,L}\right) }\right)$台服务器计算得出，负载复杂度为$O\left( L\right)$。

In computing the Cartesian product, each server receives at most $O\left( L\right)$ tuples from each ${Q}_{i}$ by hypothesis. So,each server has a load of $O\left( L\right)$ . It remains to bound the total number of servers used in this step. Note that the number of servers allocated is (big-Oh of)

计算笛卡尔积时，根据假设每台服务器从每个${Q}_{i}$至多接收$O\left( L\right)$个元组，故单服务器负载为$O\left( L\right)$。现需界定此步骤使用的服务器总数，其分配数量级为（大O记号）

$$
\mathop{\prod }\limits_{i}{p}_{i} \leq  \mathop{\prod }\limits_{i}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{i}}}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\mathcal{R}}_{i},{S}_{i},L}\right) }\right\rceil  
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\left( {{S}_{1},{S}_{2},\cdots ,{S}_{k}}\right)  \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{1} \times  {\mathcal{E}}_{2} \times  \cdots {\mathcal{E}}_{k}}}\mathop{\prod }\limits_{i}\Psi \left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{i},{\mathcal{R}}_{i},{S}_{i},L}\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{1} \times  {\mathcal{E}}_{2} \times  \cdots {\mathcal{E}}_{k}}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{E}}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) 
$$

where the second last inequality is implied by the definition of $\oplus$ and the last inequality is implied by ${\mathcal{E}}_{1} \times  {\mathcal{E}}_{2} \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{E}}_{k} \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ ,thus completing the induction proof for Case II.

倒数第二不等式由$\oplus$定义得出，末项不等式源自${\mathcal{E}}_{1} \times  {\mathcal{E}}_{2} \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{E}}_{k} \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$，由此完成情形II的归纳证明。

At last,we show how to compute $\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$ efficiently. Given the value of $L$ ,it boils down to computing a set of subjoins. In Case I,for each $S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{{e}_{1}}$ or $S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{y}}$ ,we use $O\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{E}}}\left\lceil  \frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}\right\rceil  }\right)$ servers to compute $\left| {\oplus \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S}\right) }\right|$ ’s over all heavy values or $\left| {\oplus \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}},S}\right) }\right|$ ’s over all light groups. Computing these statistics can be captured by a free-connex join-aggregate query ${}^{13}$

最后，我们展示如何高效计算$\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$。给定$L$的值，其本质可归结为计算一组子连接。在情况I中，对于每个$S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{{e}_{1}}$或$S \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{y}}$，我们使用$O\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{E}}}\left\lceil  \frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}\right\rceil  }\right)$台服务器计算所有重值上的$\left| {\oplus \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S}\right) }\right|$或所有轻量组上的$\left| {\oplus \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}},S}\right) }\right|$。这些统计量的计算可通过自由连接聚合查询${}^{13}$实现

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{x}}}{ \times  }_{{S}_{i} \in  \mathcal{T}\left\lbrack  S\right\rbrack  }{ \bowtie  }_{e \in  {S}_{i}}R\left( e\right) 
$$

where each tuple has weight/annotation as 1 . We invoke the algorithm in [16] to compute this query in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds,whose result is in forms of $\left( {t,w\left( t\right) }\right)$ for each value $t \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( \mathbf{x}\right)$ ,with size bounded by $O\left( \left| {R\left( {e}_{1}\right) }\right| \right)$ . If $\mathbf{a} \in  H\left( {\mathbf{x},\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  }\right) ,\left| {\oplus \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S}\right) }\right|  = w\left( \mathbf{a}\right)$ ; otherwise, we run the reduce-by-key primitive to compute $\left| {\oplus \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}},S}\right) }\right|  =$ $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathbf{a} \in  {I}_{j}}}w\left( \mathbf{a}\right)$ for all light groups. As there are $O\left( 1\right)$ subsets of relations,this step can be done in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds. In Case II,the values of ${p}_{i}$ ’s can be computed similarly. Together,this step can be done using $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},\{ e\} ,L}\right) }\right)$ servers,thus completing the whole proof for Theorem 3.

其中每个元组的权重/标注为1。我们调用文献[16]的算法在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内完成该查询，结果以$\left( {t,w\left( t\right) }\right)$形式呈现给每个值$t \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( \mathbf{x}\right)$，其大小受限于$O\left( \left| {R\left( {e}_{1}\right) }\right| \right)$。若$\mathbf{a} \in  H\left( {\mathbf{x},\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  }\right) ,\left| {\oplus \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S}\right) }\right|  = w\left( \mathbf{a}\right)$；否则执行reduce-by-key原语计算所有轻量组的$\left| {\oplus \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{{I}_{j}},S}\right) }\right|  =$$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathbf{a} \in  {I}_{j}}}w\left( \mathbf{a}\right)$。由于存在$O\left( 1\right)$个关系子集，此步骤可在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次完成。情况II中，${p}_{i}$的值可类似计算。综上，本步骤使用$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},\{ e\} ,L}\right) }\right)$台服务器即可完成，从而完善定理3的全部证明。

### 3.3 Choosing $L$

### 3.3 选择$L$

Theorem 3 displays a full trade-off between the number of servers available and the load complexity. A natural question arises, if we are only given $p$ servers,what’s the smallest load complexity that can be achieved for computing an acyclic join query in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds. We choose the value of $L$ as below:

定理3完整展现了可用服务器数量与负载复杂度之间的权衡关系。由此引出一个自然问题：若仅给定$p$台服务器，在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内计算无环连接查询时可实现的最小负载复杂度是多少。我们按如下方式选择$L$的值：

$$
L = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{E}}}{\left( \frac{\left| \Phi \left( \mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S\right) \right| }{p}\right) }^{\frac{1}{\left| S\right| }},
$$

where $S$ is taken over all subsets of $\mathcal{E}$ . It can be easily checked that for each $S \subseteq  \mathcal{E},\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)  \leq  p$ holds,thus this is feasible. Moreover,the value of $L$ can also be computed through a join-aggregate query similarly using $p$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load complexity $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{p}}\right)$ ,which is also bounded by $O\left( L\right)$ . Together,we come to the following result directly.

其中$S$取自$\mathcal{E}$的所有子集。易验证对于每个$S \subseteq  \mathcal{E},\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)  \leq  p$均成立，故该方案可行。此外，$L$的值同样可通过连接聚合查询计算，使用$p$台服务器在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内完成，负载复杂度为$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{p}}\right)$，该值亦受限于$O\left( L\right)$。综上，我们直接得出以下结论。

THEOREM 4. For an acyclic join query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ with a join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ ,the join result $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be computed using $p$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load $O\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{E}}}{\left( \frac{\left| \oplus \left( \mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S\right) \right| }{p}\right) }^{\frac{1}{\left| S\right| }}}\right)$ .

定理4. 对于具有连接树$\mathcal{T}$的无环连接查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$及实例$\mathcal{R}$，其连接结果$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内使用$p$台服务器计算完成，负载为$O\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \subseteq  \mathcal{E}}}{\left( \frac{\left| \oplus \left( \mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S\right) \right| }{p}\right) }^{\frac{1}{\left| S\right| }}}\right)$。

So far we have obtained an acyclic join algorithm whose load complexity is in terms of a set of subjoins. However, we observe a gap between Theorem 4 and our target $O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$ . Next,we use two examples to discuss the reasons behind this intrinsic gap.

目前我们已获得一个负载复杂度基于子连接集的无环连接算法。然而，我们注意到定理4与目标$O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$之间存在差距。接下来将通过两个示例探讨这一本质差距的成因。

<!-- Media -->

<!-- figureText: ABCD BCDE CDEF DEFG EFGH -->

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/0196522e-b0fd-7d0b-bd81-6797a8018bea_7.jpg?x=971&y=236&w=629&h=71&r=0"/>

Figure 6: A join tree of join query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,where $\mathcal{V} =$ $\{ A,B,C,D,E,F,G,H\}$ and $\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{1}\left( {ABCD}\right) ,{e}_{2}\left( {BCDE}\right) ,{e}_{3}\left( {CDEF}\right) }\right.$ , $\left. {{e}_{4}\left( {DEFG}\right) ,{e}_{5}\left( {EFGH}\right) }\right\}$ .

图6：连接查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$的连接树结构，其中$\mathcal{V} =$$\{ A,B,C,D,E,F,G,H\}$且$\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{1}\left( {ABCD}\right) ,{e}_{2}\left( {BCDE}\right) ,{e}_{3}\left( {CDEF}\right) }\right.$，$\left. {{e}_{4}\left( {DEFG}\right) ,{e}_{5}\left( {EFGH}\right) }\right\}$。

<!-- Media -->

EXAMPLE 4. Let's consider the example query in Figure 4, which has ${\rho }^{ * } = 6$ by choosing $\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{6},{e}_{7}}\right\}$ . Consider a hard instance constructed as below. There are $N$ distinct values in the domain of attributes $D,E,F,H,J,K,G$ and a single value in the domain of remaining attributes. Relation ${R}_{4}\left( {ABHJ}\right)$ is a one-to-one mapping over attributes $H,J$ ,and every remaining relation is a Cartesian product over its all attributes,containing $N$ tuples in total. Also, this instance has its join size matching the AGM bound as $O\left( {N}^{6}\right)$ . $\operatorname{On}{S}_{3} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{0},{e}_{5},{e}_{6},{e}_{7}}\right\}$ ,its subjoin has size as large as ${N}^{7}$ since $\left| {R\left( {e}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{2}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{3}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{0}\right) }\right|  = {N}^{4}$ and $\left| {R\left( {e}_{5}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{6}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{7}\right) }\right|  = {N}^{3}$ . Thus,our (conservative) generic algorithm computes this hard instance with load complexity $\Theta \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/7}}\right)$ , which is worse than the optimal target by a factor of $O\left( {p}^{7/6}\right)$ .

示例4. 考虑图4中的查询示例，通过选择$\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{6},{e}_{7}}\right\}$得到${\rho }^{ * } = 6$。构造如下困难实例：属性$D,E,F,H,J,K,G$域中有$N$个不同值，其余属性域均为单一值。关系${R}_{4}\left( {ABHJ}\right)$在属性$H,J$上形成一对一映射，其他关系均为其所有属性的笛卡尔积，共含$N$个元组。该实例的连接规模与AGM界$O\left( {N}^{6}\right)$相符。$\operatorname{On}{S}_{3} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{0},{e}_{5},{e}_{6},{e}_{7}}\right\}$时，由于$\left| {R\left( {e}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{2}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{3}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{0}\right) }\right|  = {N}^{4}$和$\left| {R\left( {e}_{5}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{6}\right)  \bowtie  R\left( {e}_{7}\right) }\right|  = {N}^{3}$，其子连接规模高达${N}^{7}$。因此，我们的（保守）通用算法处理该困难实例的负载复杂度为$\Theta \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/7}}\right)$，较最优目标存在$O\left( {p}^{7/6}\right)$倍的差距。

Careful inspection reveals that not every subset $S \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ appears in the cost formula of Theorem 4, depending on which choices the algorithm makes while running. A key observation is that join query is always reduced before going into recursion. For example, after peeling ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$ off by choosing attribute $\mathbf{x} = A,B,C$ sequentially,relation ${e}_{0}$ could be reduced since ${e}_{0} - {e}_{1} \cup  {e}_{2} \cup  {e}_{3} \subseteq  {e}_{4}$ . In this way, ${e}_{0}$ won’t appear together with any of ${e}_{5},{e}_{6},{e}_{7}$ ,and ${S}_{3}$ does not contributes to the cost formula of Theorem 3. Thus, this example implies that the gap partly comes from our non-tight analysis.

仔细观察发现，并非所有子集$S \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$都会出现在定理4的成本公式中，这取决于算法运行时的选择。关键观察在于：连接查询在进入递归前总会被简化。例如，通过顺序选择属性$\mathbf{x} = A,B,C$剥离${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$后，关系${e}_{0}$可被简化，因为${e}_{0} - {e}_{1} \cup  {e}_{2} \cup  {e}_{3} \subseteq  {e}_{4}$。这样${e}_{0}$不会与任何${e}_{5},{e}_{6},{e}_{7}$共同出现，且${S}_{3}$不会对定理3的成本公式产生贡献。该例说明性能差距部分源于我们的非紧致分析。

EXAMPLE 5. Let's consider another join query in Figure 6. This query has ${\rho }^{ * } = 2$ by choosing $\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{5}}\right\}$ . Consider a hard instance constructed as below. There are $N$ distinct values in the domain of all attributes and each relation is a one-to-one mapping over its attributes. This instance only has $O\left( N\right)$ join results,but our (conservative) generic algorithm computes it with load complexity $\Theta \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right)$ since $\oplus  \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{3},{e}_{5}}\right\}  }\right)  = {N}^{3}$ ,which is worse than our target by a factor of $O\left( {p}^{3/2}\right)$ . More specifically,this is tight when our (conservative) algorithm first (1) chooses ${e}_{1}$ as the leaf node with its parent ${e}_{2}$ , and $\mathbf{x} = B$ with ${S}^{\mathbf{x}} = \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}$ ; and then (2) chooses ${e}_{5}$ as the leaf node with its parent ${e}_{4}$ ,and $\mathbf{x} = G$ with ${S}^{\mathbf{x}} = \left\{  {e}_{5}\right\}$ .

示例5. 考虑图6中的另一个连接查询。该查询通过选择$\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{5}}\right\}$具有${\rho }^{ * } = 2$。构造如下困难实例：所有属性域中存在$N$个不同值，每个关系在其属性上构成一对一映射。该实例仅有$O\left( N\right)$个连接结果，但我们的（保守）通用算法以$\Theta \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right)$的负载复杂度计算，因为$\oplus  \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{3},{e}_{5}}\right\}  }\right)  = {N}^{3}$，这比目标值差$O\left( {p}^{3/2}\right)$倍。具体而言，当算法（1）先选择${e}_{1}$作为叶节点（父节点为${e}_{2}$）及$\mathbf{x} = B$（含${S}^{\mathbf{x}} = \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}$）；（2）再选择${e}_{5}$作为叶节点（父节点为${e}_{4}$）及$\mathbf{x} = G$（含${S}^{\mathbf{x}} = \left\{  {e}_{5}\right\}$）时，该结果是紧致的。

This example motivates us to seek for more aggressive choices for this generic algorithm in the next section.

此例促使我们在下一节为该通用算法寻求更激进的策略。

## 4 WORST-CASE OPTIMALITY FOR ACYCLIC JOINS

## 4 无环连接的最坏情况最优性

In this section, we take a further investigation of the generic algorithm presented in Section 3 and focus on the worst-case complexity. We identify a characterization of "good" choices that the algorithm can follow in each step and show that such a supervised run can achieve worst-case optimality on acyclic joins.

本节深入探究第3章的通用算法，聚焦最坏情况复杂度。我们提出算法每步应遵循的"优质"选择特征，证明这种受控运行能在无环连接上实现最坏情况最优。

### 4.1 Characterization of A Good Run

### 4.1 优质运行的特征

Assume the input join query is reduced. Now, we describe how to tackle a reduced acyclic join with its join tree through two steps: (1) decompose the join tree into a set of subtree and define an ordering on these connected subtrees; (2) tackle subtrees one by one following the ordering defined in (1), and invoke the generic algorithm in Section 3 for each one, following our specified algorithmic choices. We next present each step in more detail.

假设输入连接查询已简化。现通过两步处理简化后的无环连接及其连接树：（1）将连接树分解为子树集并定义排序；（2）按（1）的排序逐个处理子树，对每个子树调用第3章的通用算法并遵循指定选择。下文详述各步骤。

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{13}$ The definitions of join-aggregate query and free-connex query are provided in Appendix A.3. In short,for join-aggregate query $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{\mathbf{z}}Q$ ,if $Q$ is acyclic and $\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{z}$ is contained by one relation, this query is free-connex.

${}^{13}$ 连接-聚合查询与自由连接查询的定义见附录A.3。简言之，若连接-聚合查询$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{\mathbf{z}}Q$中的$Q$无环且$\mathcal{V} - \mathbf{z}$被某关系包含，则该查询是自由连接的。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

<!-- Media -->

<!-- figureText: 4 - nodes in the edge cover o nodes not in the edge cover -->

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/0196522e-b0fd-7d0b-bd81-6797a8018bea_8.jpg?x=211&y=232&w=1378&h=270&r=0"/>

Figure 7: The left is a decomposition of a join tree $\mathcal{T}$ into 6 twigs and a possible valid ordering of these twigs is(2,3,5,6,4,1). The right is an example of linear covering of twig 4 rooted at $r$ .

图7：左图展示连接树$\mathcal{T}$分解为6个枝杈，有效排序可能为(2,3,5,6,4,1)；右图是以$r$为根的枝杈4的线性覆盖示例。

<!-- Media -->

Step 1: Decompose a join tree. We start with an important property on the optimal fractional edge covering for acyclic joins.

步骤1：分解连接树。首先给出无环连接最优分数边覆盖的重要性质。

LEMMA 5. An acyclic join admits an integral solution for the optimal fractional edge covering number.

引理5. 无环连接的最优分数边覆盖数存在整数解。

The proof of Lemma 5 is left to Appendix A.1. Intuitively, given a join tree $\mathcal{T}$ for an acyclic join $Q$ ,such an optimal covering can be obtained through a greedy strategy by always choosing leaves (containing unique attributes) in $\mathcal{T}$ . Denote $\rho  : \mathcal{E} \rightarrow  \{ 0,1\}$ as the optimal edge covering for $Q$ and ${\mathcal{E}}_{\rho } = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E} : \rho \left( e\right)  = 1\}$ as the set of relations chosen by $\rho$ . Moreover, ${\rho }^{ * } = \left| {\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }\right|$ . As the query has constant size, $\rho$ as well as ${\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }$ can be computed locally. Based on $\rho$ , we first identify an important subclass of acyclic joins as below.

引理5的证明详见附录A.1。直观而言，给定无环连接$Q$的连接树$\mathcal{T}$，可通过贪心策略选择$\mathcal{T}$中的叶子节点（包含唯一属性）获得最优覆盖。记$\rho  : \mathcal{E} \rightarrow  \{ 0,1\}$为$Q$的最优边覆盖，${\mathcal{E}}_{\rho } = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E} : \rho \left( e\right)  = 1\}$为$\rho$选择的关系集合。此外，${\rho }^{ * } = \left| {\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }\right|$。由于查询规模恒定，$\rho$和${\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }$可本地计算。基于$\rho$，我们首先识别如下重要的无环连接子类。

DEFINITION 6 (TWIG JOIN). a reduced join $Q$ is a twig if it has a join tree $\mathcal{T}$ such that the set of leaves is an edge covering for $Q$ .

定义6（枝状连接）。若简化连接$Q$存在连接树$\mathcal{T}$使其叶子集合构成$Q$的边覆盖，则称其为枝状连接。

For an acyclic join $Q$ with a join tree $\mathcal{T}$ and an optimal integral edge covering $\rho$ ,we next break the join tree at every internal node $e \in  {S}_{p}$ . This decomposes the join tree into a number of twigs, denoted as $\mathcal{G}$ . Observe that in each twig,all the leaves are exactly the relations in ${S}_{\rho }$ (see Figure 7). Moreover,each pair of twigs share at most one common node in $\mathcal{E}$ ,and they are incident if sharing exactly one node. Later, we will see that relations in the same twig will be handled as a whole. Moreover,twigs in $\mathcal{G}$ will be tackled following some specific ordering, defined as below.

对于具有连接树$\mathcal{T}$的无环连接$Q$及其最优整数边覆盖$\rho$，我们在每个内部节点$e \in  {S}_{p}$处分解连接树。这将连接树分割为若干枝状结构，记为$\mathcal{G}$。注意每个枝状结构中，所有叶子节点恰好对应${S}_{\rho }$中的关系（见图7）。且任意两个枝状结构在$\mathcal{E}$中至多共享一个节点，若共享单节点则称为邻接。后续将看到同枝状结构的关系会被整体处理，而$\mathcal{G}$中的枝状结构将按如下定义的特定顺序处理。

Definition 7 (Ordering of Twigs in A Join Tree). An ordering of a set of twigs $\mathcal{G}$ is defined as follows: (1) it always chooses a twig ${\mathcal{G}}_{i}$ if it is incident to at most one twig in $\mathcal{G} - \left\{  {\mathcal{G}}_{i}\right\}$ ; (2) it removes ${\mathcal{G}}_{i}$ from $\mathcal{G}$ and apply this procedure recursively.

定义7（连接树中枝状结构的排序）。枝状集合$\mathcal{G}$的排序定义为：(1) 优先选择在$\mathcal{G} - \left\{  {\mathcal{G}}_{i}\right\}$中至多邻接一个枝状结构的${\mathcal{G}}_{i}$；(2) 将${\mathcal{G}}_{i}$从$\mathcal{G}$移除并递归应用此流程。

Note that there could be multiple valid orderings. An example is given in Figure 7. The correctness of the recursion in Definition 7 is guaranteed by the underlying tree hierarchy across twigs in $\mathcal{G}$ . We further define the root for each twig of $\mathcal{G}$ in this ordering.

注意可能存在多种有效排序。图7展示了示例。定义7中递归的正确性由$\mathcal{G}$中枝状结构间的树状层次关系保证。我们进一步在此排序中为$\mathcal{G}$的每个枝状结构定义根节点。

DEFINITION 8 (ROOT OF A TWIG). For an acyclic join with the join tree $\mathcal{T}$ and a set of twigs $\mathcal{G}$ :

定义8（枝状结构的根）。对于具有连接树$\mathcal{T}$的无环连接及其枝状集合$\mathcal{G}$：

- If $\left| \mathcal{G}\right|  = 1,\mathcal{T}$ is a twig and an arbitrary leaf of $\mathcal{T}$ is the root;

- 若$\left| \mathcal{G}\right|  = 1,\mathcal{T}$本身为枝状结构，则任选其叶子节点$\mathcal{T}$作为根；

- Otherwise,for each twig ${\mathcal{G}}_{i} \in  \mathcal{G}$ ,its root is the unique non-leaf node in ${\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }$ when it is chosen by (1) in Definition 7.

- 否则，每个枝状结构${\mathcal{G}}_{i} \in  \mathcal{G}$的根节点为：当其被定义7中(1)选中时，${\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }$内唯一的非叶节点。

Step 2: Decompose a twig. Now, we focus on handling a single twig join $Q$ with its join tree $\mathcal{T}$ inherited from last step,which enjoy very nice properties: (1) $Q$ is reduced; (2) the set of leaves of $\mathcal{T}$ is a valid edge covering for $Q$ ,which is also optimal. Let $r$ be the root of $\mathcal{Q}$ . Note that $r \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }$ only has one child in $\mathcal{T}$ ; otherwise,this twig will be further decomposed,coming to a contradiction. Let $c$ be the child of $r$ . To abuse notations,we also use $r,c$ to denote the sets of attributes contained by the corresponding relations separately.

步骤二：分解枝状连接。此时我们专注于处理单个枝状连接$Q$及其继承自上一步的连接树$\mathcal{T}$，它们具有以下优良特性：(1)$Q$是归约化的；(2)$\mathcal{T}$的叶节点集构成$Q$的有效边覆盖，且该覆盖是最优的。设$r$为$\mathcal{Q}$的根节点。注意$r \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }$在$\mathcal{T}$中仅有一个子节点，否则该枝状连接将被进一步分解，导致矛盾。令$c$表示$r$的子节点。为简化表述，我们同样使用$r,c$来分别表示对应关系所含的属性集合。

We next show how the generic algorithm in Section 3 proceeds on a twig. As the join tree of a twig join is a single connected component, the algorithm goes into Case I directly. Then, the question comes: Which attribute should be chosen to tackle first?

接下来展示第3节通用算法如何处理枝状连接。由于枝状连接的连接树是单一连通组件，算法直接进入情形I。此时核心问题是：应优先选择哪个属性进行处理？

We introduce the notion of first attribute in Definition 9. As $Q$ is reduced, $c - r \neq  \varnothing$ . The algorithm chooses an arbitrary first attribute of $\mathcal{T}$ as $\mathbf{x}$ and a leaf node $e$ containing $\mathbf{x}$ . Note that such a leaf node always exists; otherwise, $\mathbf{x}$ will only appear in nodes of $\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }$ , contradicting the fact that $\rho$ is a valid edge covering. Let $\operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)$ be the set of nodes lying on the path from $c$ to $e$ . The algorithm then chooses ${S}^{\mathbf{x}} = \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)$ .

我们在定义9中引入首属性概念。由于$Q$是归约化的，$c - r \neq  \varnothing$。算法随机选择$\mathcal{T}$的某个首属性作为$\mathbf{x}$，并选取包含$\mathbf{x}$的叶节点$e$。注意此类叶节点必然存在，否则$\mathbf{x}$将仅出现在$\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }$节点中，与$\rho$构成有效边覆盖的事实矛盾。设$\operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)$为从$c$到$e$路径上的节点集合，算法随后选择${S}^{\mathbf{x}} = \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)$。

Definition 9 (Finst Attribute in A Twig). In a twig join $\mathcal{T}$ rooted at $r$ with the child $c$ ,the first attribute is defined as an arbitrary one in $c - r$ .

定义9（枝状连接中的首属性）。对于以$r$为根节点、$c$为子节点的枝状连接$\mathcal{T}$，其首属性定义为$c - r$中的任意属性。

In step 2, the generic algorithm defines a set of heavy subqueries ${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$ and light subqueries ${Q}_{\mathbf{y}}$ . Note that ${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$ is also a twig join rooted at $r$ ,thus can be handled by invoking the whole algorithm recursively. For ${Q}_{\mathbf{y}}$ ,the join tree will be decomposed into a set of subtrees after removing all nodes in path(c,e). Consider an arbitrary node ${e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)$ . For each children ${c}^{\prime }$ of ${e}^{\prime }$ but ${c}^{\prime } \notin  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)$ ,the subtree rooted at ${c}^{\prime }$ together with ${e}^{\prime }$ form a twig join,with its root as ${e}^{\prime }$ ,and can be handled by invoking the whole algorithm recursively. Computing the join results of these subtrees fall into Case II, which is done by enumerating the Cartesian product of their individual join results first and then emitting true join results after checking join conditions locally.

步骤二中，通用算法定义了重型子查询集${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$与轻型子查询集${Q}_{\mathbf{y}}$。注意${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$仍是以$r$为根的枝状连接，可通过递归调用整个算法处理。对于${Q}_{\mathbf{y}}$，移除路径(c,e)上所有节点后，连接树将分解为若干子树。考虑任意节点${e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)$，对于${e}^{\prime }$除${c}^{\prime } \notin  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)$外的每个子节点${c}^{\prime }$，以${c}^{\prime }$为根的子树与${e}^{\prime }$构成新的枝状连接（根节点为${e}^{\prime }$），同样可通过递归调用处理。这些子树的连接结果计算属于情形II，需先枚举其笛卡尔积，再经本地连接条件检验后输出真实连接结果。

EXAMPLE 6. Consider the example in Figure 8, which is an instantiation of twig 4 in Figure 7. The root is (EFG) with its child as (CEF). The first attribute is $C$ ,leaf node $e$ is(ABC),and path $\left( {c,e}\right)  =$ $\{ \left( {ABC}\right) ,\left( {BCD}\right) ,\left( {CDE}\right) ,\left( {CEF}\right) \} .{Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$ is the residual join by replacing $\left( {ABC}\right) {by}\left( {AB}\right) ,\left( {BCD}\right) {by}\left( {BD}\right) ,\left( {CDE}\right) {by}\left( {DE}\right) {and}\left( {CEF}\right) {by}\left( {EF}\right)$ in $\mathcal{T}$ ,which is a twig join. ${Q}_{\mathrm{y}}$ is the residual join by removing (ABC), $\left( {BCD}\right) ,\left( {CDE}\right)$ and(CEF)from $\mathcal{T}$ . Computing ${Q}_{\mathrm{y}}$ degenerates to computing the Cartesian product of (1) $\{ \left( {EFG}\right) \} ;\left( 2\right) \{ \left( {FM}\right) \} ;\left( 3\right) \{ \left( {DU}\right) \}$ ; (4) $\{ \left( {BK}\right) \}$ ; and (5) $\{ \left( {FHJ}\right) ,\left( {FHZ}\right) ,\left( {WZ}\right) ,\left( {IJX}\right) ,\left( {HY}\right) \}$ . Note that (5) together with (CEF) is a twig join rooted at (CEF), which can be recursively decomposed.

示例6. 考虑图8中的实例，这是图7中枝状结构4的具体化。根节点为(EFG)，其子节点为(CEF)。首个属性是$C$，叶节点$e$为(ABC)，路径$\left( {c,e}\right)  =$$\{ \left( {ABC}\right) ,\left( {BCD}\right) ,\left( {CDE}\right) ,\left( {CEF}\right) \} .{Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$是通过替换$\mathcal{T}$中的$\left( {ABC}\right) {by}\left( {AB}\right) ,\left( {BCD}\right) {by}\left( {BD}\right) ,\left( {CDE}\right) {by}\left( {DE}\right) {and}\left( {CEF}\right) {by}\left( {EF}\right)$得到的残差连接，这属于枝状连接。${Q}_{\mathrm{y}}$则是通过从$\mathcal{T}$中移除(ABC)、$\left( {BCD}\right) ,\left( {CDE}\right)$及(CEF)得到的残差连接。计算${Q}_{\mathrm{y}}$退化为计算以下笛卡尔积：(1)$\{ \left( {EFG}\right) \} ;\left( 2\right) \{ \left( {FM}\right) \} ;\left( 3\right) \{ \left( {DU}\right) \}$；(4)$\{ \left( {BK}\right) \}$；(5)$\{ \left( {FHJ}\right) ,\left( {FHZ}\right) ,\left( {WZ}\right) ,\left( {IJX}\right) ,\left( {HY}\right) \}$。注意(5)与(CEF)共同构成以(CEF)为根的枝状连接，可递归分解。

The left is the join tree of $Q$ (left),the middle is ${Q}_{\mathrm{x}}$ ,and the right is ${Q}_{\mathrm{y}}$ .

图8：连接查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$上步骤2的运行实例，其中$\mathcal{V} = \{ A,B,C,D,E,F,G,H,I,J,K,M,U,W,X,Y,Z\}$和$\mathcal{E} = \{ \left( {ABC}\right) ,\left( {BCD}\right) ,\left( {CDE}\right) ,\left( {CEF}\right) ,\left( {EFG}\right) ,\left( {FHJ}\right) ,\left( {FM}\right) ,\left( {BK}\right) ,\left( {DU}\right) ,\left( {FHZ}\right) ,\left( {WZ}\right) ,\left( {IJX}\right) ,\left( {HY}\right) \}$。左侧为$Q$的连接树（左），中间是${Q}_{\mathrm{x}}$，右侧为${Q}_{\mathrm{y}}$。
