### 4.2 Analysis

### 4.2 分析

Next, we show the complexity for the generic algorithm following the choice in Section 4.1. Before diving into details, we first define

接下来，我们将根据4.1节的选择展示通用算法的复杂度。在深入细节前，首先定义：

$$
\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)  = \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{e \in  S}}\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}.
$$

Obviously,the value of $\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$ can be computed locally,since the query has constant size. As we tackle an acyclic join by decomposing it into a set of twig joins, we will start with the complexity for twig join and present the general result for acyclic join at last.

显然，$\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)$的值可本地计算，因为查询具有恒定大小。当我们将无环连接分解为一组枝状连接来处理时，将从枝状连接的复杂度开始，最后给出无环连接的通用结果。

Complexity of Linear join. We first identify a special case of twig join, which only contains two leaves.

线性连接的复杂度。我们首先识别枝状连接的特殊情况——仅包含两个叶节点。

Definition 10 (Linear join). A reduced join $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is linear if it has a join tree $\mathcal{T}$ with relations being arranged in a line staring at ${e}_{1}$ and ending at ${e}_{k}$ ,such that $e \in  {e}_{1} \cup  {e}_{k}$ for any $e \in$ $\mathcal{E} - \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{k}}\right\}$ .

定义10（线性连接）。若简化连接$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$的连接树$\mathcal{T}$中关系从${e}_{1}$到${e}_{k}$呈线性排列，且对任意$e \in$$\mathcal{E} - \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{k}}\right\}$满足$e \in  {e}_{1} \cup  {e}_{k}$，则该连接为线性连接。

Moreover,it can be easily shown that $\left( {{e}_{i} \cap  {e}_{k}}\right)  \subseteq  \left( {{e}_{i + 1} \cap  {e}_{k}}\right)$ and $\left( {{e}_{i + 1} \cap  {e}_{1}}\right)  \subseteq  \left( {{e}_{i} \cap  {e}_{1}}\right)$ for any $i \in  \{ 1,2,\cdots ,k - 1\}$ . Applying the rule in Definition 9 recursively will yield two valid orderings of attributes for a linear join,corresponding to the root being ${e}_{1}$ and ${e}_{k}$ separately. Surprisingly,a linear join can be computed very efficiently following the first-attribute-based orderings, as stated in Lemma 11.

此外，可以轻易证明对于任意$\left( {{e}_{i} \cap  {e}_{k}}\right)  \subseteq  \left( {{e}_{i + 1} \cap  {e}_{k}}\right)$和$\left( {{e}_{i + 1} \cap  {e}_{1}}\right)  \subseteq  \left( {{e}_{i} \cap  {e}_{1}}\right)$，递归应用定义9中的规则将生成线性连接的两种有效属性排序，分别对应根节点为${e}_{1}$和${e}_{k}$的情况。值得注意的是，如引理11所述，基于首属性的排序能高效计算线性连接。

LEMMA 11. For a linear join $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ with its join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,an instance $\mathcal{R}$ and a parameter $L$ ,the join result $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be computed using $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  S\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) }\right)$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load $O\left( L\right)$ ,where

引理11. 对于具有连接树$\mathcal{T}$的线性连接$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$、实例$\mathcal{R}$及参数$L$，连接结果$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可使用$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  S\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) }\right)$台服务器在$O\left( 1\right)$轮内完成计算，负载为$O\left( L\right)$，其中

$$
\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  = \{ \{ e,r\}  : e \in  \mathcal{E} - \{ r\} \}  \cup  \{ \{ e\}  : e \in  \mathcal{E}\} 
$$

for $r \in  \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{k}}\right\}$ and ${e}_{1},{e}_{k}$ being the two leaves in $\mathcal{T}$ .

当$r \in  \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{k}}\right\}$和${e}_{1},{e}_{k}$是$\mathcal{T}$中的两个叶节点时。

EXAMPLE 7. An example of linear join $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is in Figure 6, where $\mathcal{V} = \{ A,B,C,D,E,F,G,H\}$ and $\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{1}\left( {ABCD}\right) ,{e}_{2}\left( {BCDE}\right) }\right.$ , $\left. {{e}_{3}\left( {CDEF}\right) ,{e}_{4}\left( {DEFG}\right) ,{e}_{5}\left( {EFGH}\right) }\right\}$ . Two orderings of attributes are $D,C,B,A$ if root is ${e}_{5}$ and $E,F,G,H$ if root is ${e}_{1}$ . Let ${N}_{i} = \left| {R\left( {e}_{i}\right) }\right|$ for $i \in  \{ 1,2,3,4,5\}$ . If ${e}_{5}$ is the root,we have $S\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  = \left\{  {\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{5}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{5}}\right\}  }\right.$ , $\left. {\left\{  {{e}_{3},{e}_{5}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{5}\right\}  }\right\}$ . Implied by Lemma 11, it can be computed using $O\left( {\frac{1}{{L}^{2}} \cdot  {N}_{5} \cdot  \left( {{N}_{1} + {N}_{2} + {N}_{3} + {N}_{4}}\right)  + \frac{1}{L}\left( {{N}_{1} + }\right. }\right.$ $\left. \left. {{N}_{2} + {N}_{3} + {N}_{4} + {N}_{5}}\right) \right)$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load $O\left( L\right)$ .

示例7. 线性连接$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$的实例如图6所示，其中$\mathcal{V} = \{ A,B,C,D,E,F,G,H\}$和$\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{1}\left( {ABCD}\right) ,{e}_{2}\left( {BCDE}\right) }\right.$，$\left. {{e}_{3}\left( {CDEF}\right) ,{e}_{4}\left( {DEFG}\right) ,{e}_{5}\left( {EFGH}\right) }\right\}$。当根节点为${e}_{5}$时，两种属性排序方式为$D,C,B,A$；若根节点为${e}_{1}$则对应$E,F,G,H$。设${N}_{i} = \left| {R\left( {e}_{i}\right) }\right|$对应$i \in  \{ 1,2,3,4,5\}$。若${e}_{5}$为根节点，可得$S\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  = \left\{  {\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{5}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{5}}\right\}  }\right.$，$\left. {\left\{  {{e}_{3},{e}_{5}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  ,\left\{  {e}_{5}\right\}  }\right\}$。根据引理11，可通过$O\left( {\frac{1}{{L}^{2}} \cdot  {N}_{5} \cdot  \left( {{N}_{1} + {N}_{2} + {N}_{3} + {N}_{4}}\right)  + \frac{1}{L}\left( {{N}_{1} + }\right. }\right.$$\left. \left. {{N}_{2} + {N}_{3} + {N}_{4} + {N}_{5}}\right) \right)$台服务器在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内完成计算，负载为$O\left( L\right)$。

Complexity of Twig Join. Next, we move to a general twig join. To better describe the result, we introduce the notion of linear cover for a twig join first.

分支连接复杂度。接下来我们将讨论广义分支连接。为更清晰描述结果，首先引入分支连接的线性覆盖概念。

Definition 12 (Linear-covering of A TWIG). For a twig join $\mathcal{Q}$ with its join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,a linear-covering for $\mathcal{T}$ denoted as $\mathcal{P}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ ,is defined as follows:

定义12（树状联结的线性覆盖）。对于树状联结$\mathcal{Q}$及其联结树$\mathcal{T}$，记作$\mathcal{P}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$的$\mathcal{T}$的线性覆盖定义如下：

- If $\mathcal{T}$ has at most two leaves (i.e.,a linear join), $\mathcal{P}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  = \{ \mathcal{T}\}$ ;

- 若$\mathcal{T}$至多有两个叶节点（即线性联结），则$\mathcal{P}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  = \{ \mathcal{T}\}$；

- Otherwise, $\mathcal{P}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  = \{ \mathcal{L}\}  \cup  \left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{\ell }\mathcal{P}\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) }\right)$ where $\mathcal{L}$ is an arbitrary root-to-leaf path of $\mathcal{T}$ and $\left\{  {{\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\mathcal{T}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{T}}_{\ell }}\right\}$ is the set of connected subtrees by removing $\mathcal{L}$ from $\mathcal{T}$ .

- 否则，$\mathcal{P}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  = \{ \mathcal{L}\}  \cup  \left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{\ell }\mathcal{P}\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{i}\right) }\right)$其中$\mathcal{L}$是$\mathcal{T}$的任意根到叶路径，$\left\{  {{\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\mathcal{T}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{T}}_{\ell }}\right\}$是从$\mathcal{T}$移除$\mathcal{L}$后得到的连通子树集合。

An example of linear-covering of a twig join is shown in Figure 7. In Lemma 13, we use the linear cover to define the complexity of the first-attribute-based generic algorithm. Note that computing the cartesian product of node/relation-disjoint paths in the linear-covering has the similar complexity form as that in Lemma 13, but each path in the linear-covering may not be a linear join, thus may lead to much higher complexity. Our first-attribute-based generic algorithm is totally different from it.

图7展示了树状联结线性覆盖的示例。在引理13中，我们使用线性覆盖来定义基于首属性的通用算法复杂度。需注意，计算线性覆盖中节点/关系不相交路径的笛卡尔积，其复杂度形式与引理13相似，但线性覆盖中的每条路径可能并非线性联结，因此可能导致更高复杂度。我们的基于首属性通用算法与之截然不同。

LEMMA 13. For a twig join $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ with its join tree $\mathcal{T}$ rooted at $r$ ,an instance $\mathcal{R}$ and a parameter $L$ ,the join result $Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be computed using $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathcal{P}\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) }\right)$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load $O\left( L\right)$ ,where $\mathcal{P}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ is over all linear coverings of $\mathcal{T}$ , and

引理13。对于根在$r$的树状联结$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$及其联结树$\mathcal{T}$，实例$\mathcal{R}$和参数$L$，联结结果$Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次中用$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathcal{P}\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) }\right)$台服务器计算，负载为$O\left( L\right)$，其中$\mathcal{P}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$遍历$\mathcal{T}$的所有线性覆盖，且

$$
\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  = \left\{  {\{ S\}  : S \subseteq  \left( {{\mathcal{L}}_{1}-\{ r\} }\right)  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{2} \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }\times \{ r\} }\right\}  
$$

$$
 \cup  \left\{  {\{ S\}  : S \subseteq  \{ r\}  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{2} \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}  
$$

for $\mathcal{P}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  = \left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1},{\mathcal{L}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}$ .

对于$\mathcal{P}\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  = \left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1},{\mathcal{L}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}$。

EXAMPLE 8. Continue the example of twig join query in Figure 8. One of its linear coverings is $\left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1},{\mathcal{L}}_{2},{\mathcal{L}}_{3},{\mathcal{L}}_{4},{\mathcal{L}}_{5},{\mathcal{L}}_{6},{\mathcal{L}}_{7}}\right\}$ ,where ${\mathcal{L}}_{1} = \{ \left( {EFG}\right) ,\left( {CEF}\right) ,\left( {CDE}\right) ,\left( {BCD}\right) ,\left( {ABC}\right) \} ,{\mathcal{L}}_{2} = \{ \left( {FHJ}\right) ,\left( {IJX}\right) \} ,$ ${\mathcal{L}}_{3} = \{ \left( {FHZ}\right) ,\left( {WZ}\right) \} ,{\mathcal{L}}_{4} = \{ \left( {HY}\right) \} ,{\mathcal{L}}_{5} = \{ \left( {DU}\right) \} ,{\mathcal{L}}_{6} = \{ \left( {BK}\right) \}$ and ${\mathcal{L}}_{7} = \{ \left( {FM}\right) \}$ . By the definition of $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$ ,then $S = \{ \left( {ABC}\right) ,\left( {BK}\right)$ , $\left( {DU}\right) ,\left( {HY}\right) ,\left( {IJX}\right) ,\left( {WZ}\right) ,\left( {FM}\right) ,\left( {EFG}\right) \}$ belongs to $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$ ,but any superset of $S$ doesn’t belongs to $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$ .

示例8. 继续图8中的树枝连接查询示例。其线性覆盖之一是$\left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1},{\mathcal{L}}_{2},{\mathcal{L}}_{3},{\mathcal{L}}_{4},{\mathcal{L}}_{5},{\mathcal{L}}_{6},{\mathcal{L}}_{7}}\right\}$，其中${\mathcal{L}}_{1} = \{ \left( {EFG}\right) ,\left( {CEF}\right) ,\left( {CDE}\right) ,\left( {BCD}\right) ,\left( {ABC}\right) \} ,{\mathcal{L}}_{2} = \{ \left( {FHJ}\right) ,\left( {IJX}\right) \} ,$${\mathcal{L}}_{3} = \{ \left( {FHZ}\right) ,\left( {WZ}\right) \} ,{\mathcal{L}}_{4} = \{ \left( {HY}\right) \} ,{\mathcal{L}}_{5} = \{ \left( {DU}\right) \} ,{\mathcal{L}}_{6} = \{ \left( {BK}\right) \}$且${\mathcal{L}}_{7} = \{ \left( {FM}\right) \}$。根据$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$的定义，则$S = \{ \left( {ABC}\right) ,\left( {BK}\right)$、$\left( {DU}\right) ,\left( {HY}\right) ,\left( {IJX}\right) ,\left( {WZ}\right) ,\left( {FM}\right) ,\left( {EFG}\right) \}$属于$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$，但$S$的任何超集都不属于$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$。

Complexity of Acyclic Join. By reassembling a set of twigs into the original join tree, we obtain the complexity result in Theorem 14.

无环连接的复杂性。通过将一组树枝重组为原始连接树，我们得到定理14中的复杂性结果。

THEOREM 14. For an acyclic join $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ with join tree $\mathcal{T}$ , an instance $\mathcal{R}$ and a parameter $L$ ,the result $Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be computed using $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) }\right)$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load $O\left( L\right)$ ,where

定理14. 对于具有连接树$\mathcal{T}$的无环连接$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，实例$\mathcal{R}$和参数$L$，结果$Q\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次中使用$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) }\right)$台服务器计算，负载为$O\left( L\right)$，其中

$$
\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)  = \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{e \in  S}}\frac{\left| R\left( e\right) \right| }{L}
$$

and $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$ is recursively defined on $\mathcal{T}$ as follows:

且$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$在$\mathcal{T}$上递归定义如下：

(1): If there is a pair of nodes ${e}_{1},{e}_{0}$ such that ${e}_{1} \subseteq  {e}_{0}$ ,then $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  = \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  \cup  \left\{  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  \right\}$ . It should be noted that $\mathcal{T}$ is updated by removing ${e}_{1}$ from $\mathcal{T}$ and putting every child of ${e}_{1}$ as a new child of ${e}_{1}$ if ${e}_{1}$ is not a leaf in $\mathcal{T}$ .

(1): 若存在节点对${e}_{1},{e}_{0}$使得${e}_{1} \subseteq  {e}_{0}$，则$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  = \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  \cup  \left\{  \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  \right\}$。需注意当${e}_{1}$不是$\mathcal{T}$中的叶节点时，$\mathcal{T}$将通过从$\mathcal{T}$移除${e}_{1}$并将${e}_{1}$的每个子节点作为${e}_{1}$的新子节点来更新。

(2): $\mathcal{T}$ is decomposed into a set of twigs ${\mathcal{G}}_{1},{\mathcal{G}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{G}}_{\ell }$ as defined in Definition 7,

(2): $\mathcal{T}$被分解为定义7中定义的树枝集合${\mathcal{G}}_{1},{\mathcal{G}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{G}}_{\ell }$，

$$
\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  = \left\{  {\{ S\}  : S \subseteq  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{1}\right)  \times  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{2}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\ell }\right) }\right\}  
$$

where ${\mathcal{E}}_{i} = {\mathcal{G}}_{i} - {\mathcal{G}}_{j}$ if there exists $j > i$ such that ${\mathcal{G}}_{i} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{j} \neq  \varnothing$ and ${\mathcal{E}}_{i} = {\mathcal{G}}_{i}$ otherwise.

当存在$j > i$使得${\mathcal{G}}_{i} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{j} \neq  \varnothing$且${\mathcal{E}}_{i} = {\mathcal{G}}_{i}$时，${\mathcal{E}}_{i} = {\mathcal{G}}_{i} - {\mathcal{G}}_{j}$所在位置；否则。

(3): $\mathcal{T}$ is a twig join with a linear covering $\mathcal{P} = \left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1},{\mathcal{L}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}$ . If $\mathcal{T}$ includes the root $r$ ,

(3): $\mathcal{T}$是具有线性覆盖$\mathcal{P} = \left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1},{\mathcal{L}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}$的枝状连接。若$\mathcal{T}$包含根节点$r$，

$$
\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  = \left\{  {\{ S\}  : S \subseteq  {\mathcal{L}}_{1} \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{2} \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}  
$$

Otherwise,

否则，

$$
\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  = \left\{  {\{ S\}  : S \subseteq  \left( {{\mathcal{L}}_{1}-\{ r\} }\right)  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{2} \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }\times \{ r\} }\right\}  
$$

$$
 \cup  \left\{  {\{ S\}  : S \subseteq  \{ r\}  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{2} \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}  .
$$

EXAMPLE 9. With respect to rule (2) in Theorem 14, we use the example in Figure 7 for clarification. Assume the join tree is decomposed into 6 twigs following the ordering of $\left( {{\mathcal{G}}_{2},{\mathcal{G}}_{3},{\mathcal{G}}_{5},{\mathcal{G}}_{6},{\mathcal{G}}_{4},{\mathcal{G}}_{1}}\right)$ . Under rule (2),twigs ${\mathcal{G}}_{2},{\mathcal{G}}_{3},{\mathcal{G}}_{5},{\mathcal{G}}_{6},{\mathcal{G}}_{4}$ exclude their root and twig ${\mathcal{G}}_{1}$ includes its root in the computation,since ${\mathcal{G}}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{1} \neq  \varnothing ,{\mathcal{G}}_{3} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{1} \neq  \varnothing$ , ${\mathcal{G}}_{4} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{1} \neq  \varnothing ,{\mathcal{G}}_{5} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{4} \neq  \varnothing ,{\mathcal{G}}_{6} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{4} \neq  \varnothing .$

示例9. 针对定理14中的规则(2)，我们使用图7的示例进行说明。假设连接树按$\left( {{\mathcal{G}}_{2},{\mathcal{G}}_{3},{\mathcal{G}}_{5},{\mathcal{G}}_{6},{\mathcal{G}}_{4},{\mathcal{G}}_{1}}\right)$顺序分解为6个枝状结构。根据规则(2)，枝${\mathcal{G}}_{2},{\mathcal{G}}_{3},{\mathcal{G}}_{5},{\mathcal{G}}_{6},{\mathcal{G}}_{4}$在计算时排除其根节点，而枝${\mathcal{G}}_{1}$包含其根节点，因为${\mathcal{G}}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{1} \neq  \varnothing ,{\mathcal{G}}_{3} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{1} \neq  \varnothing$，${\mathcal{G}}_{4} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{1} \neq  \varnothing ,{\mathcal{G}}_{5} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{4} \neq  \varnothing ,{\mathcal{G}}_{6} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{4} \neq  \varnothing .$

The proof of Theorem 14, together with that of Lemma 11 and Lemma 13 are given in Appendix A.2.

定理14的证明，连同引理11和引理13的证明，均在附录A.2中给出。

### 4.3 Worst-case Optimality

### 4.3 最坏情况最优性

We run the generic algorithm following choices according to Section 4.1,but using a different value of $L$ defined as below:

我们按照第4.1节的选项运行通用算法，但使用如下定义的不同$L$值：

$$
L = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}{\left( \frac{\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{e \in  S}}\left| {R\left( e\right) }\right| }{p}\right) }^{\frac{1}{\left| S\right| }}.
$$

Since the join query has constant complexity, $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$ as well as the value of $L$ can be computed locally.

由于连接查询具有恒定复杂度，$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$以及$L$的值可在本地计算。

THEOREM 15. For an $\alpha$ -acyclic join $Q$ with a join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ ,the join result $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ can be computed using $p$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}{\left( \frac{\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{e \in  S}}\left| {R\left( e\right) }\right| }{p}\right) }^{\frac{1}{\left| S\right| }}}\right)$ .

定理15. 对于具有连接树$\mathcal{T}$的$\alpha$非循环连接$Q$及实例$\mathcal{R}$，连接结果$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$可在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次中使用$p$台服务器计算，负载为$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}{\left( \frac{\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{e \in  S}}\left| {R\left( e\right) }\right| }{p}\right) }^{\frac{1}{\left| S\right| }}}\right)$。

Moreover,when each relation has at most $N$ input tuples,such a complicated bound has a clean form as stated in Theorem 16. A more fine-grained analysis of the optimality of Theorem 15 in terms of arbitrary input sizes $N\left( e\right)$ ’s would be an interesting and challenging open question. We won't go into this direction further.

此外，当每个关系最多有$N$个输入元组时，如此复杂的边界可简化为定理16所述的简洁形式。针对任意输入规模$N\left( e\right)$对定理15最优性进行更细粒度分析，将是个有趣且具挑战性的开放问题。我们不再深入探讨此方向。

THEOREM 16. For an $\alpha$ -acyclic join $Q$ and an instance $\mathcal{R}$ where each relation contains at most $N$ tuples,there is an algorithm computing $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load $O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$ ,where ${\rho }^{ * }$ is the optimal fractional edge covering number of $Q$ ,which is worst-optimal.

定理16. 对于$\alpha$无环连接$Q$及每个关系最多包含$N$个元组的实例$\mathcal{R}$，存在一种算法可在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内以$O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\rho }^{ * }}}\right)$负载计算$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$，其中${\rho }^{ * }$为$Q$的最优分数边覆盖数，该结果是理论最优的。

Note that the optimality of Theorem 16 is shown on a hard instance implied by the AGM bound [5], where each relation contains $O\left( N\right)$ input tuples and the join size is as large as $\Theta \left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$ . Here we give a sketch proof of Theorem 16.

需注意定理16的最优性是通过AGM界[5]所隐含的困难实例证明的，其中每个关系包含$O\left( N\right)$个输入元组且连接结果集大小高达$\Theta \left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$。此处我们给出定理16的概要证明。

Proof of SKETCH. To prove $\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)  \leq  {\left( \frac{N}{L}\right) }^{{\rho }^{ * }}$ for any $S \in$ $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$ ,it suffices to show that $\left| S\right|  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }$ for any $\bar{S} \in  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$ . Recall that the join tree $\mathcal{T}$ is decomposed into a set of twigs as $\mathcal{G}$ . Let $\left( {{\mathcal{G}}_{1},{\mathcal{G}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{G}}_{\ell }}\right)$ be a valid ordering of twigs in $\mathcal{G}$ . Observe that we also obtain a partition of $S$ as ${S}_{1},{S}_{2},\cdots ,{S}_{\ell }$ such that (1) ${S}_{i} =$ $\left( {{\mathcal{G}}_{i} \cap  S}\right)  - {\mathcal{G}}_{j}$ if there exists $j > i$ such that ${\mathcal{G}}_{i} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{j} \neq  \varnothing ;\left( 2\right) {S}_{i} = {\mathcal{G}}_{i} \cap  S$ otherwise. It can be easily checked that $S = {S}_{1} \cup  {S}_{2} \cup  \cdots  \cup  {S}_{\ell }$ and ${S}_{i} \cap  {S}_{j} = \varnothing$ for any pair of $i \neq  j$ . Implied by the definition of $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$ , the number of relations in ${S}_{i}$ is at most the number of leaves in ${\mathcal{G}}_{i}$ minus 1,i.e., $\left| {S}_{i}\right|  \leq  \left| {{\mathcal{G}}_{i} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }}\right|  - 1$ . In this way,we can bound the number of relations in $S$ as

证明概要. 要证对任意$S \in$$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$的$\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right)  \leq  {\left( \frac{N}{L}\right) }^{{\rho }^{ * }}$，只需证明对任意$\bar{S} \in  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$的$\left| S\right|  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }$。连接树$\mathcal{T}$被分解为枝丛集$\mathcal{G}$，令$\left( {{\mathcal{G}}_{1},{\mathcal{G}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{G}}_{\ell }}\right)$为$\mathcal{G}$中枝丛的有效排序。可观察到$S$被划分为${S}_{1},{S}_{2},\cdots ,{S}_{\ell }$满足：(1)若存在$j > i$使得${\mathcal{G}}_{i} \cap  {\mathcal{G}}_{j} \neq  \varnothing ;\left( 2\right) {S}_{i} = {\mathcal{G}}_{i} \cap  S$则${S}_{i} =$$\left( {{\mathcal{G}}_{i} \cap  S}\right)  - {\mathcal{G}}_{j}$，否则。易验证对任意$i \neq  j$有$S = {S}_{1} \cup  {S}_{2} \cup  \cdots  \cup  {S}_{\ell }$和${S}_{i} \cap  {S}_{j} = \varnothing$。根据$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$定义，${S}_{i}$中关系数量最多为${\mathcal{G}}_{i}$的叶节点数减1，即$\left| {S}_{i}\right|  \leq  \left| {{\mathcal{G}}_{i} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }}\right|  - 1$。由此可限定$S$中关系数量为

$$
\left| S\right|  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{\ell }\left| {S}_{i}\right|  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{\ell }\left| {{\mathcal{G}}_{i} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }}\right|  - 1 = \left| {\mathcal{E}}_{\rho }\right|  = {\rho }^{ * },
$$

where the second last equality is implied by Definition 7.

其中倒数第二个等式由定义7隐含得出。

## 5 LOWER BOUNDS FOR CYCLIC JOINS

## 5 循环连接的下界

In this section,we first prove a lower bound $\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right)$ for the ${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$ query, as stated in Theorem 17, which is matched by the existing upper bound $O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right)$ [19],thus being optimal. We then identify the class of degree-two joins, as well as the edge-packing-provable conditions, such that the edge-packing-dependent lower bound $\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$ can be proved for any degree-two join,as long as it satisfies the edge-packing-provable conditions, as stated in Theorem 18.

本节首先针对${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$查询证明一个下界$\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right)$（如定理17所述），该下界与现有上界$O\left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right)$[19]相匹配，因此具有最优性。继而我们界定二度连接类及边可打包证明条件，使得对于任何满足边可打包证明条件的二度连接（如定理18所述），均可推导出依赖边打包的下界$\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$。

THEOREM 17. For any $N/{\log }^{6}N \geq  {p}^{6}$ ,there exists an instance $\mathcal{R}$ for ${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$ where each relation has $N$ input tuples such that any tuple-based algorithm computing ${\mathcal{Q}}_{ \boxminus  }\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds must incur a load of $\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right)$ ,which is optimal.

定理17. 对于任意$N/{\log }^{6}N \geq  {p}^{6}$，存在${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$的实例$\mathcal{R}$（其中每个关系包含$N$个输入元组），使得任何基于元组的算法在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{ \boxminus  }\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$时必然产生$\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/3}}\right)$的负载——该下界是最优的。

THEOREM 18. On any edge-packing-provable degree-two join $Q$ , for any $N/{\log }^{c}N \geq  {p}^{c}$ with some constant $c$ ,there exists an instance $\mathcal{R}$ for $Q$ where each relation has $N$ input tuples such that any tuple-based algorithm computing $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds must incur a load of $\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$ ,where ${\tau }^{ * }$ is the optimal fractional edge packing number.

定理18. 对于任意满足边可打包证明条件的二度连接$Q$，给定具有常数$c$的$N/{\log }^{c}N \geq  {p}^{c}$，存在$Q$的实例$\mathcal{R}$（其中每个关系包含$N$个输入元组），使得任何基于元组的算法在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内计算$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$时必然产生$\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$的负载，此处${\tau }^{ * }$为最优分数边打包数。

The high-level idea of our lower bound proof is to show that with positive probability,an instance $\mathcal{R}$ for $Q$ can be constructed with bounded $J\left( L\right)$ ,the maximum number of join results a server can produce,if it loads at most $L$ tuples from each relation. We again resort to the counting argument that each join result must be emitted by at least one server. Setting $p \cdot  J\left( L\right)  = \Omega \left( \left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right| \right)$ yields a lower bound on $L$ .

下界证明的核心思想是：以正概率构造$Q$的实例$\mathcal{R}$，在服务器从每个关系最多加载$L$个元组的条件下，其可生成的连接结果数上限$J\left( L\right)$是有界的。我们再次采用计数论证——每个连接结果必须由至少一个服务器输出。设定$p \cdot  J\left( L\right)  = \Omega \left( \left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right| \right)$即可推导出$L$的下界。

### 5.1 日-Join

### 5.1 日式连接

We now prove Theorem 17. Assume $N \geq  {p}^{3}$ . Note that $L \geq  N/p \geq$ ${N}^{2/3}$ in this case. Our hard instance $\mathcal{R}$ is constructed as follows. Hard Instance. Each one of the attributes $A,B,C$ has ${N}^{1/3}$ distinct values and each of the attributes $D,E,F$ has ${N}^{2/3}$ distinct values. Relations ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)$ and ${R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$ are Cartesian products,each with exactly $N$ tuples. Relation ${R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$ is constructed in a probabilistic way. For ${R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$ ,each combination $\left( {d,e,f}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( E\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( F\right)$ has a probability of $1/N$ to form a tuple in ${R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$ . In this way,relation ${R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$ have $N$ tuples in expectation. The join result of this instance can be represented as the Cartesian product of ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \times  {R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$ . So,this instance has input size as ${5N}$ and output size as ${N}^{2}$ in expectation. By the Chernoff bound, the probability that the input size and output size deviate from their expectation by more than a constant factor is at most $\exp \left( {-\Omega \left( N\right) }\right)$ .

现证明定理17。假设$N \geq  {p}^{3}$。注意在此情况下$L \geq  N/p \geq$${N}^{2/3}$。我们构造的困难实例$\mathcal{R}$如下：困难实例中，每个属性$A,B,C$具有${N}^{1/3}$个不同取值，每个属性$D,E,F$具有${N}^{2/3}$个不同取值。关系${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)$与${R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$为笛卡尔积，各含$N$个元组。关系${R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$采用概率化构造——每个组合$\left( {d,e,f}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( E\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( F\right)$以$1/N$概率在${R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$中形成元组，因此${R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$期望含$N$个元组。该实例连接结果可表示为${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \times  {R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$的笛卡尔积，故其输入规模期望为${5N}$，输出规模期望为${N}^{2}$。根据切尔诺夫界，输入输出规模偏离期望值超过常数倍的概率至多为$\exp \left( {-\Omega \left( N\right) }\right)$。

## Step 1: Make a reasonable restriction on loading tuples.

## 步骤1：对加载元组施加合理限制

To bound $J\left( L\right)$ ,we first argue that on any instance constructed as above,we can limit the choice of the $L$ tuples loaded from ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)$ and ${R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$ by any server in the form of ${L}_{A} \times  {L}_{B} \times  {L}_{C},{L}_{A} \times  {L}_{D},{L}_{B} \times  {L}_{D}$ ,and ${L}_{C} \times  {L}_{F}$ for ${L}_{A} \subseteq$ $\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right) ,{L}_{B} \subseteq  \operatorname{dom}\left( B\right) ,{L}_{C} \subseteq  \operatorname{dom}\left( C\right) ,{L}_{D} \subseteq  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right) ,{L}_{E} \subseteq  \operatorname{dom}\left( E\right)$ and ${L}_{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{dom}\left( F\right)$ ,i.e.,the algorithm should load tuples from ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)$ and ${R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$ in the form of Cartesian product. More precisely, we first prove this result for attribute $A$ as stated in Lemma 19. The similar argument can be applied for attributes $B$ and $C$ .

为界定$J\left( L\right)$，首先论证在上述构造的任意实例中，可将各服务器从${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)$和${R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$加载的$L$元组限制为${L}_{A} \times  {L}_{B} \times  {L}_{C},{L}_{A} \times  {L}_{D},{L}_{B} \times  {L}_{D}$形式，对${L}_{A} \subseteq$$\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right) ,{L}_{B} \subseteq  \operatorname{dom}\left( B\right) ,{L}_{C} \subseteq  \operatorname{dom}\left( C\right) ,{L}_{D} \subseteq  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right) ,{L}_{E} \subseteq  \operatorname{dom}\left( E\right)$和${L}_{F} \subseteq  \operatorname{dom}\left( F\right)$则限制为${L}_{C} \times  {L}_{F}$形式，即算法应以笛卡尔积形式加载${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)$和${R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$的元组。具体而言，我们首先针对引理19所述的属性$A$证明该结论，类似论证可推广至属性$B$和$C$。

LEMMA 19. Restricting loading tuples from ${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$ in a form of ${L}_{A} \times  {L}_{D}$ and those from ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$ in a form of ${L}_{A} \times  {L}_{BC}$ where ${L}_{BC}$ are the assignments over attributes $B,C$ ,will not make $J\left( L\right)$ smaller by more than a constant factor.

引理19. 以${L}_{A} \times  {L}_{D}$形式限制从${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$加载的元组，并以${L}_{A} \times  {L}_{BC}$形式限制从${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$加载的元组（其中${L}_{BC}$是对属性$B,C$的赋值），不会使$J\left( L\right)$缩小超过一个常数因子。

Proof. Suppose a server has loaded $L$ tuples from ${R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$ , ${R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)$ and ${R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$ . Then the server needs to decide which $L$ tuples from ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$ and ${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$ to load to maximize the number of join results produced. This is a combinatorial optimization problem that can be formulated as an integer program (IP). Introduce a variable ${x}_{abc}$ for each triple $\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( B\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( C\right)$ , ${y}_{ad}$ for each pair $\left( {a,d}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$ . Let ${I}_{def} = 1$ if tuple $\left( {d,e,f}\right)  \in  {R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$ is loaded by the server,and 0 otherwise. The similar definition applies for ${I}_{be}$ and ${I}_{cf}$ . Then $I{P}_{1}$ below defines this optimization problem,where $a$ always ranges over $\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right) ,b$ over $\operatorname{dom}\left( B\right) ,c$ over $\operatorname{dom}\left( C\right) ,d$ over $\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right) ,e$ over $\operatorname{dom}\left( E\right) ,f$ over $\operatorname{dom}\left( F\right)$ unless specified otherwise.

证明. 假设服务器已从${R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$、${R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)$和${R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$加载了$L$个元组。此时服务器需要决定从${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$和${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$加载哪些$L$个元组以最大化连接结果数。这是一个可表述为整数规划(IP)的组合优化问题。为每个三元组$\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( B\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( C\right)$引入变量${x}_{abc}$，为每个二元组$\left( {a,d}\right)  \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)  \times  \operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$引入${y}_{ad}$。当元组$\left( {d,e,f}\right)  \in  {R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$被服务器加载时令${I}_{def} = 1$=1，否则为0。${I}_{be}$和${I}_{cf}$采用类似定义。则下述$I{P}_{1}$定义了该优化问题，其中除非特别说明，$a$始终遍历$\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right) ,b$，$\operatorname{dom}\left( B\right) ,c$遍历$\operatorname{dom}\left( C\right) ,d$，$\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right) ,e$遍历$\operatorname{dom}\left( E\right) ,f$，$\operatorname{dom}\left( F\right)$遍历相应域。

$$
\left( {I{P}_{1}}\right) \;\max \;\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{a,b,c,d,e,f}}{I}_{def} \cdot  {I}_{be} \cdot  {I}_{cf} \cdot  {x}_{abc} \cdot  {y}_{ad}
$$

$$
\text{s.t.}\max \left\{  {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{abc}}{x}_{abc},\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{ad}}{y}_{ad}}\right\}   \leq  L
$$

$$
{I}_{def},{I}_{be},{I}_{cf},{x}_{abc},{y}_{ad} \in  \{ 0,1\} ,\forall a,b,c,d,e,f
$$

However, it seems very difficult to dig out any structural property of the optimal solution of $I{P}_{1}$ . Instead,we introduce a relaxed version of $I{P}_{1}$ ,shown as $I{P}_{3}$ below.

然而，要挖掘出$I{P}_{1}$最优解的任何结构性特征似乎非常困难。为此我们引入$I{P}_{1}$的松弛版本，如下所示$I{P}_{3}$。

$$
\left( {I{P}_{3}}\right) \max \mathop{\sum }\limits_{a}\Delta \left( {w}_{a}\right) 
$$

$$
\text{s.t.}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{a}{w}_{a} \leq  {2L}
$$

$$
{w}_{a} \in  \{ 1,2,\cdots ,L\} ,\forall a
$$

Note that $I{P}_{3}$ uses a function $\Delta \left( w\right)$ ,which denotes the optimal solution of $I{P}_{2}$ defined as below:

注意$I{P}_{3}$使用了函数$\Delta \left( w\right)$，该函数表示下述定义的$I{P}_{2}$的最优解：

$$
\left( {I{P}_{2}}\right) \;\max \;\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{b,c,d,e,f}}{I}_{def} \cdot  {I}_{be} \cdot  {I}_{cf} \cdot  {x}_{abc} \cdot  {y}_{ad}
$$

$$
\text{s.t.}\max \left\{  {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{bc}}{x}_{abc},\mathop{\sum }\limits_{d}{y}_{ad}}\right\}   \leq  w
$$

$$
{I}_{def},{I}_{be},{I}_{cf},{x}_{abc},{y}_{ad} \in  \{ 0,1\} ,\forall a,b,c,d,e,f
$$

$I{P}_{2}$ is parameterized by $w$ and $a$ ,which finds the maximum number of join results that can be formed by tuples loaded from ${R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$ , ${R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)$ and ${R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$ ,subject to the constraint that at most $w$ tuples containing value $a$ are loaded from ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$ and ${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$ .

$I{P}_{2}$由参数$w$和$a$确定，其求解在满足约束条件（从${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$和${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$加载的包含值$a$的元组不超过$w$个）时，从${R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$、${R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)$和${R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$加载的元组能形成的最大连接结果数。

Since all values in the domain of attribute $A$ are structurally equivalent,the optimal solution of $I{P}_{2}$ does not depend on the particular choice of $a$ ,which is why we write the optimal solution of $I{P}_{2}$ as $\Delta \left( w\right)$ . Also,it is obvious that $\Delta \left( \text{.}\right) {isanon} - {decreasing}$ function. Then, $I{P}_{3}$ tries to find the optimal allocation of the $L$ tuples to different values $a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$ so as to maximize the total number of join results formed. Let the optimal solutions of $I{P}_{1},I{P}_{3}$ be ${OP}{T}_{1},{OP}{T}_{3}$ ,respectively. Because $I{P}_{3}$ only restricts the server to load at most ${2L}$ tuples from ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$ and ${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$ in total, any feasible solution to $I{P}_{1}$ is also a feasible solution to $I{P}_{3}$ ,so ${OP}{T}_{1} \leq  {OP}{T}_{3}$ . Next we construct a feasible solution of $I{P}_{3}$ with the Cartesian product restriction above, and show that it is within a constant factor from ${OP}{T}_{3}$ ,hence ${OP}{T}_{1}$ .

由于属性$A$定义域中的所有值在结构上等价，$I{P}_{2}$的最优解并不依赖于$a$的具体选择，因此我们将$I{P}_{2}$的最优解记为$\Delta \left( w\right)$。显然，$\Delta \left( \text{.}\right) {isanon} - {decreasing}$具有函数性。接着，$I{P}_{3}$尝试寻找$L$元组到不同值$a \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$的最优分配方案，以最大化生成的连接结果总数。设$I{P}_{1},I{P}_{3}$的最优解分别为${OP}{T}_{1},{OP}{T}_{3}$。由于$I{P}_{3}$仅限制服务器从${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$和${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$加载的元组总数不超过${2L}$，任何对$I{P}_{1}$可行的解同样适用于$I{P}_{3}$，故${OP}{T}_{1} \leq  {OP}{T}_{3}$。接下来我们构造一个满足上述笛卡尔积限制的$I{P}_{3}$可行解，并证明其与${OP}{T}_{3}$相差常数倍，因此${OP}{T}_{1}$。

Regarding to the function $\Delta \left( \text{.}\right) ,{wedefine}$

关于函数$\Delta \left( \text{.}\right) ,{wedefine}$

$$
{w}^{ * } = \arg \mathop{\max }\limits_{{L/{N}^{1/3} \leq  w \leq  L}}\frac{L}{w} \cdot  \Delta \left( w\right) .
$$

We choose $\frac{L}{{w}^{ * }}$ distinct values arbitrarily from $\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$ and allocate ${w}^{ * }$ tuples to each value $a \in  A$ . For each $a$ ,we use the optimal solution of $I{P}_{2}$ to find the ${w}^{ * }$ tuples to load from ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$ and ${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$ . Note that the optimal solution is the same for every $a$ , so each $a$ will choose the same sets of(b,c)’s and $d$ ’s. Thus,this feasible solution loads tuples from ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$ and ${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$ in the form of Cartesian products. The number of join results that can be produced is $W = \frac{L}{{w}^{ * }} \cdot  \Delta \left( {w}^{ * }\right)$ . We show that $W$ is a constant-factor approximation of ${OP}{T}_{3}$ ,as stated in Lemma 20,thus completing the whole proof.

我们从$\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$中任意选取$\frac{L}{{w}^{ * }}$个不同值，并为每个值$a \in  A$分配${w}^{ * }$个元组。对于每个$a$，利用$I{P}_{2}$的最优解确定从${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$和${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$加载的${w}^{ * }$个元组。注意最优解对所有$a$相同，因此每个$a$将选择相同的(b,c)集合和$d$。此可行解以笛卡尔积形式从${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$和${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$加载元组，可生成的连接结果数为$W = \frac{L}{{w}^{ * }} \cdot  \Delta \left( {w}^{ * }\right)$。如引理20所述，$W$是${OP}{T}_{3}$的常数倍近似，从而完成整个证明。

Lemma 20. $W \geq  \frac{1}{3}{OP}{T}_{3} \geq  \frac{1}{3}{OP}{T}_{1}$ .

引理20.$W \geq  \frac{1}{3}{OP}{T}_{3} \geq  \frac{1}{3}{OP}{T}_{1}$

Proof. Suppose ${OP}{T}_{3}$ chooses a set of values ${A}^{ * } \subseteq  A$ ,and each $a \in  {A}^{ * }$ has ${w}_{a}$ tuples loaded from ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$ and ${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$ . A value $a \in  {A}^{ * }$ is efficient if $\frac{\Delta \left( {w}_{a}\right) }{{w}_{a}} \geq  \frac{\Delta \left( {w}^{ * }\right) }{{w}^{ * }}$ ,and inefficient otherwise. Let ${A}_{1}^{ * },{A}_{2}^{ * }$ be the set of efficient,inefficient values separately. Note that for every efficient value $a,{w}_{a} \leq  \frac{L}{{N}^{1/3}}$ by the definition of ${w}^{ * }$ .

证明。假设${OP}{T}_{3}$选择一组值${A}^{ * } \subseteq  A$，每个$a \in  {A}^{ * }$从${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$和${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$加载了${w}_{a}$个元组。若$\frac{\Delta \left( {w}_{a}\right) }{{w}_{a}} \geq  \frac{\Delta \left( {w}^{ * }\right) }{{w}^{ * }}$则称值$a \in  {A}^{ * }$高效，否则为低效。分别用${A}_{1}^{ * },{A}_{2}^{ * }$表示高效值与低效值集合。根据${w}^{ * }$定义，对每个高效值$a,{w}_{a} \leq  \frac{L}{{N}^{1/3}}$均有此性质。

We relate $W$ and ${OP}{T}_{3}$ by showing how to cover all the join results reported by ${OP}{T}_{3}$ with the feasible solution constructed above. First,we use $\frac{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{a \in  {A}_{2}^{ * }}}{w}_{a}}{3{w}^{ * }}$ values of $A$ each with ${w}^{ * }$ tuples from ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$ and ${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$ to cover the join results reported by ${A}_{2}^{ * }$ . The total number of tuples needed is at most $\frac{2}{3}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{a \in  {A}_{2}^{ * }}}{w}_{a} \leq  \frac{4}{3}L$ . The number of join results that can be reported is

我们通过展示如何用上述构建的可行解覆盖${OP}{T}_{3}$报告的所有连接结果，将$W$与${OP}{T}_{3}$关联起来。首先，使用$A$的$\frac{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{a \in  {A}_{2}^{ * }}}{w}_{a}}{3{w}^{ * }}$个值（每个值对应${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$和${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$中的${w}^{ * }$个元组）来覆盖${A}_{2}^{ * }$报告的连接结果。所需元组总数最多为$\frac{2}{3}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{a \in  {A}_{2}^{ * }}}{w}_{a} \leq  \frac{4}{3}L$，可报告的连接结果数量为

$$
\frac{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{a \in  {A}_{2}^{ * }}}{w}_{a}}{3{w}^{ * }} \cdot  \Delta \left( {w}^{ * }\right)  \geq  \frac{1}{3}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{a \in  {A}_{2}^{ * }}}{w}_{a} \cdot  \frac{\Delta \left( {w}_{a}\right) }{{w}_{a}} = \frac{1}{3}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{a \in  {A}_{2}^{ * }}}\Delta \left( {w}_{a}\right) .
$$

Next,we use $\frac{L}{3{w}^{ * }}$ values each with ${w}^{ * }$ tuples from ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$ and ${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$ to cover the join results reported by ${A}_{1}^{ * }$ . The total number of tuples needed is $\frac{2}{3}L$ . Recall that ${w}_{a} \leq  \frac{\widehat{L}}{{N}^{1/3}}$ for each $a \in  {A}_{1}^{ * }$ . The number of join results that can be reported is

接着，使用${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$和${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$中的${w}^{ * }$个元组（每个对应$\frac{L}{3{w}^{ * }}$个值）来覆盖${A}_{1}^{ * }$报告的连接结果。所需元组总数为$\frac{2}{3}L$。注意对于每个$a \in  {A}_{1}^{ * }$都有${w}_{a} \leq  \frac{\widehat{L}}{{N}^{1/3}}$。可报告的连接结果数量为

$$
\frac{L}{3{w}^{ * }} \cdot  \Delta \left( {w}^{ * }\right)  \geq  \frac{L}{3} \cdot  \frac{\Delta \left( \frac{L}{{N}^{1/3}}\right) }{\frac{L}{{N}^{1/3}}} = \frac{{N}^{1/3}}{3} \cdot  \Delta \left( \frac{L}{{N}^{1/3}}\right)  \geq  \frac{1}{3}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{a \in  {A}_{1}^{ * }}}\Delta \left( {w}_{a}\right) ,
$$

where the rationale behinds the last inequality is that there are at most ${N}^{1/3}$ values in ${A}_{1}^{ * }$ and there is $\Delta \left( \frac{L}{{N}^{1/3}}\right)  \geq  \Delta \left( {w}_{a}\right)$ for each $a \in  {A}_{1}^{ * }$ by the non-decreasing property of $\Delta \left( \text{.}\right) .$

最后一个不等式成立的原因是：${A}_{1}^{ * }$中最多有${N}^{1/3}$个值，且根据$\Delta \left( \text{.}\right) .$的非递减性质，每个$a \in  {A}_{1}^{ * }$对应$\Delta \left( \frac{L}{{N}^{1/3}}\right)  \geq  \Delta \left( {w}_{a}\right)$

Combining the two parts for the optimal solution ${A}^{ * }$ ,our alternative solution loads at most ${2L}$ tuples from ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$ and ${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$ , and can report at least $\frac{1}{3} \cdot  {OP}{T}_{3}$ join results.

将最优解${A}^{ * }$的两部分结合后，我们的替代方案最多从${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$和${R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right)$加载${2L}$个元组，且至少能报告$\frac{1}{3} \cdot  {OP}{T}_{3}$个连接结果。

Note that Lemma 19 implies that ${L}_{AB} = {L}_{A} \times  {L}_{B}$ and ${L}_{AC} =$ ${L}_{A} \times  {L}_{C}$ in relation ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$ . Applying a similar argument to attribute $B$ ,we get ${L}_{BC} = {L}_{B} \times  {L}_{C}$ . Together,we come to ${L}_{ABC} =$ ${L}_{A} \times  {L}_{B} \times  {L}_{C}$ ,i.e.,tuples in relation ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$ should be loaded in form of Cartesian product over all attributes.

注意引理19表明：关系${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$中的${L}_{AB} = {L}_{A} \times  {L}_{B}$与${L}_{AC} =$${L}_{A} \times  {L}_{C}$。将此论证类似应用于属性$B$，可得${L}_{BC} = {L}_{B} \times  {L}_{C}$。综合可得${L}_{ABC} =$${L}_{A} \times  {L}_{B} \times  {L}_{C}$，即关系${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)$中的元组应以所有属性的笛卡尔积形式加载。

## Step 2: Prove a upper bound on $J\left( L\right)$ .

## 步骤2：证明$J\left( L\right)$的上界

Next, we show that with positive probability (actually high probability),we obtain an instance on which $J\left( L\right)$ is bounded,no matter which $L$ tuples are loaded. By the analysis above,we only need to consider the case where tuples from ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)$ , ${R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$ are loaded in the form of Cartesian products. Denote the number of distinct values in $\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right) ,\operatorname{dom}\left( B\right)$ loaded by the server as $\alpha ,\beta$ respectively. The number of distinct values in $\operatorname{dom}\left( C\right) ,\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$ , $\operatorname{dom}\left( E\right) ,\operatorname{dom}\left( F\right)$ loaded by the server are $\frac{L}{\alpha \beta },\frac{L}{\alpha },\frac{L}{\beta },{\alpha \beta }$ . Moreover, $\frac{L}{{N}^{2/3}} \leq  \alpha ,\beta  \leq  {N}^{1/3}$ .

接下来我们证明，无论加载哪些$L$元组，以正概率（实际上是高概率）获得的实例中$J\left( L\right)$是有界的。根据上述分析，只需考虑从${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {A,D}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {B,E}\right)$、${R}_{5}\left( {C,F}\right)$以笛卡尔积形式加载元组的情况。设服务器加载的$\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right) ,\operatorname{dom}\left( B\right)$中不同值数量分别为$\alpha ,\beta$，而$\operatorname{dom}\left( C\right) ,\operatorname{dom}\left( D\right)$、$\operatorname{dom}\left( E\right) ,\operatorname{dom}\left( F\right)$中不同值数量为$\frac{L}{\alpha \beta },\frac{L}{\alpha },\frac{L}{\beta },{\alpha \beta }$。此外，$\frac{L}{{N}^{2/3}} \leq  \alpha ,\beta  \leq  {N}^{1/3}$。

There are ${L}^{3}$ combinations in terms of(a,b,c,d,e,f)in total. Each one is a valid join result if and only if $\left( {d,e,f}\right)  \in  {R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$ , which happens with probability $\frac{1}{N}$ . By the linearity of expectation, the expected number of join results can be produced by the $L$ tuples is $\frac{{L}^{3}}{N}$ . More careful inspection reveals that the ${L}^{3}$ combinations are not independent; instead we can divide them into ${L}^{2}$ independent groups where each one is associated to one distinct triple(d,e,f). Implied by the Chernoff bound, the probability that this server produces more than $2 \cdot  \frac{{L}^{3}}{N}$ join results is at most $\exp \left( {-\Omega \left( \frac{{L}^{2}}{N}\right) }\right)$ .

关于(a,b,c,d,e,f)的组合共有${L}^{3}$种。当且仅当$\left( {d,e,f}\right)  \in  {R}_{2}\left( {D,E,F}\right)$时（该事件发生概率为$\frac{1}{N}$），每个组合才是有效的连接结果。根据期望线性性质，$L$元组能产生的期望连接结果数为$\frac{{L}^{3}}{N}$。更细致分析表明，这${L}^{3}$种组合并非独立，可将其划分为${L}^{2}$个独立组，每组对应一个独特的三元组(d,e,f)。根据切尔诺夫界，该服务器产生超过$2 \cdot  \frac{{L}^{3}}{N}$个连接结果的概率至多为$\exp \left( {-\Omega \left( \frac{{L}^{2}}{N}\right) }\right)$。

For $A$ ,there are $\left( \begin{matrix} {N}^{1/3} \\  \alpha  \end{matrix}\right)  = O\left( {N}^{\frac{\alpha }{3}}\right)$ choices of loading $\alpha$ distinct values from $\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$ . Similar argument can be applied to $B,C,D,E,F$ . Over all values of $\alpha ,\beta$ ,the number of choices in total is

对于$A$，从$\operatorname{dom}\left( A\right)$加载$\alpha$个不同值有$\left( \begin{matrix} {N}^{1/3} \\  \alpha  \end{matrix}\right)  = O\left( {N}^{\frac{\alpha }{3}}\right)$种选择方案。$B,C,D,E,F$同理可证。综合$\alpha ,\beta$所有取值，总选择方案数为

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\alpha  = \frac{L}{{N}^{2/3}}}}^{{N}^{1/3}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\beta  = \frac{L}{{N}^{2/3}}}}^{{N}^{1/3}}{N}^{\frac{1}{3} \cdot  \left( {\alpha  + \beta  + \frac{L}{\alpha \beta }}\right) } \cdot  {N}^{\frac{2}{3} \cdot  \left( {\frac{L}{\alpha } + \frac{L}{\beta } + {\alpha \beta }}\right) }
$$

$$
 = \exp \left( {\widetilde{O}\left( {{\alpha \beta } + \frac{L}{\alpha } + \frac{L}{\beta }}\right) }\right)  = \exp \left( {\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right) }\right) 
$$

By the union bound, the probability that any of the choice produces more than $\frac{2{L}^{3}}{N}$ join results is at most

根据联合界定理，任一选择方案产生超过$\frac{2{L}^{3}}{N}$个连接结果的概率至多为

$$
\exp \left( {-\Omega \left( \frac{{L}^{2}}{N}\right)  + \widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right) }\right) ,
$$

which is exponentially small if

该概率在以下条件下呈指数级衰减：

$$
\frac{{L}^{2}}{N} \geq  {c}_{1} \cdot  {N}^{\frac{2}{3}} \cdot  \log N
$$

for some sufficiently large constant ${c}_{1}$ . Rearranging,we get

当常数${c}_{1}$足够大时，整理可得

$$
{L}^{2} \geq  {c}_{1} \cdot  {N}^{\frac{5}{3}} \cdot  \log N
$$

We know that $L = \Omega \left( \frac{N}{p}\right)$ ,so this is true as long as

已知$L = \Omega \left( \frac{N}{p}\right)$，因此只要满足

$$
{\left( \frac{N}{p}\right) }^{2} \geq  {c}_{2} \cdot  {N}^{\frac{5}{3}} \cdot  \log N
$$

for some sufficiently large constant ${c}_{2}$ ,or $N/{\log }^{6}N \geq  {c}_{2} \cdot  {p}^{6}$ .

对于足够大的常数${c}_{2}$（或$N/{\log }^{6}N \geq  {c}_{2} \cdot  {p}^{6}$）即成立

## Step 3: Apply counting argument.

## 第三步：应用计数论证

So far, we have shown that with exponentially high probability each server produces no more than $2 \cdot  \frac{{L}^{3}}{N}$ join results in each round. Over $p$ servers,the total number of join results that can be produced in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds is $O\left( \frac{{L}^{3}}{N}\right)$ . Each of the ${N}^{2}$ join results must be emitted at least once,so we will have $p \cdot  \frac{{L}^{3}}{N} \geq  {N}^{2}$ ,i.e., $L \geq  N/{p}^{1/3}$ .

至此我们已证明：在指数级高概率下，每台服务器每轮产生的连接结果不超过$2 \cdot  \frac{{L}^{3}}{N}$个。$p$台服务器在$O\left( 1\right)$轮中可产生的连接结果总数是$O\left( \frac{{L}^{3}}{N}\right)$。由于${N}^{2}$个连接结果每个至少需输出一次，故有$p \cdot  \frac{{L}^{3}}{N} \geq  {N}^{2}$，即$L \geq  N/{p}^{1/3}$。

We have completed the whole proof for Theorem 17.

我们已完成定理17的全部证明。

### 5.2 Degree-two Joins

### 5.2 二阶连接

Our lower bound proof for the $\boxminus$ -join can be extended to a larger class of join queries, named as degree-two join, where every vertex appears in exactly two edges. As mentioned, degree-two joins enjoy several nice properties, as stated in Lemma 21. The proof of Lemma 21 is given in Appendix A.4.

我们对$\boxminus$-连接的下界证明可推广至一类更广泛的连接查询，称为二阶连接，其中每个顶点恰好出现在两条边中。如前所述，二阶连接具有若干优良特性，如引理21所述。引理21的证明详见附录A.4。

LEMMA 21. For any reduced degree-two join $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,the following holds: (1) ${\tau }^{ * } \geq  \frac{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }{2} \geq  {\rho }^{ * };\left( 2\right) {\tau }^{ * } + {\rho }^{ * } = \left| \mathcal{E}\right|$ ; (3) The optimal fractional edge packing/covering admits half-integral solution; (4) if there exists no odd-length cycle ${}^{14}$ , the optimal fractional edge packing/covering admits an integral solution.

引理21. 对于任何化简的二阶连接$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，以下性质成立：(1)${\tau }^{ * } \geq  \frac{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }{2} \geq  {\rho }^{ * };\left( 2\right) {\tau }^{ * } + {\rho }^{ * } = \left| \mathcal{E}\right|$；(3)最优分数边填充/覆盖允许半整数解；(4)若不存在奇数长度环${}^{14}$，则最优分数边填充/覆盖允许整数解。

However, not all degree-two joins fit for the lower bound framework, two additional conditions are captured in Definition 22. Before describing the conditions, we introduce some notions first. In a hypergraph $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,let $\Gamma \left( e\right)$ be the set of neighbors of edge $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ ,i.e., $\Gamma \left( e\right)  = \left\{  {{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E} : e \cap  {e}^{\prime } \neq  \varnothing }\right\}$ . A fractional vertex covering for $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is a mapping $x$ from $\mathcal{V}\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ such that $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  \mathcal{V} : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} \geq  1$ holds for each edge $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ ; and the optimal solution is to minimize the quantity $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  \mathcal{V}}}{x}_{v}$ . In addition,a vertex covering $x$ is constant-small if $\mathop{\max }\limits_{v}{x}_{v} \leq  1 - \epsilon$ for some constant $0 < \epsilon  < 1$ .

然而并非所有二阶连接都适用于下界框架，定义22中捕获了两个附加条件。在描述条件前，我们首先引入若干概念。在超图$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$中，令$\Gamma \left( e\right)$表示边$e \in  \mathcal{E}$的邻集，即$\Gamma \left( e\right)  = \left\{  {{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E} : e \cap  {e}^{\prime } \neq  \varnothing }\right\}$。$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$的分数顶点覆盖是一个映射$x$，满足对每条边$e \in  \mathcal{E}$有$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  \mathcal{V} : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} \geq  1$；最优解是最小化量$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  \mathcal{V}}}{x}_{v}$。此外，若存在常数$0 < \epsilon  < 1$使得$\mathop{\max }\limits_{v}{x}_{v} \leq  1 - \epsilon$，则称顶点覆盖$x$为常量小。

Definition 22 (Edge-packing-provable Degree Two Join). A degree-two join $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is edge-packing-provable if (1) it is reduced; (2) there is no odd-length cycle; (3) there exists an optimal fractional constant-small vertex covering $x$ ,such that $\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}\right|  \leq  1$ for every $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ ,where ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} > 1}\right\}$ .

定义22（可边填充证明的二阶连接）。一个二阶连接$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$若满足：(1)已化简；(2)无奇数长度环；(3)存在最优分数常量小顶点覆盖$x$，使得对每个$e \in  \mathcal{E}$有$\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}\right|  \leq  1$（其中${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} > 1}\right\}$），则称其为可边填充证明的。

Note that ${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$ is an edge-packing-provable degree-two join. Obviously, ${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$ is reduced,there is no odd-length cycle in ${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$ ,and a valid vertex covering $x$ with ${x}_{A} = {x}_{B} = {x}_{C} = \frac{1}{3}$ and ${x}_{D} = {x}_{E} = {x}_{F} = \frac{2}{3}$ is constant-small, which is also used in the lower bound proof of Theorem 17. Some other examples of edge-packing-provable degree-two joins are given in Figure 9.

注意${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$即为可边填充证明的二阶连接。显然${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$是化简的，其中不存在奇数长度环，且具有${x}_{A} = {x}_{B} = {x}_{C} = \frac{1}{3}$和${x}_{D} = {x}_{E} = {x}_{F} = \frac{2}{3}$的有效顶点覆盖$x$为常量小——该性质亦用于定理17的下界证明。图9给出了其他可边填充证明的二阶连接实例。

${}^{14}$ A cycle(V,E)is defined as $\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},\cdots ,{v}_{n}}\right\}   \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ and $\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{i} = }\right.$ $\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{\left( {i + 1}\right) {\;\operatorname{mod}\;n}}}\right\}   : i \in  \{ 1,2, \cdot  ,n\}$ . The length of a cycle(V,E)is defined as $\left| \mathcal{E}\right|$ .

${}^{14}$ 环(V,E)定义为$\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2},\cdots ,{v}_{n}}\right\}   \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$和$\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{i} = }\right.$$\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{\left( {i + 1}\right) {\;\operatorname{mod}\;n}}}\right\}   : i \in  \{ 1,2, \cdot  ,n\}$。环(V,E)的长度定义为$\left| \mathcal{E}\right|$。

The detailed proof of Theorem 18 is deferred to Appendix A.5. Here, we only give some intuition why the three conditions can be put together for generalizing this framework to degree-two joins.

定理18的详细证明见附录A.5。此处仅直观说明为何这三个条件可组合用于将该框架推广至二度连接。

In Definition 22,if there is no odd-length cycle in $Q$ ,it admits integral optimal edge packing ${\tau }^{ * }$ and covering ${\rho }^{ * }$ ,implied by Lemma 21. More specifically,there exists a partition $\left( {{\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha },{\mathcal{E}}_{\beta }}\right)$ of $\mathcal{E} : {\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha } = \{ e \in$ $\mathcal{E} : {\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1,{\tau }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0\}$ and ${\mathcal{E}}_{\beta } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : {\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0,{\tau }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1}\right\}$ , for example, ${\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha } = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}$ and ${\mathcal{E}}_{\beta } = \left\{  {{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}$ in ${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$ . Moreover, all edges in ${\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha }$ are vertex-disjoint,as well as edges in ${\mathcal{E}}_{\beta }$ ,due to the fact that each vertex appears in at most two edges.

在定义22中，若$Q$中不存在奇数长度环，则由引理21可知其允许整数最优边打包${\tau }^{ * }$和覆盖${\rho }^{ * }$。具体而言，存在$\mathcal{E} : {\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha } = \{ e \in$$\mathcal{E} : {\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1,{\tau }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0\}$与${\mathcal{E}}_{\beta } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : {\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0,{\tau }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1}\right\}$的划分$\left( {{\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha },{\mathcal{E}}_{\beta }}\right)$，例如${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$中的${\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha } = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}$和${\mathcal{E}}_{\beta } = \left\{  {{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}$。此外，由于每个顶点最多出现在两条边中，${\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha }$的所有边及${\mathcal{E}}_{\beta }$的边均为顶点不相交。

Consider any optimal fractional vertex covering $x$ satisfying (2) in Definition 22. Note that $x$ defines a partition $\left( {{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime \prime }}\right)$ of $\mathcal{E}$ :

考虑定义22中满足条件(2)的最优分数顶点覆盖$x$。注意$x$定义了$\mathcal{E}$的划分$\left( {{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime \prime }}\right)$：

$$
{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  \mathcal{V} : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} > 1}\right\}  ,
$$

$$
{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime \prime } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  \mathcal{V} : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} = 1}\right\}  
$$

for example, ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}$ and ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime \prime } = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}$ in ${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$ . Note that the fractional edge packing and vertex covering are prime-dual. Implied by the slackness theorem, ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha }$ . Edges in ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ are also vertex-disjoint. The hard instance is constructed by $x$ . More specifically,the domain of each attribute $v$ contains ${N}^{{x}_{v}}$ distinct values. Relations in ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime \prime }$ are deterministically constructed as Cartesian products,containing $N$ tuples exactly,while those in ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ are probabilistically constructed. As $\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}\right|  \leq  1$ holds for every $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ ,each edge in $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ derives a connected components $C\left( e\right)  = \left\{  {{e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime \prime } : {e}^{\prime } \cap  e \neq  \varnothing }\right\}$ . More importantly, $C\left( {e}_{1}\right)  \cap  C\left( {e}_{2}\right)  = \varnothing$ for any pair of ${e}_{1},{e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ . We then apply a similar argument for ${Q}_{\text{日 }}$ to each such component.

例如，${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}$与${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime \prime } = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}$在${Q}_{ \boxminus  }$中。需注意分数边填充与顶点覆盖是原始对偶关系。根据松弛定理可推知，${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha }$。${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$中的边也是顶点不相交的。该困难实例由$x$构建。具体而言，每个属性$v$的定义域包含${N}^{{x}_{v}}$个不同取值。${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime \prime }$中的关系通过笛卡尔积确定性构造，精确包含$N$个元组，而${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$中的关系则采用概率构造。由于对每个$e \in  \mathcal{E}$都有$\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}\right|  \leq  1$成立，$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$中的每条边都导出一个连通分量$C\left( e\right)  = \left\{  {{e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime \prime } : {e}^{\prime } \cap  e \neq  \varnothing }\right\}$。更重要的是，对任意${e}_{1},{e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$对都有$C\left( {e}_{1}\right)  \cap  C\left( {e}_{2}\right)  = \varnothing$成立。随后我们对每个此类分量应用与${Q}_{\text{日 }}$类似的论证方法。

At last, the notion of "constant-small" is used to prove a upper bound on $J\left( L\right)$ with exponentially high probability; and more details can be found in Appendix A.5.

最后，"常数小"概念被用于证明$J\left( L\right)$在指数级高概率下的上界；更多细节详见附录A.5。

Remark. We only give a sufficient condition in Theorem 18. Several questions remain to be answered, for example, (1) what is a complete characterization of cyclic queries on which our framework can be applied? and (2) is there any matching upper bound on the degree-two joins? It is still unclear whether ${\psi }^{ * } = {\tau }^{ * }$ holds for a degree-two join satisfying the edge-packing-provable conditions. If this is the case,then the lower bound $\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$ will be matched by the existing one-round algorithm [19].

注记。定理18仅给出充分条件。若干问题仍有待解答，例如：(1)我们的框架可应用的循环查询的完整特征是什么？(2)对于二阶连接是否存在匹配的上界？目前尚不清楚满足边填充可证条件的二阶连接是否满足${\psi }^{ * } = {\tau }^{ * }$。若成立，则现有单轮算法[19]将达到下界$\Omega \left( \frac{N}{{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$。
