## A OMITTED PROOFS

## 附录A 省略的证明

### A.1 Acyclic joins

### A.1 无环连接

An equivalent definition for $\alpha$ -acyclicity is based on the GYO reduction [1]: (1) if there is a vertex $v \in  \mathcal{V}$ only appearing in edge $e$ , then remove $v$ from $e$ ; (2) if there is a pair of edges $e,{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ such that $e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ ,then remove $e$ from $\mathcal{E}$ . A join query $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is $\alpha$ -acyclic if the GYO reduction results in an empty hypergraph.

关于$\alpha$-无环性的等价定义基于GYO约简[1]：(1)若存在顶点$v \in  \mathcal{V}$仅出现在边$e$中，则从$e$移除$v$；(2)若存在边对$e,{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$满足$e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$，则从$\mathcal{E}$移除$e$。当GYO约简得到空超图时，连接查询$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$即为$\alpha$-无环。

A join tree can be built by the GYO reduction recursively. If some unique attribute is removed from $e$ ,we add $e$ as a leaf node if it does not exist. If $e$ is removed by (2),we put $e$ as a child of ${e}^{\prime }$ . We show some nice properties for acyclic join in Lemma A.1, A.2, and A.3. For short,we use "acyclic" to denote " $\alpha$ -acyclic" below.

可通过递归GYO约简构建连接树。若从$e$移除了某些唯一属性，则当$e$不存在时将其添加为叶节点。若$e$通过条件(2)被移除，则将其作为${e}^{\prime }$的子节点。我们在引理A.1、A.2和A.3中展示了无环连接的若干优良性质。下文简称"无环"即指"$\alpha$-无环"。

LEMMA A.1. For any acyclic join $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ and an attribute $\mathbf{x} \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,the residual join ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{x}} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{x}},{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)$ is also acyclic.

引理A.1：对于任何无环连接$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$及属性$\mathbf{x} \in  \mathcal{V}$，其残余连接${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{x}} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{\mathbf{x}},{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)$同样保持无环性。

Proof. Recall that ${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$ is the residual query by removing attribute $\mathbf{x}$ from all relations in $\mathcal{E}$ . Let $\mathcal{T}$ be the join tree of $\mathcal{Q}$ ,such that (1) there is a one-to-one correspondence between edges in $\mathcal{E}$ and nodes in $\mathcal{T}$ ; (2) for any attribute $\mathbf{x} \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,all nodes containing $\mathbf{x}$ form a connected subtree. We derive another tree ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ by removing attributes $\mathbf{x}$ from each node in $\mathcal{T}$ . It can be easily checked that ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ is a valid join tree for ${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$ ,thus ${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$ is acyclic.

证明：回顾${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$是通过从$\mathcal{E}$的所有关系中移除属性$\mathbf{x}$得到的残余查询。设$\mathcal{T}$为$\mathcal{Q}$的连接树，满足：(1) $\mathcal{E}$的边与$\mathcal{T}$的节点间存在一一对应关系；(2) 对于任意属性$\mathbf{x} \in  \mathcal{V}$，包含$\mathbf{x}$的所有节点构成连通子树。通过从$\mathcal{T}$各节点移除$\mathbf{x}$得到新树${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$。易证${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$构成${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$的有效连接树，故${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$具有无环性。

LEMMA A.2. Acyclic join has integral optimal edge covering.

引理A.2：无环连接具有整数最优边覆盖。

Proof. Let ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ be the optimal edge covering for hypergraph $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ . An edge cover of $\mathcal{Q}$ can be obtained along with its GYO reduction. More specifically,if $Q$ is emptyset,we set ${\rho }^{ * } = 0$ . In general, we apply the following two procedures: (1) If attribute $\mathbf{x} \in  \mathcal{V}$ only appears in $e$ ,then assign $e$ with weight 1 and remove all attributes in $e$ from $\mathcal{V}$ ; (2) if $e,{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ are distinct edges such that $e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ ,then assign $e$ with weight 0 and remove $e$ from $\mathcal{E}$ .

证明：设${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$为超图$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$的最优边覆盖。边覆盖$\mathcal{Q}$可通过GYO约简获得。具体而言：若$Q$为空集，则设${\rho }^{ * } = 0$。常规情况下执行以下操作：(1) 若属性$\mathbf{x} \in  \mathcal{V}$仅出现在$e$中，则赋予$e$权重1并从$\mathcal{V}$移除$e$所有属性；(2) 若$e,{e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$为满足$e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$的相异边，则赋予$e$权重0并从$\mathcal{E}$移除$e$。

Next we will prove its optimality. The base case is trivial. In general, we prove it for these two cases separately.

接下来证明其最优性。基本情况显然成立。常规情形下，我们分别针对两种情况论证。

If $\mathcal{Q}$ is reduced through (1),let ${\mathcal{Q}}_{e} = \left( {\mathcal{V} - e,{\mathcal{E}}_{e}}\right)$ be the residual join by removing attributes in $e$ from all relations in $\mathcal{E}$ . By hypothesis,let ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{e}\right)$ be the integral optimal edge covering of ${\mathcal{Q}}_{e}$ . Note that ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{e}\right)  + 1$ ,which is optimal since any edge cover require to assign ${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1$ to cover attribute $v$ . Moreover, ${\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right)$ also admits integral optimal edge covering,since ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{e}\right)$ admits integral optimal edge covering.

若$\mathcal{Q}$通过(1)约简，设${\mathcal{Q}}_{e} = \left( {\mathcal{V} - e,{\mathcal{E}}_{e}}\right)$为从$\mathcal{E}$所有关系中移除$e$属性后的残余连接。根据假设，令${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{e}\right)$为${\mathcal{Q}}_{e}$的整数最优边覆盖。注意到${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{e}\right)  + 1$（其最优性成立，因任何边覆盖都需分配${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1$来覆盖属性$v$）。此外，${\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right)$同样存在整数最优边覆盖，这是由于${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{e}\right)$具有该性质。

If $\mathcal{Q}$ is reduced through step (2),let ${Q}^{\prime } = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\right)$ be the residual join by removing edge $e$ . Obviously, ${Q}^{\prime }$ is also acyclic. By hypothesis,let ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{e}\right)$ be the integral optimal edge covering of ${\mathcal{Q}}_{e}$ . Note that ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$ ,which is optimal since we can always shift any weight assigned to $e$ to ${e}^{\prime }$ while maintaining its optimality. Thus, ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ is an optimal integral edge covering for $\mathcal{Q}$ .

若$\mathcal{Q}$通过步骤(2)被约简，令${Q}^{\prime } = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\right)$为移除边$e$后的剩余连接。显然${Q}^{\prime }$也是无环的。根据假设，令${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{e}\right)$为${\mathcal{Q}}_{e}$的整数最优边覆盖。注意到${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$始终保持最优性——因我们总可将分配给$e$的权重转移至${e}^{\prime }$。因此${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$构成$\mathcal{Q}$的最优整数边覆盖。

We next introduce the berge-acyclic joins. For a join query $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,consider the bipartite graph $G$ ,in which $\mathcal{V}$ corresponds to vertices on one side and $\mathcal{E}$ to vertices on the other side. There is an edge between $v \in  \mathcal{V}$ and $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ if $v \in  e$ . Then the hypergraph(V,E)is berge-acyclic if this bipartite graph is acyclic. This notion of acyclicity preserves many natural properties in ordinary acyclic graphs. For example, there is only one path between any two vertices $u,v \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,and any subgraph of(V,E)is still acyclic. Note that this definition of berge-acyclicity does not allow two relations to have two or more common attributes. But if these attributes always appear together in any relation, then they can be simply considered as one "combined" attribute. In a berge-acyclic join $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,a relation $e$ is called a leaf if it contains at least one unique attribute and exactly one join attribute, and non-leaf otherwise.

接下来引入Berge-无环连接(Berge-acyclic joins)。对于连接查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，考虑二分图$G$：其中$\mathcal{V}$对应一侧顶点，$\mathcal{E}$对应另一侧顶点。当$v \in  e$时，$v \in  \mathcal{V}$与$e \in  \mathcal{E}$间存在边。若该二分图无环，则超图(V,E)是Berge-无环的。这种无环性保留了普通无环图的诸多自然特性，例如任意两顶点$u,v \in  \mathcal{V}$间仅存在唯一路径，且(V,E)的任意子图仍保持无环。需注意此Berge-无环性定义不允许两个关系拥有两个及以上共同属性，但若这些属性在所有关系中始终共同出现，则可将其视为一个"组合"属性。在Berge-无环连接$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$中，若关系$e$包含至少一个唯一属性且仅含一个连接属性，则称为叶节点，否则为非叶节点。

LEMMA A.3. For any reduced berge-acyclic join $Q,{\tau }^{ * } \leq  {\rho }^{ * }$ where ${\tau }^{ * },{\rho }^{ * }$ are the optimal fractional edge packing and covering number of $Q$ respectively.

引理A.3. 对于任意约简的Berge-无环连接$Q,{\tau }^{ * } \leq  {\rho }^{ * }$，其中${\tau }^{ * },{\rho }^{ * }$分别是$Q$的最优分数边填充数和覆盖数。

Proof. We will prove this by induction for a berge-acyclic join $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ . The base case is trivial that when $\mathcal{E}$ contains a single relation,with ${\rho }^{ * } = {\tau }^{ * } = 1$ . In general,we consider two more cases:

证明. 对Berge-无环连接$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$进行归纳证明。基本情况是当$\mathcal{E}$仅含单个关系时显然成立，此时${\rho }^{ * } = {\tau }^{ * } = 1$。一般情况下需考虑两种情形：

Case 1. If $Q$ is disconnected,let ${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2},\cdots ,{Q}_{k}$ be its connected components. As ${Q}_{i}$ is also berge-acyclic for any $i \in  \{ 1,2,\cdots ,k\}$ , by hypothesis we have ${\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$ . Observe that ${\tau }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  =$ $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}{\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$ and ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}{\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$ . Thus, ${\tau }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ .

情形1. 若$Q$不连通，设${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2},\cdots ,{Q}_{k}$为其连通分量。由于对任意$i \in  \{ 1,2,\cdots ,k\}$，${Q}_{i}$仍保持Berge-无环性，根据归纳假设有${\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$。观察到${\tau }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  =$$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}{\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$且${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}{\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\right)$，故${\tau }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。

Case 2. Otherwise, $Q$ is connected. First,we can always find a non-leaf ${e}_{0}$ such that removing all leaves in $Q$ would turn it into a new leaf. Let $\Gamma \left( {e}_{0}\right)$ be the set of leaves connected with ${e}_{0}$ . Note that each relation in $\Gamma \left( {e}_{0}\right)$ include one attribute in ${e}_{0}$ . Let ${\mathcal{E}}_{x} = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E}$ : $x \in  e\}$ be the set of relations containing attribute $x$ . Define

情形2. 否则，$Q$是连通的。首先，我们总能找到一个非叶节点${e}_{0}$，使得移除$Q$中所有叶节点后，该节点将转化为新叶节点。设$\Gamma \left( {e}_{0}\right)$为与${e}_{0}$连接的叶节点集合。注意$\Gamma \left( {e}_{0}\right)$中的每个关系都包含${e}_{0}$的一个属性。令${\mathcal{E}}_{x} = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E}$：$x \in  e\}$为包含属性$x$的关系集合。定义

$$
{\mathcal{V}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {v \in  {e}_{0} : {\mathcal{E}}_{v} \subseteq  \left\{  {e}_{0}\right\}   \cup  \Gamma \left( {e}_{0}\right) }\right\}  
$$

Note that $\left| {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }\right|  = 1$ ; otherwise, ${e}_{0}$ is not a new leaf after removing all leaves in $\Gamma \left( {e}_{0}\right)$ . Define

注意$\left| {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }\right|  = 1$；否则在移除$\Gamma \left( {e}_{0}\right)$中所有叶节点后，${e}_{0}$不会成为新叶节点。定义

$$
S = \left\{  {e \in  \Gamma \left( {e}_{0}\right)  : e \cap  {e}_{0} \in  {\mathcal{V}}^{\prime }}\right\}  
$$

We further distinguish it into two more cases:

我们进一步将其区分为两种子情形：

Case 2.1. If ${e}_{0}$ contains unique attributes,let ${Q}^{\prime }$ be the residual query by removing all edges in $S$ . By hypothesis, ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)  \geq  {\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$ since ${Q}^{\prime }$ is also a reduced berge-acyclic join. Note that ${\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right)  =$ ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)  + \left| S\right|$ and ${\tau }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  {\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)  + \left| S\right|$ . Thus, ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  {\tau }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ .

情形2.1. 若${e}_{0}$包含唯一属性，设${Q}^{\prime }$为移除$S$中所有边后的残余查询。根据假设，由于${Q}^{\prime }$也是约化的Berge-无环连接，故${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)  \geq  {\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$。注意${\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right)  =$${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)  + \left| S\right|$且${\tau }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  {\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)  + \left| S\right|$。因此，${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  {\tau }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。

Case 2.2. Otherwise,let ${Q}^{\prime }$ be the residual query by removing all edges of $\left\{  {e}_{0}\right\}   \cup  S$ . By hypothesis, ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)  \geq  {\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$ since ${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }$ is also a reduced berge-acyclic join. Note that ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)  + \left| S\right|$ and ${\tau }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  {\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)  + \left| S\right|$ . Thus, ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  {\tau }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$ .

情况2.2。否则，令${Q}^{\prime }$为通过移除$\left\{  {e}_{0}\right\}   \cup  S$所有边得到的残余查询。根据假设，${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)  \geq  {\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)$因为${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }$也是约化的贝尔格无环连接。注意到${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)  + \left| S\right|$且${\tau }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \leq  {\tau }^{ * }\left( {\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime }\right)  + \left| S\right|$。因此，${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  \geq  {\tau }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{Q}\right)$。

LEMMA A.4. For a join query $Q$ ,the following properties hold:

引理A.4。对于连接查询$Q$，下列性质成立：

(1) If $Q$ is both binary-relation and $\alpha$ -acyclic,then $Q$ is berge-acyclic.

(1) 若$Q$同时满足二元关系且$\alpha$无环，则$Q$是贝尔格无环的。

(2) If $Q$ is degree-two and $\alpha$ -acyclic but not binary-relation,it may or may not be berge-acyclic.

(2) 若$Q$是二阶且$\alpha$无环但非二元关系，则其可能是也可能不是贝尔格无环的。

Proof. For (1),if a join query $\mathcal{Q}$ is both binary-relation and $\alpha$ -acyclic,the hypergraph of $Q$ is a tree,which is berge-acyclic. For (2), ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {A,B,D}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {C,E}\right)$ is not berge-acyclic,and ${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {A,D}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {B,E}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {C,F}\right)$ is berge-acyclic.

证明。(1)若连接查询$\mathcal{Q}$同时满足二元关系且$\alpha$无环，则$Q$的超图是树状结构，即贝尔格无环。(2)${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {A,B,D}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {C,E}\right)$非贝尔格无环，而${R}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {A,D}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {B,E}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {C,F}\right)$是贝尔格无环的。

### A.2 Missing Proofs in Section 4

### A.2 第4节缺失证明

Proof of Lemma 11. Without loss of generality,assume $r = {e}_{k}$ . The base case with $k = 1$ always holds. Let $\mathbf{x} \in  {e}_{k - 1} - {e}_{k}$ be the first attribute. Note that ${S}^{\mathbf{x}} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},\cdots ,{e}_{k - 1}}\right\}$ .

引理11证明。不失一般性，假设$r = {e}_{k}$。基本情况当$k = 1$时恒成立。设$\mathbf{x} \in  {e}_{k - 1} - {e}_{k}$为首个属性。注意${S}^{\mathbf{x}} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},\cdots ,{e}_{k - 1}}\right\}$。

The residual join ${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$ will be reduced first and then tackled by invoking the whole algorithm recursively. By hypothesis, each heavy instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathrm{a}}$ can be computed using $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{x}}\right) }}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathrm{a}},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil  }\right)$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load $O\left( L\right)$ . The total number of servers allocated for all heavy assignments is

残余连接${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$将先被约化，再通过递归调用整个算法处理。根据假设，每个重型实例${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathrm{a}}$可在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次中用$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{x}}\right) }}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathrm{a}},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil  }\right)$台服务器以$O\left( L\right)$负载计算。所有重型分配占用的服务器总数为

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{\mathbf{a}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}\right) }}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil  
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}\right) }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{\mathbf{a}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right)  + {2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| } \cdot  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{i}\right) \right| }{L}
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{k}\right) \right| }{L} \cdot  \frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{i}\right) \right| }{L} + \left( {{2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| } + 1}\right) \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{i}\right) \right| }{L}
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) 
$$

where the first inequality is implied by the fact that there are $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{i}\right) \right| }{L}}\right)$ heavy assignments,the second inequality is implied by distinguishing $S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}\right)$ into two cases,depending on whether $S \cap  \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},\cdots ,{e}_{k - 1}}\right\}   = \varnothing$ ,and the last inequality is implied by the definition of $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$ .

第一个不等式源于存在$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{i}\right) \right| }{L}}\right)$个重度赋值的事实，第二个不等式通过将$S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}\right)$区分为两种情况得出（取决于$S \cap  \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},\cdots ,{e}_{k - 1}}\right\}   = \varnothing$是否成立），最后一个不等式则由$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$的定义推导而来。

The residual join ${Q}_{\mathrm{y}}$ degenerates to the base case with one relation ${e}_{k}$ . Then,for each light group ${I}_{j},{Q}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{j}\right)$ can be computed using $O\left( \left\lceil  \frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{k}\right) \right| }{L}\right\rceil  \right)$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load $O\left( L\right)$ . The total number of servers allocated for all light groups is

剩余连接${Q}_{\mathrm{y}}$退化为仅含单一关系${e}_{k}$的基础情形。随后，每个轻量组${I}_{j},{Q}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{j}\right)$可通过$O\left( \left\lceil  \frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{k}\right) \right| }{L}\right\rceil  \right)$台服务器在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内完成计算，负载为$O\left( L\right)$。所有轻量组分配的服务器的总数为

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{j}\left\lceil  \frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{k}\right) \right| }{L}\right\rceil   \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{j}\left( {\frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{k}\right) \right| }{L} + 1}\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\left( {\frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{i}\right) \right| }{L} + 1}\right) }\right)  \cdot  \left( {\frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{k}\right) \right| }{L} + 1}\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) 
$$

where the second last inequality is implied by the fact that there are $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\left\lceil  \frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{i}\right) \right| }{L}\right\rceil  }\right)$ light groups ad the last inequality is implied by the definition of $\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$ .

倒数第二个不等式由存在$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\left\lceil  \frac{\left| R\left( {e}_{i}\right) \right| }{L}\right\rceil  }\right)$个轻量组的事实推导，最后一个不等式则源于$\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)$的定义。

Combining the analysis for heavy and light subqueries, we complete the whole proof for Lemma 11.

综合对重度与轻度子查询的分析，我们完成了引理11的全部证明。

Proof of Lemma 13. We will prove the following complexity for a twig join excluding its root $r$ :

引理13的证明。我们将针对排除根节点$r$的树枝连接证明以下复杂度：

$$
\mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right)  = \left\{  {\{ S\}  : S \subseteq  \left( {{\mathcal{L}}_{1}-\{ r\} }\right)  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{2} \times  \cdots  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}  
$$

Let $c$ be the child of $r$ . Let $\mathbf{x} \in  c - r$ be the first attribute,and $e$ be the leaf node with $\mathbf{x} \in  e$ . Let path(c,e)as the set of nodes lying on the path from $c$ to $e$ . The residual join ${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$ is reduced first and then tackled by invoking the algorithm recursively. By hypothesis, each heavy instance ${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}}$ induced by heavy value $\mathbf{a} \in  H\left( {\mathbf{x},{S}^{\mathbf{x}}}\right)$ can be computed using $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}\right) }}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil  }\right)$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load $O\left( L\right)$ . The total number of servers allocated for all heavy assignments is

设$c$为$r$的子节点，$\mathbf{x} \in  c - r$为首个属性，$e$为具有$\mathbf{x} \in  e$的叶节点。定义path(c,e)为从$c$到$e$路径上的节点集合。首先归约剩余连接${Q}_{\mathbf{x}}$，随后通过递归调用算法处理。根据假设，每个由重度值$\mathbf{a} \in  H\left( {\mathbf{x},{S}^{\mathbf{x}}}\right)$引发的重度实例${\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}}$可使用$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}\right) }}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil  }\right)$台服务器在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次内完成计算，负载为$O\left( L\right)$。所有重度赋值分配的服务器的总数为

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{\mathbf{a}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathbf{x}}\right) }}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathbf{a}},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil  
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{\mathrm{x}}\right) }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{\mathrm{a}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{\mathrm{a}},S,L}\right)  + {2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| } \cdot  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right) }}\frac{\left| R\left( {e}^{\prime }\right) \right| }{L}
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathcal{P} = \left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S}_{1}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},{S}_{1} \cup  \left\{  {e}^{\prime }\right\}  ,L}\right) 
$$

$$
 + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathcal{P} = \left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1},\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S}_{2}}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},{S}_{2},L}\right) 
$$

$$
 + {2}^{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| } \cdot  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right) }}\frac{\left| R\left( {e}^{\prime }\right) \right| }{L} \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathcal{P}}^{\prime }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) 
$$

for all linear coverings $\mathcal{P},{\mathcal{P}}^{\prime }$ over $\mathcal{T},{S}_{1} \subseteq  \left( {{\mathcal{L}}_{1}-\{ r\} }\right)  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{2} \times$ $\cdots  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }$ with ${S}_{1} \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)  = \varnothing$ ,and ${S}_{2} \subseteq  \left( {{\mathcal{L}}_{1}-\{ r\} }\right)  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{2} \times$ $\cdots  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }$ with ${S}_{2} \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ . Note that the first inequality is implied by the fact that there are $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right) }}\frac{\left| R\left( {e}^{\prime }\right) \right| }{L}}\right)$ heavy assignments,the second inequality is implied by distinguishing $S$ into two cases,depending on whether $S \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)  = \varnothing$ or not,and the last inequality is implied by Lemma A.5.

对于所有在$\mathcal{T},{S}_{1} \subseteq  \left( {{\mathcal{L}}_{1}-\{ r\} }\right)  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{2} \times$$\cdots  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }$上具有${S}_{1} \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)  = \varnothing$的线性覆盖$\mathcal{P},{\mathcal{P}}^{\prime }$，以及在${S}_{2} \subseteq  \left( {{\mathcal{L}}_{1}-\{ r\} }\right)  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{2} \times$$\cdots  \times  {\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }$上具有${S}_{2} \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)  \neq  \varnothing$的情况。注意第一个不等式源于存在$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right) }}\frac{\left| R\left( {e}^{\prime }\right) \right| }{L}}\right)$个重度赋值的事实，第二个不等式通过将$S$分为两种情况（取决于是否满足$S \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right)  = \varnothing$）得出，最后一个不等式则由引理A.5推导而来。

LEMMA A.5. For a twig join $Q$ with its join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,consider an arbitrary linear covering $\mathcal{P} = \left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1},{\mathcal{L}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}$ ,and an arbitrary root-to-leaf path $P$ of $\mathcal{T}$ ,where $P$ could be different from ${\mathcal{L}}_{1}$ . Then, $\left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1} - P,{\mathcal{L}}_{2} - P,\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell } - P}\right\}$ is a linear covering of $\mathcal{T} - P$ .

引理A.5. 对于具有连接树$\mathcal{T}$的树枝连接$Q$，考虑任意线性覆盖$\mathcal{P} = \left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1},{\mathcal{L}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}$及$\mathcal{T}$的任意根到叶路径$P$（其中$P$可能与${\mathcal{L}}_{1}$不同），则$\left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1} - P,{\mathcal{L}}_{2} - P,\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell } - P}\right\}$构成$\mathcal{T} - P$的线性覆盖。

Proof. We first observe that if ${\mathcal{L}}_{1} = P,\left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1} - P,{\mathcal{L}}_{2} - P,\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell } - }\right.$ $P\}  = \left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{2},{\mathcal{L}}_{3},\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}$ is a linear covering of $\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{L}}_{1}$ ,by the definition of $\mathcal{P}$ . It remains to consider the case with ${\mathcal{L}}_{1} \neq  P$ . Note that ${\mathcal{L}}_{1} - P$ is a root-to-leaf path of $\mathcal{T} - P$ . By induction,assume $\left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{2} - P,{\mathcal{L}}_{3} - P,\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell } - P}\right\}$ is a valid linear covering of $\mathcal{T} - P - {\mathcal{L}}_{1}$ . As $\left( {{\mathcal{L}}_{i} - P}\right)  \cap  \left( {{\mathcal{L}}_{1} - P}\right)  = \varnothing$ for any $i \in  \{ 2,3,\cdots ,\ell \}$ ,then ${\mathcal{L}}_{1} - P$ together with $\left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{2} - P,{\mathcal{L}}_{3} - P,\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell } - P}\right\}$ is a valid linear covering of $\mathcal{T} - P$ .

证明。我们首先观察到，若${\mathcal{L}}_{1} = P,\left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{1} - P,{\mathcal{L}}_{2} - P,\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell } - }\right.$$P\}  = \left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{2},{\mathcal{L}}_{3},\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell }}\right\}$是$\mathcal{T} - {\mathcal{L}}_{1}$的线性覆盖，根据$\mathcal{P}$的定义。接下来只需考虑${\mathcal{L}}_{1} \neq  P$的情形。注意到${\mathcal{L}}_{1} - P$是$\mathcal{T} - P$的一条根到叶路径。通过归纳法，假设$\left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{2} - P,{\mathcal{L}}_{3} - P,\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell } - P}\right\}$是$\mathcal{T} - P - {\mathcal{L}}_{1}$的有效线性覆盖。由于对于任意$i \in  \{ 2,3,\cdots ,\ell \}$都有$\left( {{\mathcal{L}}_{i} - P}\right)  \cap  \left( {{\mathcal{L}}_{1} - P}\right)  = \varnothing$，那么${\mathcal{L}}_{1} - P$与$\left\{  {{\mathcal{L}}_{2} - P,{\mathcal{L}}_{3} - P,\cdots ,{\mathcal{L}}_{\ell } - P}\right\}$的联合构成$\mathcal{T} - P$的有效线性覆盖。

Let $\left\{  {{\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\mathcal{T}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{T}}_{h}}\right\}$ be the set of connected subtrees by removing path(c,e)from $\mathcal{T}$ . Let ${\mathcal{Q}}_{i} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{i},{\mathcal{E}}_{i}}\right)$ be the twig join defined on the subtree ${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$ ,excluding its root in ${\mathcal{L}}_{1}$ . For each light group ${I}_{j}$ , computing ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{j}\right)$ degenerates to computing the cartesian products over ${\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{j}\right)$ ’s. By hypothesis, ${\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{j}\right)$ can be computed using $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathcal{S} \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{i}\right) }}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{j},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil  }\right)$ servers in $O\left( 1\right)$ rounds with load $O\left( L\right)$ . The total number of servers allocated for all light groups is

设$\left\{  {{\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\mathcal{T}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{T}}_{h}}\right\}$为从$\mathcal{T}$移除路径(c,e)后得到的连通子树集合。设${\mathcal{Q}}_{i} = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{i},{\mathcal{E}}_{i}}\right)$为定义在子树${\mathcal{T}}_{i}$上的细枝连接（排除其在${\mathcal{L}}_{1}$中的根节点）。对于每个轻量组${I}_{j}$，计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{j}\right)$可退化为计算${\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{j}\right)$上的笛卡尔积。根据假设，${\mathcal{Q}}_{i}\left( {\mathcal{R}}_{j}\right)$可在$O\left( 1\right)$轮次中使用$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\mathcal{S} \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{i}\right) }}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{j},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil  }\right)$台服务器完成计算，负载为$O\left( L\right)$。为所有轻量组分配的服务器总数为

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{j}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{i}\right) }}\left\lceil  {\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{j},S,L}\right) }\right\rceil  
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{i}\right) }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{j}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},{\mathcal{R}}_{j},S,L}\right)  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right) }}\frac{\left| R\left( {e}^{\prime }\right) \right| }{L}
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right) }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{1}\right)  \times  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{2}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  \mathcal{S}\left( {\mathcal{E}}_{h}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S \cup  \left\{  {e}^{\prime }\right\}  ,L}\right) 
$$

$$
 + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right) }}\frac{\left| R\left( {e}^{\prime }\right) \right| }{L} \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{S \in  \mathcal{S}\left( \mathcal{E}\right) }}\Psi \left( {\mathcal{T},\mathcal{R},S,L}\right) 
$$

where the first inequality is implied by the fact that there are $O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right) }}\frac{\left| R\left( {e}^{\prime }\right) \right| }{L}}\right)$ light groups and the last inequality is implied by the fact that the union of linear-covers of ${\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\mathcal{T}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{T}}_{h}$ together with path(c,e)is still a valid linear-cover of $\mathcal{T}$ .

其中第一个不等式源于存在$O\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {c,e}\right) }}\frac{\left| R\left( {e}^{\prime }\right) \right| }{L}}\right)$个轻量组的事实，最后一个不等式源于${\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\mathcal{T}}_{2},\cdots ,{\mathcal{T}}_{h}$的线性覆盖与路径(c,e)的并集仍是$\mathcal{T}$的有效线性覆盖这一事实。

When this twig join $Q$ includes root $r$ ,it degenerates to compute the Cartesian product between $r$ and the residual twig join excluding $r$ . Thus,the complexity in Lemma 13 follows.

当该枝节连接$Q$包含根节点$r$时，其退化为计算$r$与排除$r$后的剩余枝节连接的笛卡尔积。因此，引理13中的复杂度结论成立。

The proof of Theorem 14 follows directly from the primitive of reducing a join, the Case II of the generic algorithm in Section 3, and Lemma 13 sequentially.

定理14的证明直接源自连接约简的基本原理、第3节通用算法的情形II以及引理13的递推。

### A.3 Join-Aggregate Query

### A.3 连接-聚合查询

We consider join-aggregate queries over annotated relations [2, 11, 17] with one semiring. Let $\left( {\mathbb{R},\oplus , \otimes  }\right)$ be a commutative semiring. We assume that every tuple $t$ is associated with an annotation $w\left( t\right)  \in  \mathbb{R}$ . The annotation of a join result $t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ is

我们研究基于单半环的带标注关系[2,11,17]的连接-聚合查询。设$\left( {\mathbb{R},\oplus , \otimes  }\right)$为交换半环，假定每个元组$t$关联一个标注$w\left( t\right)  \in  \mathbb{R}$。连接结果$t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$的标注为

$$
w\left( t\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} { \otimes  }_{{t}_{e} \in  R\left( e\right) ,{\pi }_{e}t = {t}_{e},e \in  \mathcal{E}}w\left( {t}_{e}\right) .
$$

Let $\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ be a set of output attributes (a.k.a. free variables) and $\overline{\mathbf{y}} = \mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}$ the non-output attributes (a.k.a. bound variables). A join-aggregate query ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ asks us to compute ${ \oplus  }_{\overline{\mathbf{y}}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  =$

设$\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$为输出属性集（即自由变量），$\overline{\mathbf{y}} = \mathcal{V} - \mathbf{y}$为非输出属性（即绑定变量）。连接-聚合查询${\mathcal{Q}}_{\mathbf{y}}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$要求计算${ \oplus  }_{\overline{\mathbf{y}}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  =$

$$
\left\{  {\left( {{t}_{\mathbf{y}},w\left( {t}_{\mathbf{y}}\right) }\right)  : {t}_{\mathbf{y}} \in  {\pi }_{\mathbf{y}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) ,w\left( {t}_{\mathbf{y}}\right)  = { \oplus  }_{t \in  \mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)  : {\pi }_{\mathbf{y}}t = {t}_{\mathbf{y}}}w\left( t\right) }\right\}  .
$$

In plain language, a join-aggregate query first computes the join $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ and the annotation of each join result,which is the $\otimes$ - aggregate of the tuples comprising the join result. Then it partitions $\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ into groups by their projection on $\mathbf{y}$ . Finally,for each group, it computes the $\oplus$ -aggregate of the annotations of the join results.

通俗而言，连接-聚合查询首先计算连接$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$及每个连接结果的标注（即组成该连接结果的元组的$\otimes$聚合值），然后按$\mathbf{y}$投影将$\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$分组，最终计算每组内连接结果标注的$\oplus$聚合值。

Many queries can be formulated as special join-aggregate queries. For example,if we take $\mathbb{R}$ to be the domain of integers, $\oplus$ to be addition, $\otimes$ to be multiplication,and set $w\left( t\right)  = 1$ for all $t$ ,then it becomes the COUNT (*) GROUP BY y query; in particular,if $\mathbf{y} = \varnothing$ , the query computes $\left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|$ . The join-project query ${\pi }_{\mathbf{y}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$ ,also known as a conjunctive query, is a special join-aggregate query by discarding the annotations.

许多查询可表述为特殊连接-聚合查询。例如取$\mathbb{R}$为整数域、$\oplus$为加法、$\otimes$为乘法，且对所有$t$设$w\left( t\right)  = 1$时，即转化为COUNT(*) GROUP BY y查询；特别当$\mathbf{y} = \varnothing$时，该查询计算$\left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right|$。连接-投影查询${\pi }_{\mathbf{y}}\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right)$（即合取查询）则是通过舍弃标注得到的特殊连接-聚合查询。

With respect to join-aggregate queries, free-connex queries [6] are an important subclass. To define a free-connex query, we introduce the notion of a width-1 GHD, which can be considered as a generalized join tree. A width-1 GHD of a hypergraph $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is a tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,where each node $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ is a subset of $\mathcal{V}$ ,such that (1) for each attribute $x \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,the nodes containing $x$ are connected in $\mathcal{T}$ ; (2) for each hyperedge $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ ,there exists a node $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ such that $e \subseteq  u$ ; and (3) for each node $u \in  \mathcal{T}$ ,there exists a hyperedge $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ such that $u \subseteq  e$ .

在连接-聚合查询中，自由连接查询[6]是重要子类。为定义自由连接查询，我们引入宽度1的广义超树分解(GHD)概念——可视为广义连接树。超图$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$的宽度1 GHD是树$\mathcal{T}$，其中每个节点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$是$\mathcal{V}$的子集，满足：(1)对每个属性$x \in  \mathcal{V}$，包含$x$的节点在$\mathcal{T}$中连通；(2)对每条超边$e \in  \mathcal{E}$，存在节点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$使得$e \subseteq  u$；(3)对每个节点$u \in  \mathcal{T}$，存在超边$e \in  \mathcal{E}$使得$u \subseteq  e$。

Given a set of output attributes $\mathbf{y},\mathcal{T}$ is free-connex if there is a subset of connected nodes of $\mathcal{T}$ ,denoted as ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ (such a ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ is said to be a connex subset),such that $\mathbf{y} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }}}u$ . A join-aggregate query ${Q}_{\mathrm{y}}$ is free-connex if it has a free-connex width-1 GHD.

给定一组输出属性$\mathbf{y},\mathcal{T}$，若存在$\mathcal{T}$的连通节点子集（记作${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$，该${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$称为连通子集），使得$\mathbf{y} = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{u \in  {\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }}}u$，则该属性集为自由连通。当连接聚合查询${Q}_{\mathrm{y}}$具有自由连通的宽度1广义超树分解时，该查询即为自由连通查询。

### A.4 Proof of Lemma 21

### A.4 引理21的证明

The proof directly follows the fact that the dual of any reduced degree-two join is a reduced binary-relation join [18, 24]. We can show more details for each property in Lemma 21.

该证明直接源于以下事实：任何简化二阶连接的对偶都是简化二元关系连接[18,24]。我们可以针对引理21中的每个性质展示更多细节。

For (1),let $f$ be the mapping from $\mathcal{E}$ to $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ . Let $f\left( e\right)  = \frac{1}{2}$ for every $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ . In the reduced degree-two join $Q$ ,each vertex appears in exactly two hyperedges. In this way, $f$ is both a valid fractional edge packing and edge covering. Implied by the maximization of fractional edge packing and minimization of fractional edge covering,we get ${\tau }^{ * } \geq  \frac{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }{2} \geq  {\rho }^{ * }$ .

对于性质(1)，设$f$为$\mathcal{E}$到$\lbrack 0, + \infty )$的映射。令每个$e \in  \mathcal{E}$对应$f\left( e\right)  = \frac{1}{2}$。在简化二阶连接$Q$中，每个顶点恰好出现在两条超边中。因此$f$既是有效的分数边填充，也是边覆盖。根据分数边填充的最大化和边覆盖的最小化，可得${\tau }^{ * } \geq  \frac{\left| \mathcal{E}\right| }{2} \geq  {\rho }^{ * }$。

For (2),let $f$ be a valid fractional edge packing for $Q$ . It can be easily checked that $g = \{ 1 - f\left( e\right)  : e \in  \mathcal{E}\}$ is a valid fractional edge covering for $\mathcal{Q}$ . Thus,we get ${\rho }^{ * } = \left| \mathcal{E}\right|  - {\tau }^{ * }$ .

对于性质(2)，设$f$是$Q$的有效分数边填充。容易验证$g = \{ 1 - f\left( e\right)  : e \in  \mathcal{E}\}$构成$\mathcal{Q}$的有效分数边覆盖。因此可得${\rho }^{ * } = \left| \mathcal{E}\right|  - {\tau }^{ * }$。

A similar result has been proved for optimal fractional vertex covering for an ordinary graph [21], that the vertex packing for an ordinary graph admits half-integral solution, and the set of vertices with value $\frac{1}{2}$ form a set of vertex-disjoint odd-length cycles. Thus, (3) and (4) follow.

普通图的最优分数顶点覆盖已有类似结论[21]：普通图的顶点填充允许半整数解，且值为$\frac{1}{2}$的顶点集会形成顶点不相交的奇长度环。由此可推出性质(3)和(4)。

### A.5 Proof of Theorem 18

### A.5 定理18的证明

We first point out several important properties for degree-two joins satisfying edge-packing-provable conditions.

我们首先指出满足边填充可证条件的二阶连接具有的几个重要性质。

If there is no odd-length cycle in $Q$ ,it admits integral optimal edge packing ${\tau }^{ * }$ and covering ${\rho }^{ * }$ ,implied by Lemma 21. More specifically,there exists a partition $\left( {{\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha },{\mathcal{E}}_{\beta }}\right)$ of $\mathcal{E} : {\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha } = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E}$ : $\left. {{\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1,{\tau }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0}\right\}$ and ${\mathcal{E}}_{\beta } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : {\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0,{\tau }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1}\right\}$ . Consider any vertex $v$ incident to two edges $e,{e}^{\prime }$ . There must be $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha },{e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\beta }$ ,or $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\beta },{e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha }$ . This also implies that all edges in ${\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha }$ are vertex-disjoint,as well as edges in ${\mathcal{E}}_{\beta }$ .

若$Q$中不存在奇长度环，根据引理21可知其存在整数最优边填充${\tau }^{ * }$和覆盖${\rho }^{ * }$。具体而言，存在$\mathcal{E} : {\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha } = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E}$的划分$\left( {{\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha },{\mathcal{E}}_{\beta }}\right)$：$\left. {{\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1,{\tau }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0}\right\}$与${\mathcal{E}}_{\beta } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : {\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0,{\tau }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1}\right\}$。考虑任意顶点$v$关联的两条边$e,{e}^{\prime }$，必有$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha },{e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\beta }$或$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\beta },{e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha }$。这也意味着${\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha }$中所有边都是顶点不相交的，${\mathcal{E}}_{\beta }$中的边亦然。

Let $x$ be an optimal fractional vertex covering for $Q$ . An edge $e$ is denoted as deterministic if $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} = 1$ ,and probabilistic otherwise. Let ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ be the set of probabilistic edges,i.e. $\{ e \in  \mathcal{E}$ : $\left. {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} > 1}\right\}$ . Note that vertex covering and edge packing are prime-dual problems. The following result is directly implied by the complementary slackness.

设$x$为$Q$的最优分数顶点覆盖。若边$e$满足$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} = 1$则称其为确定性边，否则为概率性边。令${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$表示概率性边集合，即$\{ e \in  \mathcal{E}$：$\left. {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} > 1}\right\}$。需注意顶点覆盖与边填充是原始-对偶问题。以下结果直接由互补松弛性得出。

LEMMA A.6. Let ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} > 1}\right\}$ . For any $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ , ${\tau }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0$ and ${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1$ .

引理A.6。设${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} > 1}\right\}$。对于任意$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$，${\tau }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0$且${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1$。

For each edge $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ ,let $\Upsilon \left( e\right)$ be the set of vertices appearing in the neighbor of $e$ ,i.e., $\Upsilon \left( e\right)  = { \cup  }_{{e}^{\prime } \in  \Gamma \left( e\right) }{e}^{\prime } - e$ . Two nice properties on the edges in ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ are stated in Lemma A. 7 and Lemma A.8.

对于每条边$e \in  \mathcal{E}$，令$\Upsilon \left( e\right)$表示顶点集出现在$e$的邻域中，即$\Upsilon \left( e\right)  = { \cup  }_{{e}^{\prime } \in  \Gamma \left( e\right) }{e}^{\prime } - e$。关于${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$中边的两个优良性质如引理A.7和引理A.8所述。

LEMMA A.7. Let ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} > 1}\right\}$ . For any edge $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  \Upsilon \left( e\right) }}{x}_{v} = \left| {\Gamma \left( e\right) }\right| .$

引理A.7。设${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} > 1}\right\}$。对于任意边$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  \Upsilon \left( e\right) }}{x}_{v} = \left| {\Gamma \left( e\right) }\right| .$

Proof. As ${\tau }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0$ and ${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1$ ,we have ${\tau }^{ * }\left( {e}^{\prime }\right)  = 1$ and ${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0$ for every edge ${e}^{\prime } \in  \Gamma \left( e\right)$ . This also implies that for any pair of edges ${e}_{1},{e}_{2} \in  \Gamma \left( e\right) ,{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2} = \varnothing$ . Moreover,each edge ${e}^{\prime } \in  \Gamma \left( e\right)$ is deterministic implied by Lemma A.6,thus $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  {e}^{\prime }}}{x}_{v} = 1$ . We also observe that $e \cup  \left( {{ \cup  }_{{e}^{\prime } \in  \Gamma \left( e\right) }{e}^{\prime } - e}\right)  = { \cup  }_{{e}^{\prime } \in  \Gamma \left( e\right) }{e}^{\prime }$ . Thus,the left-hand-side of the target equation can be rewritten as

证明。由于${\tau }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0$和${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 1$，对每条边${e}^{\prime } \in  \Gamma \left( e\right)$有${\tau }^{ * }\left( {e}^{\prime }\right)  = 1$和${\rho }^{ * }\left( e\right)  = 0$。这也意味着对于任意边对${e}_{1},{e}_{2} \in  \Gamma \left( e\right) ,{e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2} = \varnothing$。此外，根据引理A.6每条边${e}^{\prime } \in  \Gamma \left( e\right)$均为确定性边，故$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  {e}^{\prime }}}{x}_{v} = 1$。我们还观察到$e \cup  \left( {{ \cup  }_{{e}^{\prime } \in  \Gamma \left( e\right) }{e}^{\prime } - e}\right)  = { \cup  }_{{e}^{\prime } \in  \Gamma \left( e\right) }{e}^{\prime }$。因此，目标等式左侧可改写为

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  { \cup  }_{{e}^{\prime } \in  \Gamma \left( e\right) }{e}^{\prime }}}{x}_{v} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{e}^{\prime } \in  \Gamma \left( e\right) }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} = \left| {\Gamma \left( e\right) }\right| 
$$

thus yielding the desired result.

由此可得所需结论。

LEMMA A.8. Let ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} > 1}\right\}  .{\rho }^{ * } - {\tau }^{ * } =$ $\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right|  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e : e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v}.$

引理A.8 设${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e \in  \mathcal{E} : \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} > 1}\right\}  .{\rho }^{ * } - {\tau }^{ * } =$$\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right|  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e : e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v}.$

Proof. By the duality theorem, ${\tau }^{ * } = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  \mathcal{V}}}{x}_{v}$ . In this way, we can rewrite $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v}$ as

证明 根据对偶定理，${\tau }^{ * } = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  \mathcal{V}}}{x}_{v}$。由此可将$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v}$改写为

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  \mathcal{V}}}{x}_{v} - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\alpha } - {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} = {\tau }^{ * } - \left( {{\rho }^{ * } - \left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }\right) 
$$

thus yielding the desired result.

从而得到预期结果。

Now, we are able to prove Theorem 18. As it follows the same framework as Section 5.1, we will focus on addressing the difference in this non-trivial extension. Similarly, we will show that with positive probability, an instance constructed this way will have a bounded $J\left( L\right)$ ,the maximum number of join results a server can produce,if it loads at most $L$ tuples from each relation. Then setting $p \cdot  J\left( L\right)  = \Omega \left( \left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right| \right)$ yields a lower bound on $L$ .

现在我们可以证明定理18。由于其框架与第5.1节相同，我们将重点讨论这个非平凡扩展中的差异。类似地，我们将证明以正概率构建的实例，其$J\left( L\right)$（服务器能产生的最大连接结果数）是有界的——前提是每个关系最多加载$L$个元组。而后设定$p \cdot  J\left( L\right)  = \Omega \left( \left| {\mathcal{Q}\left( \mathcal{R}\right) }\right| \right)$即可得到$L$的下界。

Hard instance construction. There are ${N}^{{x}_{v}}$ distinct values in the domain of attribute $v$ . Namely,a deterministic relation $R\left( e\right)$ is a Cartesian product over all attributes in $e$ ,with $\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{N}^{{x}_{v}} =$ ${N}^{\sum v : v \in  e{x}_{v}} = N$ tuples in total; and a probabilistic relation $R\left( e\right)$ is constructed in a probabilistic way, such that each combination $t \in  { \times  }_{v : v \in  e}\operatorname{dom}\left( v\right)$ has a probability of $p\left( e\right)  = 1/{N}^{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} - 1}$ to form a tuples in $R\left( e\right)$ ,with $\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{N}^{{x}_{v}} \cdot  p\left( e\right)  = N$ tuples in expectation. Moreover, each relation has its input size deviates from its expectation by a constant factor is at most $\exp \left( {-\Omega \left( N\right) }\right)$ . Taking all relations in the edge covering,they together form ${N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}$ results while remaining relations are deterministic Cartesian product. So this instance has its output size deviating from its expectation by a constant factor is at most $\exp \left( {-\Omega \left( N\right) }\right)$ .

困难实例构建。属性$v$的定义域中存在${N}^{{x}_{v}}$个不同值。确定性关系$R\left( e\right)$是$e$中所有属性的笛卡尔积，共含$\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{N}^{{x}_{v}} =$${N}^{\sum v : v \in  e{x}_{v}} = N$个元组；概率性关系$R\left( e\right)$则以概率方式构建，每个组合$t \in  { \times  }_{v : v \in  e}\operatorname{dom}\left( v\right)$有$p\left( e\right)  = 1/{N}^{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} - 1}$的概率形成$R\left( e\right)$中的元组，期望元组数为$\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{N}^{{x}_{v}} \cdot  p\left( e\right)  = N$。此外，每个关系的输入规模偏离期望值的倍数不超过$\exp \left( {-\Omega \left( N\right) }\right)$。覆盖边集的所有关系共同形成${N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}$个结果，其余关系为确定性笛卡尔积。因此该实例输出规模偏离期望值的倍数不超过$\exp \left( {-\Omega \left( N\right) }\right)$。

## Step 1: Making a reasonale restriction on loading tuples.

## 步骤1：对加载元组施加合理限制

LEMMA A.9. For any deterministic edge $e$ ,if $\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}\right|  \leq  1$ ,then assuming that tuples loaded from $R\left( e\right)$ should be in forms of Cartesian products over all attributes, doesn't decrease the maximum number of join results that can be produced per server by a constant factor.

引理A.9 对于任意确定性边$e$，若$\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}\right|  \leq  1$，则假设从$R\left( e\right)$加载的元组必须采用所有属性的笛卡尔积形式，不会使每个服务器能产生的最大连接结果数降低常数倍。

Proof. Note that for any vertex $v \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,if the two edges incident to it are deterministic, the same argument in Lemma 19 can be applied to $v$ ,i.e.,loading tuples in $R\left( e\right)$ for $v \in  e$ in terms of ${L}_{v} \times$ ${L}_{e-\{ v\} }$ will not decrease the optimal solution by a constant factor. Moreover,if ${L}_{e} = {L}_{v} \times  {L}_{e-\{ v\} }$ ,then ${L}_{uv} = {L}_{v} \times  {L}_{u}$ for any $u \in$ $e - \{ v\}$ . To prove this result,it suffices to show that ${L}_{uv} = {L}_{v} \times  {L}_{u}$ for every pair of vertices $u,v \in  e$ . We distinguish $e$ into two cases.

证明。注意到对于任意顶点$v \in  \mathcal{V}$，若其关联的两条边均为确定性边，则可对$v$应用引理19的相同论证，即根据${L}_{v} \times$${L}_{e-\{ v\} }$为$v \in  e$加载$R\left( e\right)$中的元组不会使最优解降低常数倍。此外，若${L}_{e} = {L}_{v} \times  {L}_{e-\{ v\} }$，则对任意$u \in$$e - \{ v\}$有${L}_{uv} = {L}_{v} \times  {L}_{u}$。为证此结论，只需证明对每对顶点$u,v \in  e$均满足${L}_{uv} = {L}_{v} \times  {L}_{u}$。我们将$e$分为两种情况讨论。

If $\Gamma \left( e\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \varnothing$ ,Lemma 19 can be applied to all vertices to $e$ , thus ${L}_{uv} = {L}_{v} \times  {L}_{u}$ for every pair of vertices $u,v \in  e$ . Otherwise, $\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}\right|  = 1$ ,say $\Gamma \left( e\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e}^{\prime \prime }\right\}$ . Applying Lemma 19 can be applied to every attribute $v \in  e - {e}^{\prime \prime }$ ,we have ${L}_{uv} = {L}_{v} \times  {L}_{u}$ for every $u \in  e,v \in  e - {e}^{\prime \prime }$ . Note that when $\left| {{e}^{\prime \prime } \cap  e}\right|  = 1$ ,we are done. The remaining case is when $\left| {{e}^{\prime \prime } \cap  e}\right|  \geq  2$ ,we can assume that the optimal edge covering $x$ could shift all weights on vertices in $e - {e}^{\prime \prime }$ to one specific vertex in $e - {e}^{\prime \prime }$ ,and assign 0 for remaining vertices in $e - {e}^{\prime \prime }$ ,without changing its optimality and property in Definition 22. In this way, the condition is also satisfied.

若$\Gamma \left( e\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \varnothing$，则引理19可应用于所有顶点至$e$，因此每对顶点$u,v \in  e$均满足${L}_{uv} = {L}_{v} \times  {L}_{u}$。否则，设$\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}\right|  = 1$为$\Gamma \left( e\right)  \cap  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {e}^{\prime \prime }\right\}$。对每个属性$v \in  e - {e}^{\prime \prime }$应用引理19后，对任意$u \in  e,v \in  e - {e}^{\prime \prime }$都有${L}_{uv} = {L}_{v} \times  {L}_{u}$。注意到当$\left| {{e}^{\prime \prime } \cap  e}\right|  = 1$时已完成证明。剩余情况是当$\left| {{e}^{\prime \prime } \cap  e}\right|  \geq  2$时，可假设覆盖$x$的最优边能够将$e - {e}^{\prime \prime }$中所有顶点的权重转移至$e - {e}^{\prime \prime }$内某一特定顶点，并为$e - {e}^{\prime \prime }$中其余顶点赋0值，同时保持其在定义22中的最优性和性质。如此，条件亦得满足。

## Step 2: Prove an upper bound on $J\left( L\right)$ .

## 步骤2：证明$J\left( L\right)$的上界

Assume the number of distinct values from attribute $v$ loaded by the server is ${z}_{v}$ . Observe that $1 \leq  {z}_{v} \leq  {N}^{{x}_{v}}$ for each vertex $v \in  V$ . Moreover, $\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{z}_{v} = L$ for each deterministic relation $e$ .

假设服务器加载的属性$v$的不同值数量为${z}_{v}$。观察到对每个顶点$v \in  V$有$1 \leq  {z}_{v} \leq  {N}^{{x}_{v}}$。此外，每个确定性关系$e$均满足$\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{z}_{v} = L$。

Recall that relations in ${\mathcal{E}}_{\beta }$ are vertex-disjoint. After loading $L$ tuples from all deterministic relations,there are ${L}^{{\tau }^{ * }}$ combinations of results in total, where each of them has a probability of

回顾${\mathcal{E}}_{\beta }$中的关系是顶点互斥的。从所有确定性关系加载$L$个元组后，根据引理A.8的暗示，总共存在${L}^{{\tau }^{ * }}$种结果组合，其中每个组合形成有效连接结果的概率为

$$
\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}p\left( e\right)  = \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\frac{1}{{N}^{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v} - 1}} = {N}^{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right|  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e : e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v : v \in  e}}{x}_{v}} = {N}^{{\rho }^{ * } - {\tau }^{ * }}
$$

to form a valid join result, implied by Lemma A.8. The expected number of join results that can be produced by one server is ${L}^{{\tau }^{ * }}$ . ${N}^{{\rho }^{ * } - {\tau }^{ * }}$ . Next,we will show that this number of join results deviates from its expectation by a constant factor is exponentially small.

单个服务器可产生的连接结果期望数量为${L}^{{\tau }^{ * }}$。${N}^{{\rho }^{ * } - {\tau }^{ * }}$。接下来我们将证明，该连接结果数量与期望值存在常数倍偏差的概率呈指数级小。

For each relation $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ ,we introduce a random variable ${Y}_{t}$ for each combination $t \in  { \times  }_{v : v \in  e}{L}_{v}$ ,which follows the bernoulli distribution with parameter $p\left( e\right)$ . Denote $Y\left( e\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{t}{Y}_{t}$ . Observe that there are ${L}^{\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right) }\right|  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  \Upsilon \left( e\right) }}{x}_{v}}$ independent random variables in the space ${ \times  }_{v : v \in  e}{L}_{v}$ . So,

对于每个关系$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$，我们为每个组合$t \in  { \times  }_{v : v \in  e}{L}_{v}$引入一个服从伯努利分布（参数为$p\left( e\right)$）的随机变量${Y}_{t}$。记作$Y\left( e\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{t}{Y}_{t}$。注意在空间${ \times  }_{v : v \in  e}{L}_{v}$中共有${L}^{\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right) }\right|  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  \Upsilon \left( e\right) }}{x}_{v}}$个独立随机变量。因此，

$$
E\left\lbrack  {Y\left( e\right) }\right\rbrack   = {L}^{\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right) }\right|  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  \Upsilon \left( e\right) }}{x}_{v}} \cdot  p\left( e\right)  = N \cdot  {\left( \frac{L}{N}\right) }^{\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right) }\right|  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  \Upsilon \left( e\right) }}{x}_{v}}
$$

where the last inequality is implied by Lemma A.7. By Chernoff bound,the probability that $Y\left( e\right)$ deviates from its expectation by a constant factor is at most $\exp ( - \Omega \left( {E\left\lbrack  {Y\left( e\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)$ .

其中最后一个不等式由引理A.7隐含。根据切尔诺夫界，$Y\left( e\right)$与其期望值偏离一个常数倍的概率至多为$\exp ( - \Omega \left( {E\left\lbrack  {Y\left( e\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)$。

Let $Y = \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{2}}}Y\left( e\right)$ . As mentioned,these ${L}^{{\tau }^{ * }}$ combinations are not fully independent,as long as they share any same variable ${Y}_{t}$ . In fact,the number of independent combinations is ${L}^{{\tau }^{ * } - {\rho }^{ * } + \left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }$ ,since all combinations can be put into disjoint groups by the random variables shared and each group has exactly ${L}^{{\rho }^{ * } - \left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }$ combinations. Thus,the probability that the server produces more than ${2}^{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }$ . $\frac{{L}^{{\tau }^{ * } - {\rho }^{ * }}}{{N}^{{\tau }^{ * } - {\rho }^{ * } - \left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }}$ join results is at most

设$Y = \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{2}}}Y\left( e\right)$。如前所述，只要这些${L}^{{\tau }^{ * }}$个组合共享任意相同变量${Y}_{t}$，它们就不是完全独立的。实际上，独立组合的数量为${L}^{{\tau }^{ * } - {\rho }^{ * } + \left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }$，因为所有组合可按共享的随机变量划分为互不相交的组，每组恰好包含${L}^{{\rho }^{ * } - \left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }$个组合。因此，服务器生成超过${2}^{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }$个连接结果的概率至多为

$$
\Pr \left( {Y \geq  {2}^{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| } \cdot  \frac{{L}^{{\tau }^{ * } - {\rho }^{ * } + \left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }}{{N}^{{\tau }^{ * } - {\rho }^{ * }}}}\right)  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\Pr \left( {Y\left( e\right)  \geq  2 \cdot  E\left\lbrack  {Y\left( e\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right) 
$$

$$
 \leq  \exp \left( {-\Omega \left( {\mathop{\min }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}E\left\lbrack  {Y\left( e\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right) }\right) 
$$

Consider an arbitrary attribute $v \in  V$ . Note that there are $\left( \begin{matrix} {N}^{{x}_{v}} \\  {z}_{v} \end{matrix}\right)  = O\left( {N}^{{x}_{v} \cdot  {z}_{v}}\right)$ ) choices of loading ${z}_{v}$ distinct values from the domain of attribute $v$ . Over all possible values of ${z}_{v}$ ,the number of choices over all attributes in total is

考虑任意属性$v \in  V$。注意从属性$v$的域中加载${z}_{v}$个不同值共有$\left( \begin{matrix} {N}^{{x}_{v}} \\  {z}_{v} \end{matrix}\right)  = O\left( {N}^{{x}_{v} \cdot  {z}_{v}}\right)$种选择方式。对所有${z}_{v}$的可能取值，所有属性的总选择数为

$$
\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{v : v \in  V}}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{{L}_{v} = 1}}^{{N}^{{x}_{v}}}\exp \left( {O\left( {N}^{{x}_{v} \cdot  {L}_{v}}\right) }\right)  = \exp \left( {\widetilde{O}\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{v}{N}^{{x}_{v}}}\right) }\right) 
$$

By the union bound, the probability that any of the choices produces more than ${2}^{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| } \cdot  \frac{{L}^{{\tau }^{ * } - {\rho }^{ * }}}{{N}^{{\tau }^{ * } - {\rho }^{ * } - \left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }}$ join results is at most

根据联合界，任一选择产生超过${2}^{\left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| } \cdot  \frac{{L}^{{\tau }^{ * } - {\rho }^{ * }}}{{N}^{{\tau }^{ * } - {\rho }^{ * } - \left| {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right| }}$个连接结果的概率至多为

$$
\exp \left( {-\Omega \left( {\mathop{\min }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}E\left\lbrack  {Y\left( e\right) }\right\rbrack  }\right)  + \widetilde{O}\left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{v \in  \mathcal{V}}}{N}^{{x}_{v}}}\right) }\right) 
$$

which is exponentially small if

该概率在以下条件下呈指数级衰减：

$$
\mathop{\min }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}E\left\lbrack  {Y\left( e\right) }\right\rbrack   \geq  {c}_{1} \cdot  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{v \in  \mathcal{V}}}{N}^{{x}_{v}} \cdot  \log N
$$

for some sufficiently large constant ${c}_{1}$ ,or

当某个足够大的常数${c}_{1}$满足时，或

$$
N \cdot  {\left( \frac{L}{N}\right) }^{\lambda \left( Q\right) } \geq  {c}_{1} \cdot  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{v \in  \mathcal{V}}}{N}^{{x}_{v}} \cdot  \log N
$$

where $\lambda \left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right) }\right|  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  \Upsilon \left( e\right) }}{x}_{v} \leq  \left| \mathcal{E}\right|$ . Rearranging it,

其中$\lambda \left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\left| {\Gamma \left( e\right) }\right|  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{v \in  \Upsilon \left( e\right) }}{x}_{v} \leq  \left| \mathcal{E}\right|$。重新整理得：

$$
{L}^{\lambda \left( \mathcal{Q}\right) } \geq  {c}_{1} \cdot  {N}^{\lambda \left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  - 1} \cdot  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{v \in  \mathcal{V}}}{N}^{{x}_{v}} \cdot  \log N
$$

We know that $L \geq  \Omega \left( \frac{N}{p}\right)$ ,so this is true as long as

已知$L \geq  \Omega \left( \frac{N}{p}\right)$，故只要满足下式即成立：

$$
{\left( \frac{N}{p}\right) }^{\lambda \left( \mathcal{Q}\right) } \geq  {c}_{2} \cdot  {N}^{\lambda \left( \mathcal{Q}\right)  - 1} \cdot  \mathop{\max }\limits_{{v \in  \mathcal{V}}}{N}^{{x}_{v}} \cdot  \log N
$$

for some sufficiently large constant ${c}_{2}$ ,or

对于某个足够大的常数${c}_{2}$，或

$$
N \geq  {c}_{3} \cdot  {p}^{\frac{\lambda \left( Q\right) }{1 - \mathop{\max }\limits_{{v : \mathcal{V} \in  \mathcal{V}}}{x}_{\mathcal{V}}}} \cdot  {\left( \log N\right) }^{\frac{1}{1 - \mathop{\max }\limits_{{v : \mathcal{V} \in  \mathcal{V}}}}}
$$

for some sufficiently large constant ${c}_{3}$ . Note that $x$ is constant-small, so $\frac{\lambda \left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }{1 - \mathop{\max }\limits_{\mathcal{O}}{x}_{\mathcal{O}}} = O\left( \left| \mathcal{E}\right| \right)$ is still a constant.

对于某个足够大的常数${c}_{3}$。注意到$x$是常数级小量，因此$\frac{\lambda \left( \mathcal{Q}\right) }{1 - \mathop{\max }\limits_{\mathcal{O}}{x}_{\mathcal{O}}} = O\left( \left| \mathcal{E}\right| \right)$仍为常数。

## Step 3: Apply counting argument.

## 步骤3：应用计数论证。

So far, we have shown that with exponentially high probability each server produces no more than $O\left( {{L}^{{\tau }^{ * }} \cdot  {N}^{{\rho }^{ * } - {\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$ join results in each round. Over $p$ servers,the number of join results produced in total is $O\left( {p{L}^{{\tau }^{ * }} \cdot  {N}^{{\rho }^{ * } - {\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$ with high probability. As there are $\Theta \left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$ join results,we must have $p{L}^{{\tau }^{ * }} \cdot  {N}^{{\rho }^{ * } - {\tau }^{ * }} \geq  {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}$ ,which leads to a lower bound $L \geq  N/{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}$ .

至此，我们已证明在指数级高概率下，每台服务器每轮产生的连接结果不超过$O\left( {{L}^{{\tau }^{ * }} \cdot  {N}^{{\rho }^{ * } - {\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$个。在$p$台服务器上，总连接结果数大概率不超过$O\left( {p{L}^{{\tau }^{ * }} \cdot  {N}^{{\rho }^{ * } - {\tau }^{ * }}}\right)$。由于实际存在$\Theta \left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}\right)$个连接结果，必有$p{L}^{{\tau }^{ * }} \cdot  {N}^{{\rho }^{ * } - {\tau }^{ * }} \geq  {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }}$，由此导出下界$L \geq  N/{p}^{1/{\tau }^{ * }}$。
